Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly rely on agentic capabilities-iterative retrieval, tool use, and decision-making-to overcome the limits of static, parametric knowledge. Yet existing agentic frameworks treat external information as unstructured text and fail to leverage the topological dependencies inherent in real-world data. To bridge this gap, we introduce Agentic Graph Learning (AGL), a paradigm that reframes graph learning as an interleaved process of topology-aware navigation and LLM-based inference. Specifically, we propose AgentGL, the first reinforcement learning (RL)-driven framework for AGL. AgentGL equips an LLM agent with graph-native tools for multi-scale exploration, regulates tool usage via search-constrained thinking to balance accuracy and efficiency, and employs a graph-conditioned curriculum RL strategy to stabilize long-horizon policy learning without step-wise supervision. Across diverse Text-Attributed Graph (TAG) benchmarks and multiple LLM backbones, AgentGL substantially outperforms strong GraphLLMs and GraphRAG baselines, achieving absolute improvements of up to 17.5% in node classification and 28.4% in link prediction. These results demonstrate that AGL is a promising frontier for enabling LLMs to autonomously navigate and reason over complex relational environments. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/sunyuanfu/AgentGL.

</details>


### [55] [LoRM: Learning the Language of Rotating Machinery for Self-Supervised Condition Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05863)
*Xiao Qin,Xingyi Song,Tong Liu,Hatim Laalej,Zepeng Liu,Yunpeng Zhu,Ligang He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LoRM将旋转机械信号视为"机器语言"，通过自监督方式实现多模态传感器信号的理解与实时健康监测。核心创新在于将连续信号量化为离散token，利用预训练语言模型进行微调，通过跟踪预测误差实现故障检测，在刀具状态监测中验证了跨刀具泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统旋转机械监测方法依赖手工设计的信号变换和特征工程，缺乏自适应性和跨任务泛化能力。同时，工业场景中标注数据稀缺，难以支撑大规模深度模型的端到端训练。如何构建一种无需人工特征设计、能够有效利用未标注数据、且具备跨场景迁移能力的信号理解框架，是本文关注的核心问题。

Method: 1. **信号语言化框架**：将多模态传感器信号类比为"机器语言"，局部信号离散化为token序列；2. **混合表示策略**：每个数据窗口中，上下文段保持连续形式（保留细粒度信息），目标预测段量化为离散token；3. **预训练模型迁移**：对通用预训练语言模型进行部分参数微调，高效适配工业信号领域，避免从头训练；4. **异常检测机制**：以token预测误差作为健康指标，误差增大表征设备退化。

Result: 刀具状态监测（TCM）实验表明：1）LoRM能够稳定实现实时健康跟踪；2）展现出较强的跨刀具泛化能力，可将在一种刀具上学习的模型迁移至其他刀具；3）为语言建模与工业信号分析之间提供了可行的技术桥梁。

Conclusion: LoRM通过将旋转机械信号转化为token化的序列预测问题，成功将自然语言处理中的预训练-微调范式迁移至工业信号领域。该方法在减少人工干预的同时，实现了有效的领域知识迁移和实时故障监测，为智能制造场景下的信号理解提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present LoRM (Language of Rotating Machinery), a self-supervised framework for multi-modal rotating-machinery signal understanding and real-time condition monitoring. LoRM is built on the idea that rotating-machinery signals can be viewed as a machine language: local signals can be tokenised into discrete symbolic units, and their future evolution can be predicted from observed multi-sensor context. Unlike conventional signal-processing methods that rely on hand-crafted transforms and features, LoRM reformulates multi-modal sensor data as a token-based sequence-prediction problem. For each data window, the observed context segment is retained in continuous form, while the future target segment of each sensing channel is quantised into a discrete token. Then, efficient knowledge transfer is achieved by partially fine-tuning a general-purpose pre-trained language model on industrial signals, avoiding the need to train a large model from scratch. Finally, condition monitoring is performed by tracking token-prediction errors as a health indicator, where increasing errors indicate degradation. In-situ tool condition monitoring (TCM) experiments demonstrate stable real-time tracking and strong cross-tool generalisation, showing that LoRM provides a practical bridge between language modelling and industrial signal analysis. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Q159753258/LormPHM.

</details>


### [56] [Understanding Performance Gap Between Parallel and Sequential Sampling in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05868)
*Xiangming Gu,Soham De,Larisa Markeeva,Petar Veličković,Razvan Pascanu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大推理模型(LRMs)的采样策略，发现并行采样优于顺序采样，尽管后者理论上具有更强的表示能力。通过多模型家族和领域的实验验证，揭示探索不足是导致顺序采样性能差距的主要原因，而非聚合算子或上下文长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型在数学和编码等难题上表现优异，但获取高质量解答往往需要多次采样。理解不同采样策略的性能差异对于优化LLM推理至关重要。

Method: 在Qwen3、DeepSeek-R1蒸馏模型、Gemini 2.5等模型家族和数学、编码等领域的问答上进行实证实验，检验三个假设：(i)聚合算子影响；(ii)上下文长度影响；(iii)探索不足影响。

Result: 实验表明，聚合算子和上下文长度并非性能差距的主因，而探索不足是主要原因。并行采样因保持多样性而优于顺序采样。

Conclusion: 探索不足（exploration）是顺序采样性能落后的关键原因。建议在设计推理策略时优先考虑保持答案多样性而非简单顺序执行。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have shown remarkable performance on challenging questions, such as math and coding. However, to obtain a high quality solution, one may need to sample more than once. In principal, there are two sampling strategies that can be composed to form more complex processes: sequential sampling and parallel sampling. In this paper, we first compare these two approaches with rigor, and observe, aligned with previous works, that parallel sampling seems to outperform sequential sampling even though the latter should have more representation power. To understand the underline reasons, we make three hypothesis on the reason behind this behavior: (i) parallel sampling outperforms due to the aggregator operator; (ii) sequential sampling is harmed by needing to use longer contexts; (iii) sequential sampling leads to less exploration due to conditioning on previous answers. The empirical evidence on various model families and sizes (Qwen3, DeepSeek-R1 distilled models, Gemini 2.5) and question domains (math and coding) suggests that the aggregation and context length do not seem to be the main culprit behind the performance gap. In contrast, the lack of exploration seems to play a considerably larger role, and we argue that this is one main cause for the performance gap.

</details>


### [57] [Mechanistic Circuit-Based Knowledge Editing in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05876)
*Tianyi Zhao,Yinhan He,Wendy Zheng,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MCircKE提出基于因果机制的电路定位与精准适配的知识编辑方法，解决现有方法在多跳推理任务中的"推理鸿沟"问题，通过在识别的因果电路上进行手术式参数更新来确保编辑知识能够被正确用于复杂推理链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前知识编辑方法虽能可靠地修正孤立事实，但在多步推理链中存在问题——模型能回忆起编辑的事实，却无法将其应用于复杂推理过程，这种"推理鸿沟"严重限制了知识编辑在真实动态环境中的应用效果。

Method: MCircKE框架采用"定位-适配"两阶段策略：首先识别特定推理任务对应的因果电路(causal circuits)，该电路同时捕获事实存储和逻辑传导；然后仅在该定位到的电路上进行精准的参数更新，实现手术式的编辑操作。

Result: 在MQuake-3K基准测试上的实验结果表明，该方法在多跳推理场景下的知识编辑任务中表现优异，有效弥合了推理鸿沟问题。

Conclusion: 通过因果电路定位与精准参数更新的结合，MCircKE能够确保编辑后的知识不仅被记忆，更能被正确地应用于复杂的多步推理过程中，为LLM知识更新提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) in real-world dynamic environments raises the challenge of updating their pre-trained knowledge. While existing knowledge editing methods can reliably patch isolated facts, they frequently suffer from a "Reasoning Gap", where the model recalls the edited fact but fails to utilize it in multi-step reasoning chains. To bridge this gap, we introduce MCircKE (\underline{M}echanistic \underline{Circ}uit-based \underline{K}nowledge \underline{E}diting), a novel framework that enables a precise "map-and-adapt" editing procedure. MCircKE first identifies the causal circuits responsible for a specific reasoning task, capturing both the storage of the fact and the routing of its logical consequences. It then surgically update parameters exclusively within this mapped circuit. Extensive experiments on the MQuAKE-3K benchmark demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method for multi-hop reasoning in knowledge editing.

</details>


### [58] [FRENCH-YMCA: A FRENCH Corpus meeting the language needs of Youth, froM Children to Adolescents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05899)
*Cherifa Ben Khelil,Jean-Yves Antoine,Anaïs Halftermeyer,Frédéric Rayar,Mathieu Thebaud*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: French-YMCA是一个专门为儿童和青少年设计的大型法语语言资源库，包含39,200个文本文件和约2,247万个单词，旨在为针对青少年语言理解的模型训练提供数据基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言资源多为成人语料，儿童语言具有独特性——处于不断发展的动态过程中，与成人语言存在显著差异，因此需要专门针对未成年人的语料库来支持相关研究和应用。

Method: 通过广泛收集来源多样的文本，经过标准化处理（语法和拼写一致性），构建了一个大规模法语青少年语言语料库，并承诺开放在线访问。

Result: French-YMCA语料库包含39,200个文本文件，共计22,471,898个单词，具有来源多样性、文本规范性和开放可访问性等特点。

Conclusion: 该语料库可作为训练语言模型的基础，使模型能够理解和预测青少年语言特征，从而提升数字交互质量，确保平台响应和推荐内容符合用户年龄特征和认知水平。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the French-YMCA corpus, a new linguistic resource specifically tailored for children and adolescents. The motivation for building this corpus is clear: children have unique language requirements, as their language skills are in constant evolution and differ from those of adults. With an extensive collection of 39,200 text files, the French-YMCA corpus encompasses a total of 22,471,898 words. It distinguishes itself through its diverse sources, consistent grammar and spelling, and the commitment to providing open online accessibility for all. Such corpus can serve as the foundation for training language models that understand and anticipate youth's language, thereby enhancing the quality of digital interactions and ensuring that responses and suggestions are age-appropriate and adapted to the comprehension level of users of this age.

</details>


### [59] [FrontierFinance: A Long-Horizon Computer-Use Benchmark of Real-World Financial Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05912)
*Michael Krumdick,Varshini Reddy,Shivani Chaudhary,William Day,Maarij Ahmed,Hayan Haqqi,Muhammad Ahsen Fahim,Hanzallah Amjad,Ahmad Orakzai,Aqsa Gul,Chris Tanner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FrontierFinance是一个长期金融基准测试，包含25个复杂金融建模任务，每个任务平均需要超过18小时的专业人力完成，旨在评估AI在金融领域专业任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI驱动的劳动力替代风险日益关注，尤其在知识密集型行业。现有基准测试无法有效衡量实际专业技能，且金融领域缺乏稳健的评估工具和问责机制。

Method: 开发了包含25个复杂金融建模任务的基准测试，涵盖五个核心金融模型。任务由金融专业人士设计，配备详细的评估标准rubrics，由人类专家定义任务、创建评分标准、评估LLM并提供人类基线。

Result: 人类专家平均得分更高，且更有可能产出客户就绪的输出结果。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的AI系统在金融专业任务上仍落后于人类专家，强调了AI在专业领域应用需谨慎推进。

Abstract: As concerns surrounding AI-driven labor displacement intensify in knowledge-intensive sectors, existing benchmarks fail to measure performance on tasks that define practical professional expertise. Finance, in particular, has been identified as a domain with high AI exposure risk, yet lacks robust benchmarks to track real-world developments. This gap is compounded by the absence of clear accountability mechanisms in current Large Language Model (LLM) deployments. To address this, we introduce FrontierFinance, a long-horizon benchmark of 25 complex financial modeling tasks across five core finance models, requiring an average of over 18 hours of skilled human labor per task to complete. Developed with financial professionals, the benchmark reflects industry-standard financial modeling workflows and is paired with detailed rubrics for structured evaluation. We engage human experts to define the tasks, create rubrics, grade LLMs, and perform the tasks themselves as human baselines. We demonstrate that our human experts both receive higher scores on average, and are more likely to provide client-ready outputs than current state-of-the-art systems.

</details>


### [60] ["I See What You Did There": Can Large Vision-Language Models Understand Multimodal Puns?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05930)
*Naen Xu,Jiayi Sheng,Changjiang Li,Chunyi Zhou,Yuyuan Li,Tianyu Du,Jun Wang,Zhihui Fu,Jinbao Li,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉-语言模型（VLM）的双关语理解能力进行系统研究。为解决缺乏基准数据集的问题，作者提出了一个多模态双关语生成pipeline，并构建了MultiPun数据集。实验表明现有模型在区分双关语方面表现不佳，通过提示级和模型级策略可实现显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双关语是一种常见的修辞手法，利用多义性和语音相似性创造幽默感。在多模态双关语中，视觉和文本元素协同作用来锚定字面意义并唤起比喻含义。尽管视觉-语言模型（VLM）广泛应用于多模态理解和生成，但由于缺乏严格的基准测试，其理解双关语的能力尚未得到系统研究。

Method: 提出了一个多模态双关语生成pipeline；构建了MultiPun数据集，包含多种类型的双关语及对抗性非双关语干扰项；对多个视觉-语言模型进行系统评估；设计了提示级和模型级策略以增强双关语理解能力。

Result: 大多数现有模型难以区分真正的双关语与干扰项；通过提出的改进策略，F1分数平均提升16.5%。

Conclusion: 提出了针对多模态幽默理解的模型改进策略（提示级和模型级），F1分数平均提升16.5%，为开发具有跨模态推理能力的多模态大模型提供有价值的洞察。

Abstract: Puns are a common form of rhetorical wordplay that exploits polysemy and phonetic similarity to create humor. In multimodal puns, visual and textual elements synergize to ground the literal sense and evoke the figurative meaning simultaneously. Although Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are widely used in multimodal understanding and generation, their ability to understand puns has not been systematically studied due to a scarcity of rigorous benchmarks. To address this, we first propose a multimodal pun generation pipeline. We then introduce MultiPun, a dataset comprising diverse types of puns alongside adversarial non-pun distractors. Our evaluation reveals that most models struggle to distinguish genuine puns from these distractors. Moreover, we propose both prompt-level and model-level strategies to enhance pun comprehension, with an average improvement of 16.5% in F1 scores. Our findings provide valuable insights for developing future VLMs that master the subtleties of human-like humor via cross-modal reasoning.

</details>


### [61] [BOSCH: Black-Box Binary Optimization for Short-Context Attention-Head Selection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05942)
*Abbas Ghaddar,Ivan Kobyzev,Boxing Chen,Yufei Cui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BOSCH是一种无需训练的LLM混合注意力优化方法，通过大邻域搜索解决滑动窗口注意力（SWA）中的头选择问题。该方法包含三个子问题：层重要性检测、自适应SWA比率分配和分组头级优化。实验表明，在1.7B至30B参数的4个LLM上，BOSCH始终优于层级和静态头级方法，且在不同SWA比率下表现出显著的头部更替。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后训练混合方案存在缺陷：层级方案忽略同层内局部/全局依赖的头级路由特性；静态头级排名遭受"纠缠"问题——混合后头部行为会改变。现有方法无法为不同目标SWA比率自适应选择最优注意力头配置。

Method: 提出BOSCH（Black-box Binary Optimization for Short-context Head Selection），将问题形式化为大邻域搜索（LNS），分解为三个子问题：(i)通过小预算黑盒探测进行层重要性检测；(ii)基于敏感性的自适应逐层SWA比率分配；(iii)比率桶内的分组头级优化。方法为训练无关（training-free）。

Result: 在4个LLM（1.7B-30B参数）、4种SWA比率上，BOSCH一致优于层级启发式和6种强静态头级方法，且SWA比率越高收益越大。持续预训练场景下，BOSCH能更快、更高水平恢复原始长上下文性能。

Conclusion: 不同SWA比率下BOSCH选择的头部存在显著更替（substantial turnover），强调了对每个目标比率单独执行头级选择的重要性，而非依赖固定局部性排名。静态排名方法失效的根本原因在于忽视混合后头部行为的动态变化。

Abstract: Post-training hybridization of large language models (LLMs) often replaces quadratic self-attention with sliding-window attention (SWA) to reduce KV cache usage and improve latency. Existing hybridization schemes are typically defined either at the layer level (e.g., interleaving) or at the head level via static rankings from local to global. Layer-level schemes ignore that local and global dependencies are routed through heads within the same layer, while static head-level rankings suffer from entanglement: a head's local/global behavior can change after hybridization. We propose BOSCH, Black-box Binary Optimization for Short-context Head Selection, a training-free method that formulates the problem as a Large Neighborhood Search and decomposes it into three subproblems: (i) layer-importance detection via small-budget black-box probes, (ii) adaptive per-layer SWA-ratio assignment based on these sensitivities, and (iii) grouped head-level optimization within ratio buckets. Extensive experiments on 4 LLMs ranging from 1.7B to 30B parameters, across 4 SWA ratios, show that BOSCH consistently outperforms layer-level heuristics and 6 strong static head-level methods, with larger gains at higher SWA ratios. Under continual pretraining, BOSCH recover original long-context performance faster and to a higher level. Analysis of the selected heads reveals substantial turnover for BOSCH across different SWA ratios, underscoring the importance of performing head-level selection for each target ratio rather than relying on fixed locality rankings.

</details>


### [62] [FinReporting: An Agentic Workflow for Localized Reporting of Cross-Jurisdiction Financial Disclosures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05966)
*Fan Zhang,Mingzi Song,Rania Elbadry,Yankai Chen,Shaobo Wang,Yixi Zhou,Xunwen Zheng,Yueru He,Yuyang Dai,Georgi Georgiev,Ayesha Gull,Muhammad Usman Safder,Fan Wu,Liyuan Meng,Fengxian Ji,Junning Zhao,Xueqing Peng,Jimin Huang,Yu Chen,Xue,Liu,Preslav Nakov,Zhuohan Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FinReporting是一个用于跨司法管辖区财务报告的智能体工作流系统，通过构建统一的规范本体ontology，将财务报告流程分解为可审计的阶段（文件获取、提取、规范映射和异常日志），并创新性地将LLM作为约束验证器而非自由形式生成器使用。系统在美、日、中三国年报上进行了评估，提升了异构报告制度下的一致性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有财务报告系统多假设单一市场设置，未能解决不同司法管辖区之间的结构性差异问题。不同国家的会计准则分类法、标记基础设施（如XBRL vs. PDF）以及汇总惯例存在差异，导致跨辖区报告面临语义对齐和验证的挑战。

Method: (1) 构建统一的规范本体ontology，覆盖三大财务报表（利润表、资产负债表、现金流量表）；(2) 将报告流程分解为四个可审计阶段：文件获取、提取、规范映射和异常日志；(3) 将LLM作为约束验证器使用，在明确决策规则和证据基础上运作，而非作为自由形式生成器；(4) 在美、日、中三国年报上进行评估。

Result: 系统提升了异构报告制度下一致性和可靠性，证明了该方法在跨辖区财务报告场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过规范本体约束和LLM验证器架构，可有效解决跨司法管辖区的财务报告语义对齐问题。

Abstract: Financial reporting systems increasingly use large language models (LLMs) to extract and summarize corporate disclosures. However, most assume a single-market setting and do not address structural differences across jurisdictions. Variations in accounting taxonomies, tagging infrastructures (e.g., XBRL vs. PDF), and aggregation conventions make cross-jurisdiction reporting a semantic alignment and verification challenge. We present FinReporting, an agentic workflow for localized cross-jurisdiction financial reporting. The system builds a unified canonical ontology over Income Statement, Balance Sheet, and Cash Flow, and decomposes reporting into auditable stages including filing acquisition, extraction, canonical mapping, and anomaly logging. Rather than using LLMs as free-form generators, FinReporting deploys them as constrained verifiers under explicit decision rules and evidence grounding. Evaluated on annual filings from the US, Japan, and China, the system improves consistency and reliability under heterogeneous reporting regimes. We release an interactive demo supporting cross-market inspection and structured export of localized financial statements. Our demo is available at https://huggingface.co/spaces/BoomQ/FinReporting-Demo . The video describing our system is available at https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=f65jdEL31Kk

</details>


### [63] [The Model Agreed, But Didn't Learn: Diagnosing Surface Compliance in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05995)
*Xiaojie Gu,Ziying Huang,Weicong Hong,Jian Xie,Renze Lou,Kai Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了当前知识编辑方法存在"表面顺从"问题——编辑器通过模仿目标输出获得高分，而非真正修改模型的内在信念。此外，递归编辑会累积表征残留，导致认知不稳定并降低记忆状态的可逆性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有知识编辑方法在标准基准测试上表现出高成功率，但这些评估框架是否真正验证了记忆的实质性修改仍存疑问。研究需要新的评估范式来检验编辑是否真正改变了模型的内在信念，而非仅在特定提示条件下模仿输出。

Method: 提出一个简单的诊断框架，让模型在上下文学习(ICL)设置下进行判别性自我评估，通过仔细审视记忆修改诱导的细微行为差异来检验编辑效果。

Result: 发现普遍存在的"表面顺从"(Surface Compliance)现象：编辑器通过模仿目标输出获得高分，但并未结构性覆写内在信念。递归修改会累积表征残留，触发认知不稳定并永久降低模型记忆状态的可逆性。

Conclusion: 当前编辑范式存在重大风险，稳健的记忆修改对构建可信、长期可持续的LLM系统至关重要。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) internalize vast world knowledge as parametric memory, yet inevitably inherit the staleness and errors of their source corpora. Consequently, ensuring the reliability and malleability of these internal representations is imperative for trustworthy real-world deployment. Knowledge editing offers a pivotal paradigm for surgically modifying memory without retraining. However, while recent editors demonstrate high success rates on standard benchmarks, it remains questionable whether current evaluation frameworks that rely on assessing output under specific prompting conditions can reliably authenticate genuine memory modification. In this work, we introduce a simple diagnostic framework that subjects models to discriminative self-assessment under in-context learning (ICL) settings that better reflect real-world application environments, specifically designed to scrutinize the subtle behavioral nuances induced by memory modifications. This probing reveals a pervasive phenomenon of Surface Compliance, where editors achieve high benchmark scores by merely mimicking target outputs without structurally overwriting internal beliefs. Moreover, we find that recursive modifications accumulate representational residues, triggering cognitive instability and permanently diminishing the reversibility of the model's memory state. These insights underscore the risks of current editing paradigms and highlight the pivotal role of robust memory modification in building trustworthy, long-term sustainable LLM systems. Code is available at https://github.com/XiaojieGu/SA-MCQ.

</details>


### [64] [Disentangling MLP Neuron Weights in Vocabulary Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06005)
*Asaf Avrahamy,Yoav Gur-Arieh,Mor Geva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ROTATE是一种无需数据、无需前向传播的权重空间方法，通过优化神经元权重的旋转来最大化词汇空间峰度，从而恢复可解释的"词汇通道"。在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Gemma-2-2B-it上的实验表明，该方法能恢复忠实于神经元行为的可解释方向，通道级描述聚合效果优于激活基线方法2-3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型权重中编码信息的可解释性是机制可解释性的核心挑战。现有方法多依赖激活数据或前向传播，计算开销大且难以扩展。

Method: ROTATE利用关键统计观察：编码连贯单语义概念的神经元在投影到词汇空间时呈现高峰度(kurtosis)。通过优化神经元权重的旋转使词汇空间峰度最大化，直接在权重空间 disentangle MLP神经元，恢复稀疏可解释的方向——词汇通道(vocabulary channels)。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Gemma-2-2B-it上验证：(1)恢复的词汇通道忠实于神经元行为；(2)单独消融通道可选择性禁用对应输入激活或特定概念的促进；(3)通道级描述聚合生成的全面的神经元描述优于优化激活基线2-3倍。

Conclusion: ROTATE提供了神经元权重的无数据分解，是可扩展的细粒度LM可解释性构建模块，为大规模语言模型的机制可解释性研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: Interpreting the information encoded in model weights remains a fundamental challenge in mechanistic interpretability. In this work, we introduce ROTATE (Rotation-Optimized Token Alignment in weighT spacE), a data-free method requiring no forward passes that disentangles MLP neurons directly in weight space. Our approach relies on a key statistical observation: neurons that encode coherent, monosemantic concepts exhibit high kurtosis when projected onto the model's vocabulary. By optimizing rotations of neuron weights to maximize their vocabulary-space kurtosis, our method recovers sparse, interpretable directions which we name vocabulary channels. Experiments on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Gemma-2-2B-it demonstrate that ROTATE consistently recovers vocabulary channels that are faithful to the neuron's behavior. ablating individual channels selectively disables corresponding input activations or the promotion of specific concepts. Moreover, aggregating channel-level descriptions yields comprehensive neuron descriptions that outperform optimized activation-based baselines by 2-3x in head-to-head comparisons. By providing a data-free decomposition of neuron weights, ROTATE offers a scalable, fine-grained building block for interpreting LMs.

</details>


### [65] [BiMind: A Dual-Head Reasoning Model with Attention-Geometry Adapter for Incorrect Information Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06022)
*Zhongxing Zhang,Emily K. Vraga,Jisu Huh,Jaideep Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BiMind框架，通过双头推理机制将内容内部推理与知识增强推理解耦，并引入注意力几何适配器、自检索知识机制和不确定性感知融合策略来解决错误信息检测中的注意力崩溃问题，提升检测性能并提供可解释性诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错误信息检测面临重大挑战：现有方法难以在 collapsed attention geometries 条件下平衡文本内容验证与外部知识修改。注意力崩溃（attention collapse）问题限制了模型对知识增强推理的有效利用。

Method: 提出BiMind双头推理框架，包含三大核心创新：(1) 注意力几何适配器，通过token条件化偏移量重塑注意力logits，缓解注意力崩溃；(2) 自检索知识机制，通过kNN检索构建领域语义记忆，通过特征级线性调制注入检索邻居；(3) 不确定性感知融合策略，包括熵门控融合和可训练 agreement head，由对称KL散度agreement正则化器稳定训练。

Result: 在公开数据集上的实验结果表明，BiMind模型优于先进检测方法，并提供可解释性诊断，揭示知识何时及为何起作用。

Conclusion: 引入的VoX（Value-of-eXperience）指标可量化知识贡献的实例级logit增益，为理解知识增强推理的价值提供了可解释的评估框架。

Abstract: Incorrect information poses significant challenges by disrupting content veracity and integrity, yet most detection approaches struggle to jointly balance textual content verification with external knowledge modification under collapsed attention geometries. To address this issue, we propose a dual-head reasoning framework, BiMind, which disentangles content-internal reasoning from knowledge-augmented reasoning. In BiMind, we introduce three core innovations: (i) an attention geometry adapter that reshapes attention logits via token-conditioned offsets and mitigates attention collapse; (ii) a self-retrieval knowledge mechanism, which constructs an in-domain semantic memory through kNN retrieval and injects retrieved neighbors via feature-wise linear modulation; (iii) the uncertainty-aware fusion strategies, including entropy-gated fusion and a trainable agreement head, stabilized by a symmetric Kullback-Leibler agreement regularizer. To quantify the knowledge contributions, we define a novel metric, Value-of-eXperience (VoX), to measure instance-wise logit gains from knowledge-augmented reasoning. Experiment results on public datasets demonstrate that our BiMind model outperforms advanced detection approaches and provides interpretable diagnostics on when and why knowledge matters.

</details>


### [66] [From Hallucination to Structure Snowballing: The Alignment Tax of Constrained Decoding in LLM Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06066)
*Hongxu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨通过Outlines约束解码强制结构化反思是否能在无需额外训练的情况下打破LLM推理中的错误传播。结果表明，简单施加结构约束不仅无法提升自修正性能，反而触发"结构雪球效应"这一新失败模式——模型为满足严格格式要求而产生认知过载，导致近乎完美的句法对齐却无法检测语义错误，揭示了约束解码中结构粒度与模型内部能力之间的"对齐税"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在开放域推理任务中常因"幻觉雪球效应"导致早期错误递归强化。虽然结构化反馈可缓解此问题，但现有方法依赖外部批评者或符号工具，降低了智能体自主性。本研究旨在探索纯通过约束解码实现结构化反思是否能在不增加额外训练的前提下打破错误传播链。

Method: 使用8B参数模型Qwen3-8B，对比自由文本反思与Outlines约束解码两种条件下的自修正表现，通过格式化约束引导模型遵循特定输出结构。

Result: 单纯施加结构约束未能改善自修正性能，反而触发新型失败模式"结构雪球效应"。模型为满足严格格式规则产生认知负担，陷入"格式陷阱"，表现为接近完美的句法对齐却无法识别深层语义错误。

Conclusion: 约束解码存在固有的"对齐税"问题：结构粒度与模型内部能力之间存在张力。结构化约束可能以牺牲语义准确性为代价换取句法规范性，这对自主智能体的工作流设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Intrinsic self-correction in Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently fails in open-ended reasoning tasks due to ``hallucination snowballing,'' a phenomenon in which models recursively justify early errors during free-text reflection. While structured feedback can mitigate this issue, existing approaches often rely on externally trained critics or symbolic tools, reducing agent autonomy. This study investigates whether enforcing structured reflection purely through Outlines-based constrained decoding can disrupt error propagation without additional training. Evaluating an 8-billion-parameter model (Qwen3-8B), we show that simply imposing structural constraints does not improve self-correction performance. Instead, it triggers a new failure mode termed ``structure snowballing.'' We find that the cognitive load required to satisfy strict formatting rules pushes the model into formatting traps. This observation helps explain why the agent achieves near-perfect superficial syntactic alignment yet fails to detect or resolve deeper semantic errors. These findings expose an ``alignment tax'' inherent to constrained decoding, highlighting a tension between structural granularity and internal model capacity in autonomous workflows. Code and raw logs are available in the GitHub repository: https://github.com/hongxuzhou/agentic_llm_structured_self_critique.

</details>


### [67] [Short Data, Long Context: Distilling Positional Knowledge in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06070)
*Patrick Huber,Ernie Chang,Chinnadhurai Sankar,Rylan Conway,Igor Fedorov,Md Rifat Arefin,Adithya Sagar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过基于logit的知识蒸馏将长上下文检索能力迁移到学生模型，无需昂贵的预训练，仅使用打包的短上下文样本即可实现。核心发现包括：分阶段RoPE缩放效果最佳；知识蒸馏可直接实现位置信息迁移；查询状态在长上下文扩展中呈现结构化更新模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展语言模型上下文窗口通常需要昂贵的长期预训练，对训练效率和数据收集都带来重大挑战。本文旨在探索是否可以通过知识蒸馏方法，在不进行长上下文预训练的情况下，将长上下文能力迁移到学生模型。

Method: 采用基于logit的知识蒸馏方法，在长上下文窗口内仅使用打包的短上下文样本进行训练。通过旋转位置嵌入（RoPE）的视角进行全面分析，并设计使用打包重复token序列的实验设置，追踪位置扰动从Query和Key向量通过transformer层到输出logits的传播过程。

Result: 三个关键发现：1）分阶段RoPE缩放可最大化各训练阶段的旋转频谱利用率，在知识蒸馏设置下也取得最佳长上下文性能；2）基于logit的知识蒸馏可直接实现位置信息迁移，位置信息系统性地影响教师模型的输出分布，进而传递给学生模型；3）分析发现查询状态在长上下文扩展中呈现结构化更新模式，不同参数范围对长上下文训练的敏感性存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 通过知识蒸馏可在不进行长上下文预训练的情况下有效实现长上下文能力迁移，RoPE缩放策略和位置信息传递机制是实现这一目标的关键因素，为低成本的模型上下文扩展提供了新思路。

Abstract: Extending the context window of language models typically requires expensive long-context pre-training, posing significant challenges for both training efficiency and data collection. In this paper, we present evidence that long-context retrieval capabilities can be transferred to student models through logit-based knowledge distillation, even when training exclusively on packed short-context samples within a long-context window. We provide comprehensive insights through the lens of Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) and establish three key findings. First, consistent with prior work, we show that phase-wise RoPE scaling, which maximizes rotational spectrum utilization at each training stage, also achieves the best long-context performance in knowledge distillation setups. Second, we demonstrate that logit-based knowledge distillation can directly enable positional information transfer. Using an experimental setup with packed repeated token sequences, we trace the propagation of positional perturbations from query and key vectors through successive transformer layers to output logits, revealing that positional information systematically influences the teacher's output distribution and, in turn, the distillation signal received by the student model. Third, our analysis uncovers structured update patterns in the query state during long-context extension, with distinct parameter spans exhibiting strong sensitivity to long-context training.

</details>


### [68] [LAG-XAI: A Lie-Inspired Affine Geometric Framework for Interpretable Paraphrasing in Transformer Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06086)
*Olexander Mazurets,Olexander Barmak,Leonid Bedratyuk,Iurii Krak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LAG-XAI是一种基于李代数几何的可解释AI框架，将释义建模为嵌入空间中的仿射变换（旋转、形变、平移），在Twitter语料上达到0.7713的AUC，同时保持了可解释性（27.84°稳定角度、近零形变），并成功应用于LLM幻觉检测（95.3%检测率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Transformer语言模型如同"黑箱"，其潜在语义空间难以解释。该研究旨在通过几何方法突破这一瓶颈，将离散的语言现象建模为连续的几何变换，实现对Transformer机制的透明化理解。

Method: LAG-XAI框架将释义理解为语义流形上的连续几何流动，借助局部李群作用提出计算高效的均值场近似。将释义转换分解为三个几何可解释分量：旋转（rotation）、形变（deformation）和平移（translation）。使用Sentence-BERT编码文本，采用PIT-2015 Twitter语料库进行实验。

Result: 1. AUC达0.7713，相比非线性的强基线（0.8405）保留了约80%有效分类能力
2. 发现稳定的矩阵重配置角（~27.84°）和近零形变，表明局部等距性
3. 在独立TURL数据集上验证了跨域泛化能力
4. HaluEval幻觉检测：使用"廉价几何检查"自动检测出95.3%的事实扭曲

Conclusion: LAG-XAI提供了数学严格且资源高效的可解释路径。通过将语义变换映射到几何不变量（旋转角等），可追踪Transformer的决策机制，同时在幻觉检测等安全关键任务中展现实际应用价值。

Abstract: Modern Transformer-based language models achieve strong performance in natural language processing tasks, yet their latent semantic spaces remain largely uninterpretable black boxes. This paper introduces LAG-XAI (Lie Affine Geometry for Explainable AI), a novel geometric framework that models paraphrasing not as discrete word substitutions, but as a structured affine transformation within the embedding space. By conceptualizing paraphrasing as a continuous geometric flow on a semantic manifold, we propose a computationally efficient mean-field approximation, inspired by local Lie group actions. This allows us to decompose paraphrase transitions into geometrically interpretable components: rotation, deformation, and translation. Experiments on the noisy PIT-2015 Twitter corpus, encoded with Sentence-BERT, reveal a "linear transparency" phenomenon. The proposed affine operator achieves an AUC of 0.7713. By normalizing against random chance (AUC 0.5), the model captures approximately 80% of the non-linear baseline's effective classification capacity (AUC 0.8405), offering explicit parametric interpretability in exchange for a marginal drop in absolute accuracy. The model identifies fundamental geometric invariants, including a stable matrix reconfiguration angle (~27.84°) and near-zero deformation, indicating local isometry. Cross-domain generalization is confirmed via direct cross-corpus validation on an independent TURL dataset. Furthermore, the practical utility of LAG-XAI is demonstrated in LLM hallucination detection: using a "cheap geometric check," the model automatically detected 95.3% of factual distortions on the HaluEval dataset by registering deviations beyond the permissible semantic corridor. This approach provides a mathematically grounded, resource-efficient path toward the mechanistic interpretability of Transformers.

</details>


### [69] [Exclusive Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06154)
*Mutsumi Sasaki,Kouta Nakayama,Yusuke Miyao,Yohei Oseki,Masaru Isonuma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"排他性遗忘"（Exclusive Unlearning）方法，通过广泛遗忘除目标知识外的所有内容来实现LLM安全防护，而非传统方法中逐一列举有害内容进行删除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前将LLM应用于医疗、教育等工业场景时，生成有害内容的风险是重大挑战。现有机器遗忘方法需逐一列举待遗忘目标，但有害内容多样性导致全面删除困难。

Method: 提出排他性遗忘（EU）策略：不是逐一列出遗忘目标，而是采用"广泛遗忘除目标知识外的所有内容"的反向思路，通过保留特定领域（如医学、数学）知识的同时，消除其他所有可能产生有害内容的响应能力。

Result: 实验证明排他性遗忘方法可使模型：1）针对包括越狱攻击在内的多种输入保持安全性；2）仍能响应医学、数学等特定领域的指令。

Conclusion: 排他性遗忘是一种有效的LLM安全防护方法，通过"反向遗忘"策略，在保证广泛安全性的同时保留特定领域能力。

Abstract: When introducing Large Language Models (LLMs) into industrial applications, such as healthcare and education, the risk of generating harmful content becomes a significant challenge. While existing machine unlearning methods can erase specific harmful knowledge and expressions, diverse harmful content makes comprehensive removal difficult. In this study, instead of individually listing targets for forgetting, we propose Exclusive Unlearning (EU), which aims for broad harm removal by extensively forgetting everything except for the knowledge and expressions we wish to retain. We demonstrate that through Exclusive Unlearning, it is possible to obtain a model that ensures safety against a wide range of inputs, including jailbreaks, while maintaining the ability to respond to diverse instructions related to specific domains such as medicine and mathematics.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [70] [EduIllustrate: Towards Scalable Automated Generation Of Multimodal Educational Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05005)
*Shuzhen Bi,Mingzi Zhang,Zhuoxuan Li,Xiaolong Wang,keqian Li,Aimin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: EduIllustrate是一个用于评估LLM生成K-12 STEM多模态教学内容的基准测试，包含230道题目、5个学科、3个年级水平，以及8维评估体系和顺序锚定生成协议。Gemini 3.0 Pro Preview表现最佳(87.8%)，Kimi-K2.5成本效益最高($0.12/题)。顺序锚定策略可将视觉一致性提升13%且降低成本94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM教育能力评估主要集中于问答和辅导任务，缺乏对多模态教学内容生成能力的系统评估。关键缺口在于：LLM能否生成结合几何精确视觉与逐步推理的图文交织解释。

Method: 构建EduIllustrate基准测试：(1)230道K-12 STEM题目，涵盖5学科3年级；(2)顺序锚定生成协议确保跨图视觉一致性；(3)基于多媒体学习理论的8维评估标准；(4)对10个LLM进行评估，并进行工作流消融实验和20位专家的人类评估验证。

Result: Gemini 3.0 Pro Preview以87.8%的准确率领先；Kimi-K2.5达到80.8%且成本效益最佳($0.12/题)。顺序锚定使视觉一致性提升13%，成本降低94%。LLM作为评判者在客观维度上与人类评估高度一致(ρ≥0.83)，但在主观视觉评估上存在局限。

Conclusion: EduIllustrate填补了LLM多模态教育内容生成评估的空白，揭示了当前模型在图文一致性方面的差距，为未来教育AI系统的开发提供了标准化评估框架和成本效益分析。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as educational assistants, yet evaluation of their educational capabilities remains concentrated on question-answering and tutoring tasks. A critical gap exists for multimedia instructional content generation -- the ability to produce coherent, diagram-rich explanations that combine geometrically accurate visuals with step-by-step reasoning. We present EduIllustrate, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs on interleaved text-diagram explanation generation for K-12 STEM problems. The benchmark comprises 230 problems spanning five subjects and three grade levels, a standardized generation protocol with sequential anchoring to enforce cross-diagram visual consistency, and an 8-dimension evaluation rubric grounded in multimedia learning theory covering both text and visual quality. Evaluation of ten LLMs reveals a wide performance spread: Gemini 3.0 Pro Preview leads at 87.8\%, while Kimi-K2.5 achieves the best cost-efficiency (80.8\% at \\$0.12/problem). Workflow ablation confirms sequential anchoring improves Visual Consistency by 13\% at 94\% lower cost. Human evaluation with 20 expert raters validates LLM-as-judge reliability for objective dimensions ($ρ\geq 0.83$) while revealing limitations on subjective visual assessment.

</details>


### [71] [Context Collapse: Barriers to Adoption for Generative AI in Workplace Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05151)
*Emanuel Moss,Elizabeth Watkins,Christopher Persaud,Dawn Nafus,Passant Karunaratne,Mona Sloane*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过专家访谈研究生成式AI在工作场景中如何处理用户上下文的失败及其应对策略。研究发现计算方法在处理上下文时存在根本性缺陷：多个上下文相互混淆或随时间"腐化"退化。作者呼吁从无差别的上下文数据收集转向更具交互性的实践方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI工具在工作场景中对用户上下文的处理方式未能满足用户期望和需求，需要深入理解这些失败的表现形式及其根本原因。

Method: 专家访谈（empirical expert interviews）

Result: 识别出计算式上下文处理的多个缺陷：上下文相互侵蚀或随时间退化；工具开发者、用户和社会科学家对"上下文"的概念化存在差异，导致系统性错位。

Conclusion: 建议从无差别的上下文数据收集转向更具交互性的实践方式，以更恰当地将生成式AI嵌入用户的使用情境中。

Abstract: As generative AI technologies are pressed into service in workplace settings, current approaches to account for the contexts in which such technologies are used fall short of users' expectations and needs. This paper empirically demonstrates, through expert interviews, both how these tools fail to account for users' context and how users deploy concrete strategies address such failures. The paper analyzes how context is variously conceptualized by tool developers, users, and social scientists to identify specific pitfalls inherent in computational approaches to context. Multiple distinct contexts tend to collapse into one another or rot, degrading over time, reducing the utility of any efforts to account for context. The paper concludes with a provocation to shift from an indiscriminate collection of context-relevant data toward a more interactional set of practices to embed GenAI systems more appropriately into users' contexts of use.

</details>


### [72] [A Multi-Agent Approach to Validate and Refine LLM-Generated Personalized Math Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05160)
*Fareya Ikram,Nischal Ashok Kumar,Junyang Lu,Hunter McNichols,Candace Walkington,Neil Heffernan,Andrew S. Lan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多智能体框架，通过生成-验证-修订的迭代流程，利用LLM为学生生成个性化数学题目。该框架包含四个专业验证智能体（可解性、现实性、可读性、真实性），在600道来自ASSISTments平台的题目上进行评估。研究发现真实性和现实性是LLM生成题目最常见的失败模式，单次修订迭代即可显著减少这些失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM生成的个性化数学题目存在数量不现实、上下文不合理、可读性差、真实性不足以及数学不一致等问题，需要一个系统化的方法来提升生成质量。

Method: 设计多智能体框架，将个性化过程形式化为迭代的生成-验证-修订流程，使用四个专门验证智能体分别针对可解性、现实性、可读性、真实性和数学一致性进行检验，评估三种不同的反馈协调修订策略。

Result: 真实性和现实性是个性化题目最常见的失败模式；单次修订迭代可显著减少这些失败；不同修订策略在不同评估标准上各有优势；验证器的可靠性在现实性上最高，在真实性上最低。

Conclusion: 多智能体验证-修订框架能有效提升LLM生成个性化数学题目的质量；需要更好的评估协议来考虑教师和学生的个人特征，以提升真实性验证的可靠性。

Abstract: Students benefit from math problems contextualized to their interests. Large language models (LLMs) offer promise for efficient personalization at scale. However, LLM-generated personalized problems may often have problems such as unrealistic quantities and contexts, poor readability, limited authenticity with respect to students' experiences, and occasional mathematical inconsistencies. To alleviate these problems, we propose a multi-agent framework that formalizes personalization as an iterative generate--validate--revise process; we use four specialized validator agents targeting the criteria of solvability, realism, readability, and authenticity, respectively. We evaluate our framework on 600 problems drawn from a popular online mathematics homework platform, ASSISTments, personalizing each problem to a fixed set of 20 student interest topics. We compare three refinement strategies that differ in how validation feedback is coordinated into revisions. Results show that authenticity and realism are the most frequent failure modes in initial LLM-personalized problems, but that a single refinement iteration substantially reduces these failures. We further find that different refinement strategies have different strengths on different criteria. We also assess validator reliability via human evaluation. Results show that reliability is highest on realism and lowest on authenticity, highlighting the need for better evaluation protocols that consider teachers' and students' personal characteristics.

</details>


### [73] [AI-Augmented Peer Review and Scientific Productivity: A Cross-Country Panel and SEM Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05463)
*Dongsoo Han*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究首次跨国实证验证了AI辅助同行评审系统对国家层面科学生产力的影响，通过构建AI评审能力指数(AIRC)，发现其可使科学生产力提升18-25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统同行评审存在效率低下问题，但缺乏对AI整合在国家层面系统性影响的实证研究。

Method: 构建了AI评审能力指数(AIRC)，采用OECD国家面板数据，运用固定效应回归和结构方程模型(SEM)进行分析。

Result: AI辅助评审显著提升科学生产力并降低研究质量差异。AIRC每增加一个标准差，科学生产力提升18-25%，该效应通过改善评审效率和可重复性来实现。

Conclusion: 本研究为AI增强型科学评价系统提供了首个跨国实证验证，表明AI可作为知识生产的结构性驱动因素，对推进科研评价体系改革具有政策参考价值。

Abstract: This study empirically investigates the impact of AI-augmented peer review systems on scientific productivity using panel data from OECD countries. While prior research has highlighted inefficiencies in traditional peer review, little empirical work has quantified the systemic impact of AI integration at the national level. We construct a novel AI Review Capability Index (AIRC) and examine its effects on research productivity, reproducibility, and innovation output. Using fixed-effects regression and structural equation modeling (SEM), we show that AI-assisted evaluation significantly enhances productivity and reduces variance in research quality. Results indicate that a one standard deviation increase in AIRC is associated with an 18-25% increase in scientific productivity, mediated through improvements in review efficiency and reproducibility. This paper provides the first cross-country empirical validation of AI-augmented scientific evaluation systems and contributes to the emerging literature on AI as a structural driver of knowledge production.

</details>


### [74] [From Pixels to Personas: Tracking the Evolution of Anime Characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05507)
*Rongze Liu,Jiaxin Pei,Jian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究利用大规模多模态动漫角色数据集，结合LLM提取的人格特征与头像特征，运用计算方法分析了动漫角色的人格原型与视觉刻板印象的历时演变规律。研究揭示了动漫目标受众从儿童向青少年/青年人群体的系统性转变，以及角色设计"萌化"趋势，发现视觉信号在塑造观众偏好方面比人格特征更具主导作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动漫文化现象研究对动漫角色如何随时间演变缺乏深入理解。尽管动漫作为文化产品在现代社会影响力巨大，尤其在年轻群体中广受欢迎，但尚未有研究系统揭示其角色设计的历时演变规律及其与受众偏好的关系。

Method: 研究团队从动漫评论网站获取大规模多模态动漫角色数据集，整合文本、视觉和生产特征信息，结合在线人气追踪数据。使用LLM提取角色人格特征，并与头像特征进行整合分析，识别反复出现的人格原型和视觉刻板印象及其跨十年期的时序演变。

Result: 研究获得四项主要发现：(1)动漫目标受众经历了从儿童向青少年/青年群体的系统性转变；(2)角色设计呈现"萌化"趋势，2000年代以来更柔和或性化的女性特征日益突出；(3)部分人格原型在视觉上具有可预测性，但观众也倾向于偏好非常规化的角色；(4)视觉信号在塑造观众偏好方面比人格特征更具主导作用，"萌"系面孔和机械设计等人格特征对人气贡献巨大。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了视觉信号（尤其是"萌"系特征和机械设计元素）在决定动漫角色流行度中的核心作用。这些发现为理解动漫文化与创意实践的更广泛动态提供了重要洞察，表明动漫产业正在经历从儿童娱乐向成熟受众市场的转型。

Abstract: Anime, originated from Japan, is one of the most influential cultural products in modern society and is especially popular among younger generations. The popularity of anime reflects important cultural evolutions in our society. Despite existing research on anime as a cultural phenomenon, we still have a limited understanding of how anime really evolves over the years. In this study, using a large-scale multimodal dataset of anime characters from an anime review site, we applied computational methods that integrate textual, visual, and production features of anime characters with online popularity traces. By combining LLM-extracted personality features with avatar features, we identify recurring personality archetypes and visual tropes with their temporal evolution over the past decades. We found that the target audience of anime has undergone a systematic shift from children to a maturing audience of teenagers and young adults over time. Character design has been undergoing moe-ification, with softer or sexualized female traits becoming increasingly prominent since the 2000s. Some personality archetypes are often visually predictable, yet audiences also tend to prefer less conventionalized characters. Finally, we reveal that visual signals play a more dominant role than personality traits in shaping audience preferences, with features such as moe-style faces and mechanical designs contributing greatly to popularity. These findings offer insights into the broader dynamics of anime's cultural and creative practices.

</details>


### [75] [Conditional Publics: Shared Events and Divergent Meanings in the European Twitter Debate on the Ukraine War](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05800)
*Corrado Monti,Arthur Capozzi,Yelena Mejova,Gianmarco De Francisci Morales*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究分析了俄乌战争前8个月来自20个欧洲国家的3800万条推文，发现欧洲各国内部普遍存在"鹰派"与"鸽派"观点集群。 polarization并非源于激进化的加剧，而是由普通用户的退出所驱动。对立阵营是否关注同一事件取决于议题性质：务实性议题下形成对抗性公共领域，而解释性议题下则形成情感性公共领域与反公共领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前地缘政治危机频发，理解公众如何在社交媒体上讨论冲突具有重要意义。研究的核心问题是：欧洲公众在讨论俄乌冲突时是否共享同一个信息现实？结构性极化是如何形成的？对立观点群体是否会关注相同的事件？

Method: 研究采集了3800万条来自20个欧洲国家的带地理标记的推文，通过转发社区检测识别意见群体，并对六个议题进行立场标注以区分"鹰派"与"鸽派"立场。

Result: 研究发现：①几乎每个国家都存在鹰派与鸽派两大阵营；②极化的驱动因素是普通用户的退出而非激进化的加剧；③对立群体是否共享事件参照框架取决于议题类型——务实性议题下双方对同一高关注事件做出反应，形成"对抗性公共领域"，而解释性议题下双方构建了不同的情感性公共领域。

Conclusion: 研究提出了"条件性公共领域"(Conditional Publics)概念，用以描述其关系结构（共享或分裂参照框架）取决于辩论议题的认知性质的公共形成体。这一框架有助于理解数字时代极化并非简单的好战vs温和二元对立，而是在不同议题上呈现动态的公共领域结构。

Abstract: How do European publics debate a geopolitical crisis on social media, and do they inhabit a shared informational reality? We analyze over 38 million geolocated tweets from 20 European countries during the first eight months of the Russian invasion of Ukraine. Using retweet community detection and stance annotation across six issues, we identify 'hawkish' and 'doveish' opinion clusters present within almost every country studied. We find that structural polarization is driven not by radicalization, but by the exit of casual users. Crucially, whether opposing sides orient to the same events depends on the issue. On pragmatist issues, both sides react to the same high-profile events, forming an agonistic public sphere. Instead, on interpretive issues, they operate as affective publics and counterpublics constructing divergent meanings. We propose conditional publics to describe formations whose relational structure, sharing or fracturing a referential frame, depends on the epistemic character of the debated issue.

</details>


### [76] [Governance and Regulation of Artificial Intelligence in Developing Countries: A Case Study of Nigeria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06018)
*Uloma Okoro,Tammy Mckenzie,Branislav Radeljic*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究以尼日利亚为案例，探讨法律专业人士对发展中国家人工智能治理的看法。研究采用定性案例研究方法，通过27次半结构化访谈和7人焦点小组访谈收集数据。研究发现，法律从业者对数据隐私风险和缺乏可执行的法律框架表示担忧，对机构能力信心有限，强调需要本地化适应的人工智能治理模式，而非直接照搬外国框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能技术的快速发展对发展中国家的法律和监管框架提出了挑战。现有研究多聚焦于发达国家的AI治理实践，而对发展中国家特别是非洲国家法律专业人士的视角关注不足。本研究旨在填补这一研究空白，为发展中国家量身定制AI治理策略提供实证依据。

Method: 研究采用定性案例研究设计，以尼日利亚为研究情境。数据收集方法包括：(1)对27名尼日利亚法律从业者进行半结构化访谈；(2)与来自金融、保险和公司法等领域的7名法律从业者进行焦点小组讨论。数据采用主题分析法进行分析，以识别受访者回应中的关键模式。

Result: 研究结果显示：①法律从业者对数据隐私风险表示担忧；②现有法律框架缺乏可执行性；③对机构能力信心有限；④强调需要本地化适应的治理模式，而非直接采用外国框架；⑤对AI潜力持谨慎乐观态度，但强调需有强有力的法律监督和公众问责机制。

Conclusion: 研究为发展中国家AI治理话语体系做出了贡献，强调监管方法应具有情境适应性、包容性，并能够弥合全球伦理原则与本地现实之间的差距。研究结论为政策制定者、监管机构和学者提供了实践指导，特别适用于与尼日利亚具有相似社会经济和法律环境的发展中国家。

Abstract: This study examines the perception of legal professionals on the governance of AI in developing countries, using Nigeria as a case study. The study focused on ethical risks, regulatory gaps, and institutional readiness. The study adopted a qualitative case study design. Data were collected through 27 semi-structured interviews with legal practitioners in Nigeria. A focus group discussion was also held with seven additional legal practitioners across sectors such as finance, insurance, and corporate law. Thematic analysis was employed to identify key patterns in participant responses. Findings showed that there were concerns about data privacy risks and the lack of enforceable legal frameworks. Participants expressed limited confidence in institutional capacity and emphasized the need for locally adapted governance models rather than direct adoption of foreign frameworks. While some expressed optimism about AI's potential, this was conditional on the presence of strong legal oversight and public accountability. The study contributes to the growing discourse on AI governance in developing countries by focusing on the perspectives of legal professionals. It highlights the importance of regulatory approaches that are context-specific, inclusive, and capable of bridging the gap between global ethical principles and local realities. These insights offer practical guidance for policymakers, regulators, and scholars working to shape responsible AI governance in similar environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>
