Motivation: 现有强化学习方法生成不透明神经策略难以部署，程序合成依赖限制性领域特定语言；交通信号控制需要既有效又可解释的策略，同时需处理紧急车辆通行、公交优先等多样化事件场景。

Method: 1）LLM作为进化技能生成器生成包含理由、选择指导和可执行代码的控制技能；2）模拟器指标转化为自然语言反馈引导技能改进（进化信号）；3）事件检测器识别四类事件，优先调度器选择专门技能；4）技能独立进化，运行时通过优先链组合无需重训练。

Result: 常规场景：平均延迟7.8-9.2秒，达最优方法的93%-97%，随机种子方差低；事件场景：紧急车辆延迟11.2-18.5秒（vs MaxPressure 42.3-72.3秒、DQN 78.5-95.3秒），公交人员延迟9.8-11.5秒（vs MaxPressure 38.7-45.2秒）；技能从简单线性规则进化为多特征交互的条件策略。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的进化框架可生成高效且完全可解释的交通信号控制技能，事件驱动组合机制有效处理多样化场景，技能可直接被交通工程师检查和修改，为可部署的可解释TSC提供新范式。

Abstract: Traffic signal control TSC requires strategies that are both effective and interpretable for deployment, yet reinforcement learning produces opaque neural policies while program synthesis depends on restrictive domain-specific languages. We present SIGNALCLAW, a framework that uses large language models LLMs as evolutionary skill generators to synthesize and refine interpretable control skills for adaptive TSC. Each skill includes rationale, selection guidance, and executable code, making policies human-inspectable and self-documenting. At each generation, evolution signals from simulation metrics such as queue percentiles, delay trends, and stagnation are translated into natural language feedback to guide improvement. SignalClaw also introduces event-driven compositional evolution: an event detector identifies emergency vehicles, transit priority, incidents, and congestion via TraCI, and a priority dispatcher selects specialized skills. Each skill is evolved independently, and a priority chain enables runtime composition without retraining. We evaluate SignalClaw on routine and event-injected SUMO scenarios against four baselines. On routine scenarios, it achieves average delay of 7.8 to 9.2 seconds, within 3 to 10 percent of the best method, with low variance across random seeds. Under event scenarios, it yields the lowest emergency delay 11.2 to 18.5 seconds versus 42.3 to 72.3 for MaxPressure and 78.5 to 95.3 for DQN, and the lowest transit person delay 9.8 to 11.5 seconds versus 38.7 to 45.2 for MaxPressure. In mixed events, the dispatcher composes skills effectively while maintaining stable overall delay. The evolved skills progress from simple linear rules to conditional strategies with multi-feature interactions, while remaining fully interpretable and directly modifiable by traffic engineers.

</details>


### [148] [Label Effects: Shared Heuristic Reliance in Trust Assessment by Humans and LLM-as-a-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05593)
*Xin Sun,Di Wu,Sijing Qin,Isao Echizen,Abdallah El Ali,Saku Sugawara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM判断者对信息来源标签存在偏见，相同内容被标记为"人类创作"时获得的信任度高于"AI生成"，这种偏见与人类使用标签作为判断启发式的行为一致，且可能通过偏好对齐被传播到模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型被广泛用作自动化评估工具（LLM-as-a-Judge），其评估结果的可靠性受到质疑。研究旨在探究LLM判断者是否与人类一样，会受到信息来源标签的偏见影响。

Method: 采用反事实设计（counterfactual design），同时收集人类和LLM的判断数据；使用眼动追踪技术记录人类阅读模式；分析LLM内部状态（注意力权重、logits）以揭示模型决策机制。

Result: 1. 人类和LLM均对"人类创作"标签赋予更高信任度；2. 人类依赖标签作为判断的启发式线索；3. LLM在标签区域的注意力密度高于内容区域，且"Human"标签下的标签主导效应更强；4. "AI"标签下决策不确定性更高。

Conclusion: 信息来源标签是影响人类和LLM判断的显著启发式线索，LLM-as-a-Judge在标签敏感场景下存在效度问题；模型与人类偏好的对齐可能将人类的启发式依赖传播给模型，需要开发去偏评估和对齐方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as automated evaluators (LLM-as-a-Judge). This work challenges its reliability by showing that trust judgments by LLMs are biased by disclosed source labels. Using a counterfactual design, we find that both humans and LLM judges assign higher trust to information labeled as human-authored than to the same content labeled as AI-generated. Eye-tracking data reveal that humans rely heavily on source labels as heuristic cues for judgments. We analyze LLM internal states during judgment. Across label conditions, models allocate denser attention to the label region than the content region, and this label dominance is stronger under Human labels than AI labels, consistent with the human gaze patterns. Besides, decision uncertainty measured by logits is higher under AI labels than Human labels. These results indicate that the source label is a salient heuristic cue for both humans and LLMs. It raises validity concerns for label-sensitive LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation, and we cautiously raise that aligning models with human preferences may propagate human heuristic reliance into models, motivating debiased evaluation and alignment.

</details>


### [149] [Beyond Behavior: Why AI Evaluation Needs a Cognitive Revolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05631)
*Amir Konigsberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为Turing在1950年提出的行为主义测试不仅是务实简化，更是一种认识论承诺，使关于过程、机制和内部组织的问题无法被提出。AI领域需要进行类似心理学"认知革命"的认识论转型，承认行为证据不足以支撑该领域想要做出的智力归因声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Turing的行为主义认识论在70年间悄然制约了AI研究，将关于过程、机制和内部组织的问题排除在研究视野之外。行为评估无法区分通过根本不同计算过程获得相同输出的系统，而这正是智力归因所依赖的区分。

Method: 本文采用历史-概念分析方法，将AI领域对行为评估的承诺与心理学从行为主义到认知主义的转型进行结构类比，追溯Turing行为认识论如何嵌入该领域的评估基础设施。

Result: 识别出当前AI研究无法提出的一系列问题，这些问题需要新的认识论框架才能被提出和回答。

Conclusion: AI领域需要进行类似心理学"认知革命"的认识论转型：不是放弃行为证据，而是认识到仅靠行为证据不足以支撑该领域所做的智力归因声明。

Abstract: In 1950, Alan Turing proposed replacing the question "Can machines think?" with a behavioral test: if a machine's outputs are indistinguishable from those of a thinking being, the question of whether it truly thinks can be set aside. This paper argues that Turing's move was not only a pragmatic simplification but also an epistemological commitment, a decision about what kind of evidence counts as relevant to intelligence attribution, and that this commitment has quietly constrained AI research for seven decades. We trace how Turing's behavioral epistemology became embedded in the field's evaluative infrastructure, rendering unaskable a class of questions about process, mechanism, and internal organization that cognitive psychology, neuroscience, and related disciplines learned to ask. We draw a structural parallel to the behaviorist-to-cognitivist transition in psychology: just as psychology's commitment to studying only observable behavior prevented it from asking productive questions about internal mental processes until that commitment was abandoned, AI's commitment to behavioral evaluation prevents it from distinguishing between systems that achieve identical outputs through fundamentally different computational processes, a distinction on which intelligence attribution depends. We argue that the field requires an epistemological transition comparable to the cognitive revolution: not an abandonment of behavioral evidence, but a recognition that behavioral evidence alone is insufficient for the construct claims the field wishes to make. We articulate what a post-behaviorist epistemology for AI would involve and identify the specific questions it would make askable that the field currently has no way to ask.

</details>


### [150] [PECKER: A Precisely Efficient Critical Knowledge Erasure Recipe For Machine Unlearning in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05634)
*Zhiyong Ma,Zhitao Deng,Huan Tang,Jialin Chen,Zhijun Zheng,Zhengping Li,Qingyuan Chuai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PECKER是一种高效的大模型遗忘方法，通过在蒸馏框架中引入显著性掩码来优先更新对遗忘目标数据贡献最大的参数，从而减少不必要的梯度计算，缩短训练时间，同时保持遗忘效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘方法存在训练时间长和计算成本高的问题，根源在于梯度更新方向不当，导致训练效率低且收敛不稳定。

Method: PECKER在蒸馏框架中引入显著性掩码（saliency mask），优先更新对遗忘目标数据贡献最大的参数，减少不必要的梯度计算。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和STL-10数据集上，PECKER能更快地遗忘相关类别或概念，同时更贴近真实图像分布，实现更短训练时间。

Conclusion: PECKER在保持遗忘效果的同时，显著降低了训练时间和计算开销，在类别遗忘和概念遗忘任务上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Machine unlearning (MU) has become a critical technique for GenAI models' safe and compliant operation. While existing MU methods are effective, most impose prohibitive training time and computational overhead. Our analysis suggests the root cause lies in poorly directed gradient updates, which reduce training efficiency and destabilize convergence. To mitigate these issues, we propose PECKER, an efficient MU approach that matches or outperforms prevailing methods. Within a distillation framework, PECKER introduces a saliency mask to prioritize updates to parameters that contribute most to forgetting the targeted data, thereby reducing unnecessary gradient computation and shortening overall training time without sacrificing unlearning efficacy. Our method generates samples that unlearn related class or concept more quickly, while closely aligning with the true image distribution on CIFAR-10 and STL-10 datasets, achieving shorter training times for both class forgetting and concept forgetting.

</details>


### [151] [CuraLight: Debate-Guided Data Curation for LLM-Centered Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05663)
*Qing Guo,Xinhang Li,Junyu Chen,Zheng Guo,Shengzhe Xu,Lin Zhang,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CuraLight是一个以大语言模型为核心的交通信号控制框架，通过强化学习智能体辅助微调LLM控制器，生成高质量交互轨迹用于模仿学习，并采用多LLM集成 deliberation 系统评估信号配时方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习和大语言模型的交通信号控制方法存在可解释性有限、交互数据不足以及对异构交叉口泛化能力弱等问题。

Method: CuraLight框架包含两个核心模块：(1) RL智能体探索交通环境生成高质量交互轨迹，转换为提示-响应对用于模仿微调；(2) 多LLM集成deliberation系统通过结构化辩论评估候选信号配时方案，提供偏好感知的监督信号。实验在SUMO仿真平台上进行，使用济南、杭州、亦庄的真实路网。

Result: CuraLight在异构真实路网上始终优于基线方法，平均旅行时间降低5.34%，平均排队长度降低5.14%，平均等待时间降低7.02%。

Conclusion: RL辅助探索与deliberation驱动的数据筛选相结合，可实现可扩展且可解释的交通信号控制。

Abstract: Traffic signal control (TSC) is a core component of intelligent transportation systems (ITS), aiming to reduce congestion, emissions, and travel time. Recent approaches based on reinforcement learning (RL) and large language models (LLMs) have improved adaptivity, but still suffer from limited interpretability, insufficient interaction data, and weak generalization to heterogeneous intersections.
  This paper proposes CuraLight, an LLM-centered framework where an RL agent assists the fine-tuning of an LLM-based traffic signal controller. The RL agent explores traffic environments and generates high-quality interaction trajectories, which are converted into prompt-response pairs for imitation fine-tuning. A multi-LLM ensemble deliberation system further evaluates candidate signal timing actions through structured debate, providing preference-aware supervision signals for training.
  Experiments conducted in SUMO across heterogeneous real-world networks from Jinan, Hangzhou, and Yizhuang demonstrate that CuraLight consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, reducing average travel time by 5.34 percent, average queue length by 5.14 percent, and average waiting time by 7.02 percent. The results highlight the effectiveness of combining RL-assisted exploration with deliberation-based data curation for scalable and interpretable traffic signal control.

</details>


### [152] [QA-MoE: Towards a Continuous Reliability Spectrum with Quality-Aware Mixture of Experts for Robust Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05704)
*Yitong Zhu,Yuxuan Jiang,Guanxuan Jiang,Bojing Hou,Peng Yuan Zhou,Ge Lin Kan,Yuyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态情感分析中动态噪声和模态缺失的问题，提出连续可靠性谱概念，并设计QA-MoE框架，通过自监督 aleatoric uncertainty 量化和引导专家路由，实现对连续变化可靠性条件的自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将多模态输入的不完美（如动态噪声、模态缺失）视为离散情况处理，或假设固定的 corruption ratio，无法适应现实世界中连续变化的可靠性条件。这限制了模型在实际部署中的鲁棒性和适应性。

Method: 1) 提出 Continuous Reliability Spectrum（连续可靠性谱），将缺失性和质量退化统一到单一框架中；2) 设计 QA-MoE（Quality-Aware Mixture-of-Experts）框架，通过自监督 aleatoric uncertainty 量化模态可靠性；3) 利用该机制显式引导 expert routing，抑制不可靠信号带来的误差传播，同时保留任务相关信息。

Result: QA-MoE 在多种退化场景下达到 competitive 或 state-of-the-art 性能表现。

Conclusion: QA-MoE 具有 One-Checkpoint-for-All 特性，在实践中展现出良好的实用价值，能够自适应处理连续变化的可靠性条件。

Abstract: Multimodal Sentiment Analysis (MSA) aims to infer human sentiment from textual, acoustic, and visual signals. In real-world scenarios, however, multimodal inputs are often compromised by dynamic noise or modality missingness. Existing methods typically treat these imperfections as discrete cases or assume fixed corruption ratios, which limits their adaptability to continuously varying reliability conditions. To address this, we first introduce a Continuous Reliability Spectrum to unify missingness and quality degradation into a single framework. Building on this, we propose QA-MoE, a Quality-Aware Mixture-of-Experts framework that quantifies modality reliability via self-supervised aleatoric uncertainty. This mechanism explicitly guides expert routing, enabling the model to suppress error propagation from unreliable signals while preserving task-relevant information. Extensive experiments indicate that QA-MoE achieves competitive or state-of-the-art performance across diverse degradation scenarios and exhibits a promising One-Checkpoint-for-All property in practice.

</details>


### [153] [Can Large Language Models Reinvent Foundational Algorithms?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05716)
*Jian Zhao,Haoren Luo,Yu Wang,Yuhan Cao,Pingyue Sheng,Tianxing He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过"Unlearn-and-Reinvent"管道测试LLM在遗忘特定算法后能否重新发明这些算法。结果显示Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507在无提示下成功重新发明50%的算法，验证器在避免"思维崩溃"现象中起关键作用，揭示了LLM创新思维的潜力与当前局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究旨在回答一个基础性问题：LLM是否具备进行基础性创新的能力。具体而言，研究者将问题聚焦为：LLM在遗忘某个基础算法后，能否在受控环境中重新发明它。这一问题被视为检验LLM"foundational innovation"能力的前提条件。

Method: 采用"Unlearn-and-Reinvent"管道：使用基于GRPO的on-policy遗忘方法移除模型的预训练知识中的特定基础算法（如Dijkstra算法、Euclid算法），然后在受控环境中测试模型能否重新发明该算法。实验覆盖10个目标算法、3个开源模型和3个提示级别。研究还探索了测试时强化学习（test-time RL）和生成式验证器（generative verifier）在重新发明阶段的作用。

Result: 最强模型Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507在无提示下成功重新发明50%算法，提示级别1时达70%，提示级别2时达90%。少量高层提示可提高重新发明成功率，但即使是分步提示对复杂算法仍会失败。测试时强化学习使Strassen算法在提示级别2下成功重新发明。生成式验证器在维持模型推理能力、避免"思维崩溃"现象中起关键作用。

Conclusion: LLM展现出在遗忘后重新发明基础算法的部分能力，但当前仍存在明显局限。高层提示有助于激发重新发明能力，但复杂算法仍难以突破。生成式验证器是防止推理能力退化的关键组件。这些发现为理解LLM创新思维的潜力和当前边界提供了实证依据。

Abstract: LLMs have shown strong potential to advance scientific discovery. Whether they possess the capacity for foundational innovation, however, remains an open question. In this work, we focus on a prerequisite for foundational innovation: can LLMs reinvent foundational algorithms in computer science? Our \textit{Unlearn-and-Reinvent} pipeline applies LLM unlearning to remove a specific foundational algorithm, such as Dijkstra's or Euclid's algorithm, from an LLM's pretrained knowledge, and then tests whether the model can reinvent it in a controlled environment. To enable effective unlearning, we adopt a GRPO-based, on-policy unlearning method. Across 10 target algorithms, 3 strong open-weight models, and 3 hint levels, our experiments demonstrate that (1) the strongest model Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 successfully reinvents 50% of the algorithms with no hint, 70% at hint level 1, and 90% at hint level 2; (2) a few high-level hints can enhance the reinvention success rate, but even step-by-step hints fail for those complicated algorithms; and (3) test-time reinforcement learning enables successful reinvention for the Strassen algorithm at hint level 2. Through analyses of output trajectories and ablation studies, we find that generative verifier in the reinvention phase plays a critical role in sustaining models' reasoning strength, helping to avoid the ``thought collapse'' phenomenon. These findings offer insights into both the potential and current limits of LLMs' innovative thinking.

</details>


### [154] [Contextual Control without Memory Growth in a Context-Switching Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03479)
*Song-Ju Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于干预的循环架构，通过对共享的潜在状态进行干预而非扩大循环维度来实现上下文依赖。实验结果表明该方法在上下文切换的顺序决策任务中表现优异，且能在固定潜在状态下保持正的条件上下文信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上下文依赖的顺序决策主要通过两种方式解决：显式将上下文作为输入，或扩大循环记忆维度。然而，显式上下文输入可能破坏循环核心的通用性，而内存增长会增加计算成本。本文探索第三种替代方案：通过干预共享循环潜在状态来实现上下文依赖，而不扩大循环维度。

Method: 提出干预式循环架构（Intervention-based Recurrent Architecture）：循环核心首先构建共享的干预前潜在状态，然后上下文通过加性的、上下文索引的算子作用于该状态。模型不使用直接的上下文输入到循环核心，也不增加循环维度。评估采用条件互信息 I(C;O | S) 作为理论驱动的操作探针，测量固定潜在状态下的上下文依赖性。

Result: 在主要基准测试中，干预模型在不增加循环维度的情况下表现强劲。对于任务相关的阶段1结果，干预模型展现出正的条件上下文信息，证明其能够在共享潜在状态下有效编码上下文信息。三种模型对比：标签辅助基线（直接访问上下文）、记忆基线（扩大循环状态）、干预模型（无直接上下文输入、无内存增长）。

Conclusion: 在上下文切换的顺序决策任务中，对共享循环状态进行干预为实现上下文控制提供了一种可行的替代方案，无需通过循环内存增长来编码上下文信息。这种方法在保持参数效率的同时，能够有效捕获任务相关的上下文依赖。

Abstract: Context-dependent sequential decision making is commonly addressed either by providing context explicitly as an input or by increasing recurrent memory so that contextual information can be represented internally. We study a third alternative: realizing contextual dependence by intervening on a shared recurrent latent state, without enlarging recurrent dimensionality. To this end, we introduce an intervention-based recurrent architecture in which a recurrent core first constructs a shared pre-intervention latent state, and context then acts through an additive, context-indexed operator. We evaluate this idea on a context-switching sequential decision task under partial observability. We compare three model families: a label-assisted baseline with direct context access, a memory baseline with enlarged recurrent state, and the proposed intervention model, which uses no direct context input to the recurrent core and no memory growth. On the main benchmark, the intervention model performs strongly without additional recurrent dimensions. We also evaluate the models using the conditional mutual information (I(C;O | S)) as a theorem-motivated operational probe of contextual dependence at fixed latent state. For task-relevant phase-1 outcomes, the intervention model exhibits positive conditional contextual information. Together, these results suggest that intervention on a shared recurrent state provides a viable alternative to recurrent memory growth for contextual control in this setting.

</details>


### [155] [Emergent social transmission of model-based representations without inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05777)
*Silja Keßler,Miriam Bautista-Salinero,Claudio Tennie,Charley M. Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过强化学习模拟，展示了简单的社会学习线索如何在不涉及心理化推断的情况下，间接传递更高级别的知识表征。模仿者通过启发式选择动作或提升价值表征来利用观察到的行为，最终其表征向专家收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类如何在有限认知容量下从他人那里获取丰富灵活的环境知识？传统观点认为依赖计算成本高昂的心理化（如推断他人信念），本研究旨在探索是否可以通过更简单的非心理化社会学习机制实现文化传递。

Method: 使用强化学习模拟一个在可重构环境中搜索奖励的初学者 agent，学习方式分为独立学习或观察专家两种。通过启发式选择动作或提升价值表征，而非推断心理状态。

Result: 结果表明这些社会线索会影响学习者的经验，使其表征向专家收敛。基于模型的强化学习者从社会暴露中获益最多，表现出更快的学习速度和更像专家的表征。

Conclusion: 文化传承可以通过简单的、非心理化的过程来实现，这些过程利用了非社会性学习机制。简单的社会学习线索可以间接传递更高层次的知识表征。

Abstract: How do people acquire rich, flexible knowledge about their environment from others despite limited cognitive capacity? Humans are often thought to rely on computationally costly mentalizing, such as inferring others' beliefs. In contrast, cultural evolution emphasizes that behavioral transmission can be supported by simple social cues. Using reinforcement learning simulations, we show how minimal social learning can indirectly transmit higher-level representations. We simulate a naïve agent searching for rewards in a reconfigurable environment, learning either alone or by observing an expert - crucially, without inferring mental states. Instead, the learner heuristically selects actions or boosts value representations based on observed actions. Our results demonstrate that these cues bias the learner's experience, causing its representation to converge toward the expert's. Model-based learners benefit most from social exposure, showing faster learning and more expert-like representations. These findings show how cultural transmission can arise from simple, non-mentalizing processes exploiting asocial learning mechanisms.

</details>


### [156] [Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning with Augmented Step-Level Transitions for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05808)
*Shuai Zhen,Yanhua Yu,Ruopei Guo,Nan Cheng,Yang Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出STEP-HRL框架，通过层级强化学习和本地进度模块解决大语言模型(LLM)智能体在长交互历史下的高计算成本问题，实现高效决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体依赖日益增长的交互历史，导致计算成本高、可扩展性受限。

Method: STEP-HRL采用层级强化学习框架，仅基于单步转换学习。通过已完成子任务表示全局进度，引入本地进度模块对子任务内交互历史进行迭代选择性摘要，生成紧凑的本地进度摘要，从而增强高层和低层策略的步级转换。

Result: 在ScienceWorld和ALFWorld基准测试中，STEP-HRL在性能和泛化能力上显著优于基线方法，同时降低了token使用量。

Conclusion: STEP-HRL通过层级结构和本地进度摘要机制，有效解决了长交互历史带来的计算效率问题，为LLM智能体的高效决策提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in complex interactive decision-making tasks. However, existing LLM agents typically rely on increasingly long interaction histories, resulting in high computational cost and limited scalability. In this paper, we propose STEP-HRL, a hierarchical reinforcement learning (HRL) framework that enables step-level learning by conditioning only on single-step transitions rather than full interaction histories. STEP-HRL structures tasks hierarchically, using completed subtasks to represent global progress of overall task. By introducing a local progress module, it also iteratively and selectively summarizes interaction history within each subtask to produce a compact summary of local progress. Together, these components yield augmented step-level transitions for both high-level and low-level policies. Experimental results on ScienceWorld and ALFWorld benchmarks consistently demonstrate that STEP-HRL substantially outperforms baselines in terms of performance and generalization while reducing token usage. Our code is available at https://github.com/TonyStark042/STEP-HRL.

</details>


### [157] [Vision-Guided Iterative Refinement for Frontend Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05839)
*Hannah Sansford,Derek H. C. Law,Wei Liu,Abhishek Tripathi,Niresh Agarwal,Gerrit J. J. van den Burg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全自动的批评者-循环框架，利用视觉-语言模型(VLM)作为视觉批评者，为生成的网页代码提供结构化反馈，指导迭代优化。在WebDev Arena数据集上，该方法在三个优化周期内实现了17.8%的性能提升。此外，通过LoRA参数高效微调，代码生成LLM可内化批评者提供的改进，达到最佳批评者-循环方案75%的增益效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的代码生成依赖多阶段人工反馈，成本高昂，尤其在需要渲染视觉输出的前端开发领域更为突出。现有方法缺乏对视觉输出质量的自动化评估和反馈机制。

Method: 1) 构建VLM视觉批评者框架，对渲染后的网页进行结构化视觉反馈；2) 采用迭代式代码优化循环；3) 使用LoRA进行参数高效微调，使代码生成模型内化批评者提供的改进策略。

Result: 1) 视觉批评者框架在三个优化周期内实现最高17.8%的性能提升；2) LoRA微调可达到最佳批评者-循环方案约25%的增益效果，且未显著增加token消耗；3) 自动化VLM批评显著优于单次LLM推理。

Conclusion: 自动化VLM批评驱动的代码优化框架能显著提升前端代码生成质量；迭代优化对网页开发等复杂视觉输出任务至关重要；通过LoRA微调可部分内化批评策略，为降低推理成本提供可行路径。

Abstract: Code generation with large language models often relies on multi-stage human-in-the-loop refinement, which is effective but very costly - particularly in domains such as frontend web development where the solution quality depends on rendered visual output. We present a fully automated critic-in-the-loop framework in which a vision-language model serves as a visual critic that provides structured feedback on rendered webpages to guide iterative refinement of generated code. Across real-world user requests from the WebDev Arena dataset, this approach yields consistent improvements in solution quality, achieving up to 17.8% increase in performance over three refinement cycles. Next, we investigate parameter-efficient fine-tuning using LoRA to understand whether the improvements provided by the critic can be internalized by the code-generating LLM. Fine-tuning achieves 25% of the gains from the best critic-in-the-loop solution without a significant increase in token counts. Our findings indicate that automated, VLM-based critique of frontend code generation leads to significantly higher quality solutions than can be achieved through a single LLM inference pass, and highlight the importance of iterative refinement for the complex visual outputs associated with web development.

</details>


### [158] [Deep Researcher Agent: An Autonomous Framework for 24/7 Deep Learning Experimentation with Zero-Cost Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05854)
*Xiangyue Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Deep Researcher Agent是一个开源框架，使大型语言模型（LLM）智能体能够全天候自主开展深度学习实验，涵盖假设形成、代码实现、训练执行、结果分析和迭代优化的完整实验生命周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI研究助手主要聚焦于论文撰写或代码生成，缺乏能够覆盖完整实验生命周期的系统。研究人员需要能够自主进行假设形成、代码实现、训练执行、结果分析和迭代优化的智能系统。

Method: 该框架提出三项关键创新：（1）零成本监控——通过进程级检查和日志文件读取，在模型训练期间不产生LLM API成本；（2）双层固定大小记忆——无论运行时间多长，记忆上限保持在约5K字符，防止长时间运行智能体的上下文无限增长；（3）最小工具集领导-工作架构——多智能体设计，每个工作智能体仅配备3-5个工具，减少每次调用token开销高达73%。

Result: 在30多天的持续部署中，该框架在四个并行研究项目中自主完成了500多个实验周期，在一个项目中通过200多次自动化实验实现了比基线指标提升52%的效果，平均每24小时周期LLM成本仅为0.08美元。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了低成本、高效率的持续自主深度学习研究，为AI辅助科学研究提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present \textbf{Deep Researcher Agent}, an open-source framework that enables large language model (LLM) agents to autonomously conduct deep learning experiments around the clock. Unlike existing AI research assistants that focus on paper writing or code generation, our system addresses the full experiment lifecycle: hypothesis formation, code implementation, training execution, result analysis, and iterative refinement. The framework introduces three key innovations: (1) \textbf{Zero-Cost Monitoring} -- a monitoring paradigm that incurs zero LLM API costs during model training by relying solely on process-level checks and log file reads; (2) \textbf{Two-Tier Constant-Size Memory} -- a memory architecture capped at $\sim$5K characters regardless of runtime duration, preventing the unbounded context growth that plagues long-running agents; and (3) \textbf{Minimal-Toolset Leader-Worker Architecture} -- a multi-agent design where each worker agent is equipped with only 3--5 tools, reducing per-call token overhead by up to 73\%. In sustained deployments spanning 30+ days, the framework autonomously completed 500+ experiment cycles across four concurrent research projects, achieving a 52\% improvement over baseline metrics in one project through 200+ automated experiments -- all at an average LLM cost of \$0.08 per 24-hour cycle. Code is available at https://github.com/Xiangyue-Zhang/auto-deep-researcher-24x7.

</details>


### [159] [When Do We Need LLMs? A Diagnostic for Language-Driven Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05859)
*Uljad Berdica,Fernando Acero,Anton Ipsen,Parisa Zehtabi,Michael Cashmore,Manuela Veloso*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究上下文多臂老虎机(CMABs)在金融等领域的顺序决策问题，对比了LLM推理与轻量级数值老虎机在文本嵌入上的性能，提出LLMP-UCB算法，并证明嵌入维度可作为探索-利用权衡的实际杠杆，同时设计了几何诊断方法指导LLM与轻量级方案的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在上下文多臂老虎机问题中应用时计算成本高、不确定性估计困难，亟需在性能与成本间寻找平衡。

Method: 1) 提出LLMP-UCB算法，通过重复推理从LLM获取不确定性估计；2) 对比轻量级数值老虎机（基于密集或Matryoshka嵌入）；3) 探索嵌入维度对探索-利用平衡的影响；4) 设计基于嵌入的几何诊断方法决定策略选择。

Result: 轻量级数值老虎机在文本嵌入上的准确率与LLM方案相当甚至更优，成本仅为后者的很小一部分；嵌入维度可作为探索-利用权衡的实际杠杆，实现无需提示复杂度的成本-性能权衡。

Conclusion: 本文为金融AI应用中的成本效益型、不确定性感知决策系统提供了原则性部署框架，可广泛应用于金融服务的各类AI用例。

Abstract: We study Contextual Multi-Armed Bandits (CMABs) for non-episodic sequential decision making problems where the context includes both textual and numerical information (e.g., recommendation systems, dynamic portfolio adjustments, offer selection; all frequent problems in finance). While Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to these settings, utilizing LLMs for reasoning at every decision step is computationally expensive and uncertainty estimates are difficult to obtain. To address this, we introduce LLMP-UCB, a bandit algorithm that derives uncertainty estimates from LLMs via repeated inference. However, our experiments demonstrate that lightweight numerical bandits operating on text embeddings (dense or Matryoshka) match or exceed the accuracy of LLM-based solutions at a fraction of their cost. We further show that embedding dimensionality is a practical lever on the exploration-exploitation balance, enabling cost--performance tradeoffs without prompt complexity. Finally, to guide practitioners, we propose a geometric diagnostic based on the arms' embedding to decide when to use LLM-driven reasoning versus a lightweight numerical bandit. Our results provide a principled deployment framework for cost-effective, uncertainty-aware decision systems with broad applicability across AI use cases in financial services.

</details>


### [160] [Joint Knowledge Base Completion and Question Answering by Combining Large Language Models and Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05875)
*Yinan Liu,Dongying Lin,Sigang Luo,Xiaochun Yang,Bin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Knowledge Bases (KBs) play a key role in various applications. As two representative KB-related tasks, knowledge base completion (KBC) and knowledge base question answering (KBQA) are closely related and inherently complementary with each other. Thus, it will be beneficial to solve the task of joint KBC and KBQA to make them reinforce each other. However, existing studies usually rely on the small language model (SLM) to enhance them jointly, and the large language model (LLM)'s strong reasoning ability is ignored. In this paper, by combining the strengths of the LLM with the SLM, we propose a novel framework JCQL, which can make these two tasks enhance each other in an iterative manner. To make KBC enhance KBQA, we augment the LLM agent-based KBQA model's reasoning paths by incorporating an SLM-trained KBC model as an action of the agent, alleviating the LLM's hallucination and high computational costs issue in KBQA. To make KBQA enhance KBC, we incrementally fine-tune the KBC model by leveraging KBQA's reasoning paths as its supplementary training data, improving the ability of the SLM in KBC. Extensive experiments over two public benchmark data sets demonstrate that JCQL surpasses all baselines for both KBC and KBQA tasks.

</details>


### [161] [HybridKV: Hybrid KV Cache Compression for Efficient Multimodal Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05887)
*Bowen Zeng,Feiyang Ren,Jun Zhang,Xiaoling Gu,Ke Chen,Lidan Shou,Huan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HybridKV通过将注意力头分类为静态/动态类型，采用混合压缩策略（文本优先剪枝+分块检索），在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上实现最高7.9倍KV缓存内存压缩和1.52倍解码加速，性能几乎无下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中，视觉输入产生数千个token导致KV缓存线性增长，驻留GPU内存造成严重的内存开销和延迟问题。现有方法仅关注分配策略，忽视了不同注意力头的异构行为需要差异化的压缩策略。

Method: 提出三阶段混合压缩框架：1）基于文本中心注意力将注意力头分类为静态/动态类型；2）自上而下分层分配KV预算；3）静态头采用文本优先剪枝压缩，动态头采用分块检索压缩。

Result: 在11个多模态基准测试上，HybridKV实现最高7.9倍KV缓存内存压缩和1.52倍解码加速，性能几乎无下降甚至优于全缓存MLLM。

Conclusion: 通过识别注意力头的异构行为并采用差异化压缩策略，HybridKV在显著降低内存开销和推理延迟的同时保持了模型性能，为MLLM的高效推理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have advanced unified reasoning over text, images, and videos, but their inference is hindered by the rapid growth of key-value (KV) caches. Each visual input expands into thousands of tokens, causing caches to scale linearly with context length and remain resident in GPU memory throughout decoding, which leads to prohibitive memory overhead and latency even on high-end GPUs. A common solution is to compress caches under a fixed allocated budget at different granularities: token-level uniformly discards less important tokens, layer-level varies retention across layers, and head-level redistributes budgets across heads. Yet these approaches stop at allocation and overlook the heterogeneous behaviors of attention heads that require distinct compression strategies. We propose HybridKV, a hybrid KV cache compression framework that integrates complementary strategies in three stages: heads are first classified into static or dynamic types using text-centric attention; then a top-down budget allocation scheme hierarchically assigns KV budgets; finally, static heads are compressed by text-prior pruning and dynamic heads by chunk-wise retrieval. Experiments on 11 multimodal benchmarks with Qwen2.5-VL-7B show that HybridKV reduces KV cache memory by up to $7.9\times$ and achieves $1.52\times$ faster decoding, with almost no performance drop or even higher relative to the full-cache MLLM.

</details>


### [162] [Towards Trustworthy Report Generation: A Deep Research Agent with Progressive Confidence Estimation and Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05952)
*Yi Yuan,Xuhong Wang,Shanzhe Lei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合渐进式置信度估计与校准机制的深度研究智能体，通过 deliberation search model 实现可信赖的研究报告生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架多基于主观维度，难以捕获报告质量的关键维度——可信赖性。在缺乏 ground-truth 的开放研究场景中，当前方法无法有效衡量生成内容的认知置信度，导致校准困难，用户易受到误导或幻觉信息的侵害。

Method: 提出一种新型深度研究智能体，集成渐进式置信度估计与报告生成管道。具体包括：(1) Delibration Search Model，结合深度检索与多跳推理，将输出锚定在可验证证据上；(2) 为单个声明分配置信度分数；(3) 精心设计的工作流程，提升透明度和可解释性。

Result: 实验结果和案例研究表明，该方法显著提升了可解释性，并大幅增加了用户信任度。

Conclusion: 通过将置信度估计嵌入报告生成流程，该方法能够产生更具可信赖性和透明度的研究报告，有效缓解幻觉信息问题。

Abstract: As agent-based systems continue to evolve, deep research agents are capable of automatically generating research-style reports across diverse domains. While these agents promise to streamline information synthesis and knowledge exploration, existing evaluation frameworks-typically based on subjective dimensions-fail to capture a critical aspect of report quality: trustworthiness. In open-ended research scenarios where ground-truth answers are unavailable, current evaluation methods cannot effectively measure the epistemic confidence of generated content, making calibration difficult and leaving users susceptible to misleading or hallucinated information. To address this limitation, we propose a novel deep research agent that incorporates progressive confidence estimation and calibration within the report generation pipeline. Our system leverages a deliberative search model, featuring deep retrieval and multi-hop reasoning to ground outputs in verifiable evidence while assigning confidence scores to individual claims. Combined with a carefully designed workflow, this approach produces trustworthy reports with enhanced transparency. Experimental results and case studies demonstrate that our method substantially improves interpretability and significantly increases user trust.

</details>


### [163] [Beyond Compromise: Pareto-Lenient Consensus for Efficient Multi-Preference LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05965)
*Renxuan Tan,Rongpeng Li,Zhifeng Zhao,Honggang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Transcending the single-preference paradigm, aligning LLMs with diverse human values is pivotal for robust deployment. Contemporary Multi-Objective Preference Alignment (MPA) approaches predominantly rely on static linear scalarization or rigid gradient projection to navigate these trade-offs. However, by enforcing strict conflict avoidance or simultaneous descent, these paradigms often prematurely converge to local stationary points. While mathematically stable, these points represent a conservative compromise where the model sacrifices potential global Pareto improvements to avoid transient local trade-offs. To break this deadlock, we propose Pareto-Lenient Consensus (PLC), a game-theoretic framework that reimagines alignment as a dynamic negotiation process. Unlike rigid approaches, PLC introduces consensus-driven lenient gradient rectification, which dynamically tolerates local degradation provided there is a sufficient dominant coalition surplus, thereby empowering the optimization trajectory to escape local suboptimal equilibrium and explore the distal Pareto-optimal frontier. Theoretical analysis validates PLC can facilitate stalemate escape and asymptotically converge to a Pareto consensus equilibrium. Moreover, extensive experiments show that PLC surpasses baselines in both fixed-preference alignment and global Pareto frontier quality. This work highlights the potential of negotiation-driven alignment as a promising avenue for MPA. Our codes are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/aaa-6BB8.

</details>


### [164] [Epistemic Blinding: An Inference-Time Protocol for Auditing Prior Contamination in LLM-Assisted Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06013)
*Michael Cuccarese*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"认知遮蔽"(epistemic blinding)方法,解决大型语言模型在药物靶点优先级排序等agentic系统中悄然混合数据驱动推理与训练记忆先验的问题。该方法通过在推理时将实体标识符替换为匿名代码来恢复可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM输出的数据驱动推理与训练记忆先验之间的混合是不可见的,无法从单一输出判断有多少来自页面数据、多少来自模型参数记忆。这严重影响了agentic系统的可审计性,尤其在生物学等需要严格数据溯源的领域。

Method: 认知遮蔽是一种推理时协议:将实体标识符替换为匿名代码后再进行提示,然后与未遮蔽的对照组输出进行比较。完整系统包括LLM引导的评分函数进化优化和用于靶点合理化的遮蔽agentic推理两部分。

Result: 在四种癌症类型的肿瘤药物靶点优先级排序中,遮蔽改变了16%的top-20预测,但保持了对验证靶点相同的回收率。在S&P 500股票筛选中,品牌识别偏差在5个随机种子下重塑了30-40%的top-20排名。

Conclusion: 认知遮蔽不声称产生更好的结果,而是恢复了关键的可审计维度——测量输出中有多少来自提供的数据而非模型参数知识。协议已开源发布,并提供Claude Code技能以支持在agentic工作流中一键执行认知遮蔽。

Abstract: This paper presents epistemic blinding in the context of an agentic system that uses large language models to reason across multiple biological datasets for drug target prioritization. During development, it became apparent that LLM outputs silently blend data-driven inference with memorized priors about named entities - and the blend is invisible: there is no way to determine, from a single output, how much came from the data on the page and how much came from the model's training memory. Epistemic blinding is a simple inference-time protocol that replaces entity identifiers with anonymous codes before prompting, then compares outputs against an unblinded control. The protocol does not make LLM reasoning deterministic, but it restores one critical axis of auditability: measuring how much of an output came from the supplied data versus the model's parametric knowledge. The complete target identification system is described - including LLM-guided evolutionary optimization of scoring functions and blinded agentic reasoning for target rationalization - with demonstration that both stages operate without access to entity identity. In oncology drug target prioritization across four cancer types, blinding changes 16% of top-20 predictions while preserving identical recovery of validated targets. The contamination problem is shown to generalize beyond biology: in S&P 500 equity screening, brand-recognition bias reshapes 30-40% of top-20 rankings across five random seeds. To lower the barrier to adoption, the protocol is released as an open-source tool and as a Claude Code skill that enables one-command epistemic blinding within agentic workflows. The claim is not that blinded analysis produces better results, but that without blinding, there is no way to know to what degree the agent is adhering to the analytical process the researcher designed.

</details>


### [165] [How LLMs Follow Instructions: Skillful Coordination, Not a Universal Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06015)
*Elisabetta Rocchetti,Alfio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过诊断探测方法研究了指令微调语言模型遵循指令的机制，发现指令遵循并非依赖单一的通用机制，而是由多种语言能力的协调组合实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究普遍认为指令微调赋予了语言模型通用的指令遵循能力，但这一能力背后的具体机制尚不清楚——究竟是单一通用机制还是组合式技能部署？

Method: 在三个指令微调模型上，针对九个不同任务进行诊断探测分析。具体包括：训练跨任务通用探测器与任务特定探测器进行对比、跨任务迁移分析、因果消融实验，以及层次和时间维度的分析。

Result: 多方面证据一致表明指令遵循并非依赖通用机制：(1)通用探测器表现始终不如任务专用探测器；(2)跨任务迁移能力弱且按技能相似性聚类；(3)因果消融揭示稀疏非对称依赖而非共享表征；(4)任务按复杂度在模型不同层次上分化，结构约束出现早，语义任务出现晚；(5)时序分析表明约束满足在生成过程中动态监控而非预生成规划。

Conclusion: 指令遵循应被理解为多种语言能力的 skillful coordination（巧妙的协调），而非单一抽象约束检查过程的部署。这一发现对理解大语言模型的能力组织和训练策略具有重要意义。

Abstract: Instruction tuning is commonly assumed to endow language models with a domain-general ability to follow instructions, yet the underlying mechanism remains poorly understood. Does instruction-following rely on a universal mechanism or compositional skill deployment? We investigate this through diagnostic probing across nine diverse tasks in three instruction-tuned models.
  Our analysis provides converging evidence against a universal mechanism. First, general probes trained across all tasks consistently underperform task-specific specialists, indicating limited representational sharing. Second, cross-task transfer is weak and clustered by skill similarity. Third, causal ablation reveals sparse asymmetric dependencies rather than shared representations. Tasks also stratify by complexity across layers, with structural constraints emerging early and semantic tasks emerging late. Finally, temporal analysis shows constraint satisfaction operates as dynamic monitoring during generation rather than pre-generation planning.
  These findings indicate that instruction-following is better characterized as skillful coordination of diverse linguistic capabilities rather than deployment of a single abstract constraint-checking process.

</details>


### [166] [Claw-Eval: Toward Trustworthy Evaluation of Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06132)
*Bowen Ye,Rang Li,Qibin Yang,Yuanxin Liu,Linli Yao,Hanglong Lv,Zhihui Xie,Chenxin An,Lei Li,Lingpeng Kong,Qi Liu,Zhifang Sui,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Claw-Eval是一个端到端评估套件，旨在弥补现有智能体基准测试的三个关键缺陷：轨迹不透明评分、安全与鲁棒性评估不足、以及模态覆盖范围窄。该套件包含300个经人工验证的任务，涵盖9个类别，通过三种独立证据通道（执行追踪、审计日志、环境快照）实现轨迹感知评分。实验表明：轨迹不透明评估会遗漏44%的安全违规和13%的鲁棒性失败；受控错误注入主要损害一致性而非峰值能力；多模态性能差异显著，大多数模型在视频任务上表现弱于文档/图像任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试存在三个关键局限：(1) 轨迹不透明评分，仅检查最终输出；(2) 安全性和鲁棒性评估不足；(3) 模态覆盖和交互范式狭窄。这些局限性导致无法全面评估LLM智能体在真实软件环境中的表现。

Method: Claw-Eval包含300个人工验证任务，分布在9个类别、3个分组（通用服务编排、多模态感知与生成、多轮专业对话）。通过三种独立证据通道（执行追踪、审计日志、环境快照）记录每个智能体动作，实现基于2,159个细粒度评分项的轨迹感知评分。评分协议评估完成度、安全性和鲁棒性，报告Average Score、Pass@k和Pass^k三个指标。

Result: 对14个前沿模型的实验表明：(1) 轨迹不透明评估系统性不可靠，遗漏44%的安全违规和13%的鲁棒性失败；(2) 受控错误注入主要降低一致性（Pass^3下降24%）而峰值能力稳定（Pass@3不变）；(3) 多模态性能差异大，大多数模型视频表现弱于文档/图像；(4) 没有单一模型在所有模态上占优。

Conclusion: Claw-Eval揭示了构建可靠可部署智能体所需的关键要素，强调不仅需要能力强大，更需要可靠可部署。该评估套件为智能体开发提供了可操作的方向指引。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents executing multi-step workflows in real-world software environments. However, existing agent benchmarks suffer from three critical limitations: (1) trajectory-opaque grading that checks only final outputs, (2) underspecified safety and robustness evaluation, and (3) narrow modality coverage and interaction paradigms. We introduce Claw-Eval, an end-to-end evaluation suite addressing all three gaps. It comprises 300 human-verified tasks spanning 9 categories across three groups (general service orchestration, multimodal perception and generation, and multi-turn professional dialogue). Every agent action is recorded through three independent evidence channels (execution traces, audit logs, and environment snapshots), enabling trajectory-aware grading over 2,159 fine-grained rubric items. The scoring protocol evaluates Completion, Safety, and Robustness, reporting Average Score, Pass@k, and Pass^k across three trials to distinguish genuine capability from lucky outcomes. Experiments on 14 frontier models reveal that: (1) trajectory-opaque evaluation is systematically unreliable, missing 44% of safety violations and 13% of robustness failures that our hybrid pipeline catches; (2) controlled error injection primarily degrades consistency rather than peak capability, with Pass^3 dropping up to 24% while Pass@3 remains stable; (3) multimodal performance varies sharply, with most models performing poorer on video than on document or image, and no single model dominating across all modalities. Beyond benchmarking, Claw-Eval highlights actionable directions for agent development, shedding light on what it takes to build agents that are not only capable but reliably deployable.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Analyzing Persistent Alltoallv RMA Implementations for High-Performance MPI Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05099)
*Evelyn Namugwanya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对HPC应用中的MPI_Alltoallv集体通信操作，设计了基于fence和lock同步的持久化MPI RMA变体，通过将一次性初始化阶段与每次迭代执行分离，实现通信元数据和窗口状态的复用。实验表明fence持久化变体在大消息场景下性能提升显著，最高可达44%运行时降低，448进程下从2.49s降至1.54s（快38%）。盈亏平衡点为32,768字节，适用于大消息工作负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPI_Alltoallv等集体通信操作是许多HPC应用的核心，尤其对于不规则消息大小的应用。现有的非持久化实现需要在每次迭代中重复处理通信元数据，导致性能开销。通过将初始化阶段与执行阶段分离，实现元数据和窗口状态的复用，有望显著提升重复通信场景的性能。

Method: 设计并实现了两种持久化MPI RMA Alltoallv变体：基于fence同步和基于lock同步的方案。关键创新在于将通信元数据创建和窗口状态配置分离到一次性初始化阶段，使后续迭代可直接复用这些数据结构。实验在LLNL Dane超级计算机上进行，包含消息大小扫描、不规则稀疏通信模式测试，以及fence与lock方案的分层扩展比较。

Result: Fence持久化变体在大消息场景下始终优于非持久化基线，最高实现44%运行时降低。在448进程规模下，运行时间从2.49s降至1.54s（提速38%），且可扩展性随进程数增加而改善。盈亏平衡分析表明，当消息大小≥32,768字节时持久化立即产生收益；小消息因元数据摊销成本限制，收益有限。

Conclusion: 持久化RMA Alltoallv对于大消息工作负载是实用方案，消除了重复元数据处理开销后，运行时间主要由数据移动主导（表现为消息越大节省越多）。研究明确了现代HPC系统上fence同步与lock同步的权衡取舍，为高性能计算应用的通信优化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Collective communication operations such as MPI_Alltoallv are central to many HPC applications, particularly those with irregular message sizes. We design, implement, and evaluate persistent MPI RMA variants of Alltoallv based on fence and lock synchronization, separating a one time initialization phase from per iteration execution to enable reuse of communication metadata and window state across repeated epochs.
  Our benchmarks tested on LLNL's Dane supercomputer show that the fence-persistent variant consistently outperforms the non-persistent baseline for large message sizes, achieving up to 44% reduction in runtime and improving scalability with increasing process counts; at 448 processes the runtime decreases from 2.49s to 1.54s (38% faster). We further evaluate the algorithms under irregular sparse communication patterns and compare fence- and lock-based designs, including hierarchical extensions.
  Message-size sweeps and a break-even model demonstrate that persistence provides immediate payoff for messages greater or equal to 32,768 bytes, while smaller messages show limited benefit due to metadata amortization costs. These results indicate that persistent RMA Alltoallv is a practical approach for workloads with large messages, where removing repeated metadata processing leaves runtime dominated by data movement, as evidenced by the increasing time savings with message size, and they clarify the trade-offs between fence and lock synchronization on modern HPC systems.

</details>


### [168] [Optimizing OpenFaaS on Kubernetes: Comparative Analysis of Language Runtimes and Cluster Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05496)
*Ehsan Ataie,Mohammadreza Pooshani,Hossein Aqasizade*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在CloudLab平台上评估了OpenFaaS在四种Kubernetes发行版（Kubeadm、K3s、MicroK8s、K0s）上的性能表现，并比较了Python、Go和Node.js三种编程语言开发函数时的性能差异。研究表明Go在吞吐量和CPU使用率方面优于其他语言；K3s和Kubeadm整体性能最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Serverless FaaS平台虽已广泛部署，但其在不同Kubernetes发行版上的兼容性与性能表现缺乏系统性对比研究，且编程语言对平台性能的影响尚未被充分探讨。

Method: 实验基于CloudLab基础设施构建，使用Kubeadm、K3s、MicroK8s和K0s四种Kubernetes发行版部署OpenFaaS，采用Python、Go和Node.js开发函数，在不同并发调用等级下测量吞吐量、CPU使用率和延迟等指标。

Result: Go语言在吞吐量和CPU使用率上始终优于Python和Node.js；K3s和Kubeadm表现最优，其中Kubeadm延迟低且CPU效率高，K3s吞吐量更高。

Conclusion: Go是Serverless应用的最佳运行时选择；K3s和Kubeadm是部署Kubernetes环境下OpenFaaS的推荐方案。研究为优化OpenFaaS平台部署提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Serverless computing, particularly Function-as-a-Service (FaaS), has revolutionized cloud computing by abstracting infrastructure management and enabling dynamic resource allocation. This paper examines the performance and compatibility of OpenFaaS, an open-source serverless platform, when deployed on various Kubernetes distributions, including Kubeadm, K3s, MicroK8s, and K0s. Moreover, leveraging the CloudLab infrastructure, this study examines the impact of Python, Go, and Node.js programming languages on the performance of Kubernetes-enabled OpenFaaS, specifically when these languages are used to develop functions deployed on the platform. The performance is evaluated and analyzed under various levels of concurrent invocations using several usage-level metrics, such as throughput and CPU usage, as well as responsiveness metrics, such as delay. According to our findings, Go consistently outperforms Python and Node.js in terms of throughput and CPU usage, making it the ideal runtime for serverless applications. Among the Kubernetes distributions, K3s and Kubeadm exhibit superior performance, with Kubeadm maintaining low latency and efficient CPU usage, and K3s demonstrating high throughput. This study provides valuable insights into optimizing the Kubernetes-enabled OpenFaaS platform, highlighting the strengths and trade-offs of different Kubernetes distributions and language runtimes.

</details>


### [169] [Communication Requirements for Linearizable Registers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05862)
*Raïssa Nataf,Yoram Moses*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在异步消息传递系统中，线性化原子寄存器的实现必须产生大量的消息链。研究表明，由于进程无法确定操作的精确时序和实时顺序，消息链成为确保线性化的必要手段，且所有类型的操作都需要消息链连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性化是分布式系统的基本正确性条件，但确保实现的线性化非常复杂。在异步系统中，进程无法确定操作的精确时序和实时顺序，只能依赖消息发送与接收的顺序关系。本文旨在研究异步消息传递系统中线性化原子寄存器实现的通信需求。

Method: 首先证明两个通用定理，将消息链与异步系统执行中操作延迟和重排序的能力联系起来；然后应用这些定理分析线性化寄存器实现的通信模式。

Result: 证明线性化寄存器实现必须在所有类型的操作之间创建大量的消息链连接。

Conclusion: 结果表明异步系统中的线性化实现必然是昂贵的且非平凡的，并揭示了线性化实现的基本结构特征。

Abstract: While linearizability is a fundamental correctness condition for distributed systems, ensuring the linearizability of implementations can be quite complex. An essential aspect of linearizable implementations of concurrent objects is the need to preserve the real-time order of operations. In many settings, however, processes cannot determine the precise timing and relative real-time ordering of operations. Indeed, in an asynchronous system, the only ordering information available to them is based on the fact that sending a message precedes its delivery. We show that as a result, message chains must be used extensively to ensure linearizability. This paper studies the communication requirements of linearizable implementations of atomic registers in asynchronous message passing systems. We start by proving two general theorems that relate message chains to the ability to delay and reorder actions and operations in an execution of an asynchronous system, without the changes being noticeable to the processes. These are then used to prove that linearizable register implementations must create extensive message chains among operations of \emph{all} types. In particular, our results imply that linearizable implementations in asynchronous systems are necessarily costly and nontrivial, and provide insight into their structure.

</details>


### [170] [JZ-Tree: GPU friendly neighbour search and friends-of-friends with dual tree walks in JAX plus CUDA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05885)
*Jens Stücker,Oliver Hahn,Lukas Winkler,Adrian Gutierrez Adame,Thomas Flöss*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对GPU架构优化的Morton（z-order）平面树层次结构，解决空间树算法在GPU上因线程分化和不规则内存访问导致的性能瓶颈问题。基于该框架实现了k近邻搜索和Friends-of-Friends聚类算法，在大规模数据（N≥10^7）上实现超过10倍的性能提升，并支持多GPU分布式扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间树遍历算法在CPU高性能计算中高效灵活，但直接迁移到GPU架构时，由于树算法的分支特性和不规则内存访问模式导致严重的线程分化和低效内存访问，无法充分发挥GPU高计算吞吐量的优势。

Method: 提出Morton（z-order）"平面树层次结构"：采用扁平化数据布局实现高效双向树遍历，支持线程组协作执行，获得高度合并的内存访问模式。基于该框架实现两个重要空间算法：精确k近邻搜索和FoF聚类。

Result: 大规模问题规模（N≥10^7）下，性能提升超过一个数量级，支持分布式多GPU系统的强扩展性。

Conclusion: 开源实现'JZ-Tree'（JAX z-order tree），为广泛的树基算法提供高效GPU实现基础。

Abstract: Algorithms based on spatial tree traversal are widely regarded as among the most efficient and flexible approaches for many problems in CPU-based high-performance computing (HPC). However, directly transferring these algorithms to GPU architectures often yields substantially smaller performance gains than expected in light of the high computational throughput of modern GPUs. The branching nature of tree algorithms leads to thread divergence and irregular memory access patterns -- both of which may severely limit GPU performance. To address these challenges, we propose a Morton (z-order) 'plane-based tree hierarchy' that is specifically designed for GPU architectures. The resulting flattened data layout enables efficient dual-tree traversal with collaborative execution across thread groups, leading to highly coalesced memory access patterns. Based on this framework we present implementations of two important spatial algorithms -- exact $k$-nearest neighbour search and friends-of-friends (FoF) clustering. For both cases, we observe more than an order-of-magnitude performance improvement over the closest competing GPU libraries for large problem sizes ($N \gtrsim 10^7$), together with strong scaling to distributed multi-GPU systems. We provide an open-source implementation, 'JZ-Tree' (JAX z-order tree), which serves as a foundation for efficient GPU implementations of a broad class of tree-based algorithms.

</details>


### [171] [GTaP: A GPU-Resident Fork-Join Task-Parallel Runtime with a Pragma-Based Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05982)
*Yuki Maeda,Kenjiro Taura*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GTaP是一个GPU-resident运行时，支持fork-join任务并行。它基于persistent kernel模型，提供thread block和thread两级worker粒度，使用continuation表示join，state machine执行任务，扩展Clang前端支持pragma编程模型，采用work stealing负载均衡和EPAQ减少warp divergence。在不规则应用上优于72核CPU上的OpenMP和naive GPU方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU擅长规则的数据并行负载，但不规则应用通常表现为fork-join结构的任务并行。现有CPU上的fork-join运行时已成熟，但在GPU上高效支持仍具挑战性。

Method: 基于persistent kernel模型，支持thread block和thread两级worker粒度；用continuation表示join，将每个task实现为可分割的state machine；扩展Clang前端添加pragma编程模型；采用work stealing负载均衡；对thread-level worker引入EPAQ按用户定义标准分区任务队列以减少warp divergence。

Result: 在代表性不规则应用上，GTaP在计算密集型任务和大数据量场景下优于72核CPU上的OpenMP。EPAQ效果因工作负载而异，在Fibonacci上可达1.8倍加速。

Conclusion: GTaP成功将fork-join任务并行引入GPU，其设计选择优于naive GPU替代方案，为GPU上的不规则应用提供了高效的运行时支持。

Abstract: Graphics Processing Units (GPUs) excel at regular data-parallel workloads where massive hardware parallelism can be readily exploited. In contrast, many important irregular applications are naturally expressed as task parallelism with a fork-join control structure. While CPU runtimes for fork-join task parallelism are mature, it remains challenging to efficiently support it on GPUs.
  We propose GTaP, a GPU-resident runtime that supports fork-join task parallelism. GTaP is based on the persistent kernel model, and supports two worker granularities: thread blocks and individual threads. To realize fork-join on GPUs, GTaP represents joins as continuations and executes each task as a state machine that can be split into multiple execution segments. We also extend Clang's frontend with a pragma-based programming model that enables programmers to express fork-join without exposing low-level mechanisms. GTaP employs work stealing for load balancing, providing better scalability than a global-queue approach. For thread-level workers, we further introduce Execution-Path-Aware Queueing (EPAQ), which allows programmers to partition task queues using user-defined criteria, reducing warp divergence caused by mixing heterogeneous control flows within a warp.
  Across representative irregular applications, GTaP outperforms OpenMP task-parallel execution on a 72-core CPU in many cases, especially for large problem sizes with compute-intensive tasks. We also show that GTaP's design choices outperform naive GPU alternatives. The benefit of EPAQ is workload-dependent: it can improve performance for some benchmarks while having little effect on others; on Fibonacci, EPAQ achieves up to a 1.8$\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [172] [CoStream: Codec-Guided Resource-Efficient System for Video Streaming Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06036)
*Yulin Zou,Yan Chen,Wenyan Chen,JooYoung Park,Shivaraman Nitin,Luo Tao,Francisco Romero,Dmitrii Ustiugov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CoStream是一个基于编解码器引导的流式视频分析系统，利用视频编解码器提取的元数据作为低开销运行时信号，统一优化视频解码、视觉处理和LLM预填充，相比现有方法实现3倍吞吐量提升和87%的GPU计算减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频流分析是视觉-语言模型服务的重要工作负载，但多模态推理的高成本限制了可扩展性。现有系统通过利用视频流中的时间和空间冗余来降低推理成本，但它们要么针对ViT，要么针对LLM，视角有限，未能充分利用端到端优化机会。此外，现有方法在识别冗余时会产生显著开销，无论是通过离线分析和训练还是昂贵的在线计算，都不适合动态实时流场景。

Method: CoStream基于一个关键观察：视频编解码器在压缩过程中已经提取了每个流的时间和空间结构作为副产品。CoStream将这种编解码器元数据作为低开销运行时信号，用于：1) ViT编码前的编解码器引导补丁剪枝；2) LLM预填充期间的选择性键值缓存刷新。这两种优化都是完全在线的，不需要离线训练。

Result: 实验表明，CoStream相比最先进的基线方法实现了高达3倍的吞吐量提升和高达87%的GPU计算减少，同时保持具有竞争力的准确性，F1分数仅下降0-8%。

Conclusion: CoStream通过直接操作压缩比特流，将传输减少作为内在优势，展示了编解码器引导的优化在视频分析系统中的潜力，为视觉-语言模型的高效流式服务提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Video streaming analytics is a crucial workload for vision-language model serving, but the high cost of multimodal inference limits scalability. Prior systems reduce inference cost by exploiting temporal and spatial redundancy in video streams, but they target either the vision transformer (ViT) or the LLM with a limited view, leaving end-to-end opportunities untapped. Moreover, existing methods incur significant overhead to identify redundancy, either through offline profiling and training or costly online computation, making them ill-suited for dynamic real-time streams.
  We present CoStream, a codec-guided streaming video analytics system built on a key observation that video codecs already extract the temporal and spatial structure of each stream as a byproduct of compression. CoStream treats this codec metadata as a low-cost runtime signal to unify optimization across video decoding, visual processing, and LLM prefilling, with transmission reduction as an inherent benefit of operating directly on compressed bitstreams. This drives codec-guided patch pruning before ViT encoding and selective key-value cache refresh during LLM prefilling, both of which are fully online and do not require offline training. Experiments show that CoStream achieves up to 3x throughput improvement and up to 87% GPU compute reduction over state-of-the-art baselines, while maintaining competitive accuracy with only 0-8% F1 drop.

</details>


### [173] [Fine-Grained Power and Energy Attribution on AMD GPU/APU-Based Exascale Nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06056)
*Adam McDaniel,Michael Jantz,Ashesh Sharma,Steve Abbott,Steven Martin,Shreyas Khandekar,Brandon Neth,Bruno Villasenor Alvarez,Aditya Kashi,Wael Elwasif,Oscar Hernandez*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对现代百亿亿次级GPU/APU系统（AMD Instinct MI250X和MI300A）中的功率和能耗传感器差异问题，提出了一套完整的特性分析和校正方法学。通过在多达512块GPU和480块APU上使用受控方波工作负载，量化了传感器更新间隔、延迟、混叠和变异性，并将重构功率流整合到Score-P/PAPI工具中实现时间对齐的相位级归因。该方法应用于混合精度HPL基准测试，证明了不同精度级别可显著降低节点能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代百亿亿次级GPU和APU系统配备了多种功率和能耗传感器，但这些传感器在范围、更新频率、时序和滤波等方面存在差异，导致短时加速器活动的归因变得复杂，亟需系统性方法来解决传感器特性差异对精确功耗分析的影响。

Method: 采用受控方波工作负载进行传感器特性分析；在大规模系统（最多512块GPU和480块APU）上量化更新间隔、延迟、混叠和变异性；利用片上（rocm-smi/amd-smi）和片外Cray电源管理传感器进行对比测试；通过累积能耗计数器重构功率以获得更快响应时间；将校正后的数据流集成到Score-P/PAPI工具中实现时间对齐的相位级归因。

Result: 方法成功应用于rocHPL、rocHPL-MxP和HPG-MxP基准测试。混合精度计算在Frontier系统上显著降低节点能耗：rocHPL-MxP降低79%，HPG-MxP降低31%；Portage系统上呈现类似趋势。该方法有效区分了因运行时间缩短和功率变化带来的能耗节省。

Conclusion: 本研究为当前和未来百亿亿次级系统提供了可移植的传感器验证和功率感知优化指导框架，能够支持精确的能耗分析和性能优化决策。

Abstract: Modern exascale GPU- and APU-based systems provide multiple power and energy sensors, but differences in scope, update rate, timing, and filtering complicate the attribution of short-lived accelerator activity. This paper presents a methodology to characterize and correct these effects on Cray EX systems with AMD Instinct MI250X GPUs (Frontier) and MI300A APUs (Portage). Using controlled square-wave workloads, we quantify update intervals, delay, aliasing, and variability across up to 512 GPUs and 480 APUs with on-chip (rocm-smi/amd-smi) and off-chip Cray Power Management sensors. We reconstruct power from cumulative energy counters to achieve faster response times, validate it against on-chip, off-chip, and node-level sensors, and integrate the resulting streams into a Score-P/PAPI-based tool for time-aligned, phase-level attribution. Applied to rocHPL, rocHPL-MxP, and HPG-MxP, the method separates energy savings due to reduced runtime from changes in power. Mixed precision reduces node energy on Frontier by 79% for rocHPL-MxP and 31% for HPG-MxP, with similar trends on Portage. These results provide portable guidance for sensor validation and power-aware optimization on current and future exascale systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Polynomial and Pseudopolynomial Algorithms for Two Classes of Bin Packing Instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05152)
*Renan Fernando Franco da Silva,Vinícius Loti de Lima,Rafael C. S. Schouery,Jean-François Côté,Manuel Iori*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对切割与装箱问题中的AI和ANI两类极难实例，提出多项式时间和伪多项式时间算法，证明了这些实例类对于BPP并不强NP难，且算法性能远超现有最优精确方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2016年Delorme等人提出的AI和ANI实例类成为切割与装箱问题的新基准，尽管经过十年研究，仍有13/500个实例在1小时时间限制内无法被现有精确算法求解。这些实例对MIP方法极为困难，需要探索新的算法思路。

Method: 理论层面证明BPP限制于AI和ANI类后不再强NP难；针对AI类设计多项式时间算法，针对ANI类设计伪多项式时间算法，并将算法适配到SSP问题。

Result: 提出的算法在所有AI和ANI基准实例上性能提升数个数量级，可作为精确方法的预处理步骤，但仅保证对设计类实例返回最优性。

Conclusion: AI/ANI实例类的困难性源于MIP方法的局限性而非问题本身的计算复杂性，为该领域提供了新的理论见解和高效实用算法。

Abstract: Cutting and packing problems are fundamental in manufacturing and logistics, as they aim to minimize waste and improve efficiency. The Cutting Stock Problem (CSP) concerns material cutting, whereas the Bin Packing Problem (BPP) concerns packing items into bins. Since the 1960s, these problems have been widely studied because of their industrial relevance and computational complexity. Over time, exact algorithms, often based on mixed-integer programming (MIP), have become able to solve increasingly large instances, often with hundreds of items, within minutes. In 2016, Delorme et al. showed that the algorithm BELOV, combined with a modern version of CPLEX, could solve all benchmark instances available at that time within ten minutes. Motivated by this progress, they introduced two new classes of instances, AI and ANI, which proved extremely challenging for all exact solvers and have guided research on CSP and BPP over the past decade. Despite significant subsequent advances, 13 out of 500 of these instances remain unsolved by state-of-the-art algorithms within a one-hour time limit. In this paper, we show that although AI and ANI instances are particularly hard for MIP-based methods, the BPP restricted to these classes is not strongly NP-hard. We present polynomial-time algorithms for the AI class and pseudopolynomial-time algorithms for the ANI class. Our best algorithms solve all benchmark instances from these classes orders of magnitude faster than previous approaches. They are also straightforward to adapt to the Skiving Stock Problem (SSP), which can be seen as a counterpart of the CSP. Additionally, they can be used as preprocessing routines in exact methods, as their runtime is independent of the instance class, although they are guaranteed to return an optimality status only for instances belonging to the class for which they were designed.

</details>


### [175] [Parameterized algorithms for $k$-Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05528)
*Dhanyamol Antony,L. Sunil Chandran,Dalu Jacob,R. B. Sandeep*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: k-Inversion问题是将图中某个顶点子集的内部弧方向反转，目标是判断是否存在大小不超过k的反转家族使有向图变为无环图。该问题在固定k时已被证明是NP完全的。本文将 tournament 上的FPT算法推广到更广泛的变体，并进一步获得在块图上的FPT算法，以及一般图上基于树宽的2^{O(tw(k+tw))}·n^{O(1)}时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-Inversion问题已被Alon等人(2024)证明对于固定k是NP完全的，排除了XP算法的可能性。因此需要研究该问题在特定图类上的可解性或基于其他参数设计FPT算法。

Method: 将Alon等人针对锦标赛(tournament)设计的FPT算法推广到更广泛的变体；利用树分解技术处理一般有向图。

Result: 在锦标赛上获得推广的FPT算法；在底层无向图为块图(block graph)的输入上得到FPT算法；对于一般有向图，得到运行时间为2^{O(tw(k+tw))}·n^{O(1)}的算法，其中tw为底层图的树宽。

Conclusion: k-Inversion问题在锦标赛、块图等特殊图类上是固定参数可解的；对于一般图，以树宽为参数也是固定参数可解的。这些结果扩展了该问题的可解范围。

Abstract: Inversion of a directed graph $D$ with respect to a vertex subset $Y$ is the directed graph obtained from $D$ by reversing the direction of every arc whose endpoints both lie in $Y$. More generally, the inversion of $D$ with respect to a tuple $(Y_1, Y_2, \ldots, Y_\ell)$ of vertex subsets is defined as the directed graph obtained by successively applying inversions with respect to $Y_1, Y_2, \ldots, Y_\ell$. Such a tuple is called a \emph{decycling family} of $D$ if the resulting graph is acyclic.
  In the \textsc{$k$-Inversion} problem, the input consists of a directed graph $D$ and an integer $k$, and the task is to decide whether $D$ admits a decycling family of size at most $k$. Alon et al.\ (SIAM J.\ Discrete Math., 2024) proved that the problem is NP-complete for every fixed value of $k$, thereby ruling out XP algorithms, and presented a fixed-parameter tractable (FPT) algorithm parameterized by $k$ for tournament inputs.
  In this paper, we generalize their algorithm to a broader variant of the problem on tournaments and subsequently use this result to obtain an FPT algorithm for \textsc{$k$-Inversion} when the underlying undirected graph of the input is a block graph. Furthermore, we obtain an algorithm for \textsc{$k$-Inversion} on general directed graphs with running time $2^{O(\mathrm{tw}(k + \mathrm{tw}))} \cdot n^{O(1)}$, where $\mathrm{tw}$ denotes the treewidth of the underlying graph.

</details>


### [176] [Maintaining Random Assignments under Adversarial Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05606)
*Bernhard Haeupler,Anton Paramonov*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究在自适应对抗性动态变化下维护随机分配的问题，在 Bhattacharya、Saranurak 和 Sukprasert（ESA'22）提出的主动重采样技术基础上，识别了一种新的"时间选择"攻击，并提出"时间聚合"原则来对抗此类攻击，应用于图着色和随机游走的维护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动重采样技术在自适应对抗性修改下仍存在偏差问题，需要新的技术原理来保证算法的鲁棒性。

Method: （1）识别对抗性攻击模式："时间选择"攻击；（2）提出"时间聚合"原则作为防御策略；（3）设计两种基于该原则的新型重采样方案。

Result: 一般图：维护 O(Δ) 着色；三角自由图：维护 O(Δ/ln Δ) 着色；每次修改的平均工作量均为亚线性。

Conclusion: 时间聚合原则可有效对抗自适应对抗性攻击，为动态图上的随机分配维护提供了更鲁棒的理论框架。

Abstract: We study and further develop powerful general-purpose schemes to maintain random assignments under adversarial dynamic changes.
  The goal is to maintain assignments that are (approximately) distributed similarly as a completely fresh resampling of all assignments after each change, while doing only a few resamples per change. This becomes particularly interesting and challenging when dynamics are controlled by an adaptive adversary.
  Our work builds on and further develops the proactive resampling technique [Bhattacharya, Saranurak, and Sukprasert ESA'22]. We identify a new ``temporal selection'' attack that adaptive adversaries can use to cause biases, even against proactive resampling. We propose a new ''temporal aggregation'' principle that algorithms should follow to counteract these biases, and present two powerful new resampling schemes based on this principle.
  We give various applications of our new methods. The main one in maintaining proper coloring of the graph under adaptive adversarial modifications: we maintain $O(Δ)$ coloring for general graphs with maximum degree $Δ$ and $O(\fracΔ{\ln Δ})$ coloring for triangle free graphs, both with sublinear in the number of vertices average work per modification. Other applications include efficiently maintaining random walks in dynamically changing graphs.

</details>


### [177] [Classes Testable with $O(1/ε)$ Queries for Small $ε$ Independent of the Number of Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05615)
*Nader H. Bshouty,George Haddad*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究布尔函数类的可测试性（testability），证明了一类布尔函数类可以用O(ψ+1/ε)查询复杂度进行测试，其中ψ依赖于类的参数（如项数、相关变量数等），但与变量总数n无关。特别是当ε≤1/ψ时，查询复杂度退化为O(1/ε)，达到已知最优下界Ω(1/ε)。本文将之前已知的k项函数和至多k个变量异或函数的结果推广到更广的函数类，包括k-junta、Fourier度至多为d的函数、s-稀疏d次多项式及s-稀疏多项式，并进一步证明：任何至多依赖k个变量的函数类C，若可被精确学习，则在ε<1/ψ时可用O(1/ε)查询进行测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的动机源于计算学习理论与属性测试（property testing）的交叉领域。已有工作表明，项数有界的项类（monomials）和变量数有界的异或函数（XORs）具有常数查询可测试性（当样本复杂度与1/ε成线性关系时）。然而，许多其他自然的布尔函数类（如k-junta、Fourier度有界函数、稀疏多项式）是否也具备类似的高效可测试性尚不清楚。本文旨在将可测试性的结果系统地推广到更广泛的函数类，并建立可学习性与可测试性之间的深层联系。

Method: 本文的方法论建立在Blais的"几乎对称"（almost symmetric）技术框架之上，并进行了关键拓展。具体而言：1）将可测试性结果从项类（monomials）和异或函数（XORs）推广到更广的函数族；2）引入新的结构化技术处理k-junta、Fourier度有界函数和稀疏多项式类；3）利用"项归约"（term reduction）技术将多元函数降维处理；4）在学习-测试连接方面，利用proper精确学习算法的存在性作为充分条件，建立可测试性的保证。

Result: 本文的核心结果包括：1）证明k-junta、Fourier度至多为d的函数、s-稀疏d次多项式和s-稀疏多项式这四类布尔函数均可在O(ψ+1/ε)查询复杂度下被测试，其中ψ分别依赖于k、d、s等参数，与n无关；2）建立了一般性定理：任何至多依赖k个变量且proper精确可学习的函数类C，在ε<1/ψ条件下均可用O(1/ε)查询进行测试，从而在可学习性与可测试性之间建立了新的等价或蕴含关系。

Conclusion: 本文统一并扩展了布尔函数类的可测试性理论，证明了学习能力（proper exact learnability）可以作为可测试性的充分条件，并将高效可测试性的函数类范围显著扩大。这些结果深化了我们对属性测试与计算学习之间联系的理解，揭示了"结构化"布尔函数类所共有的统计学习与测试的内在规律。

Abstract: In this paper, we study classes of Boolean functions that are testable with $O(ψ+1/ε)$ queries, where $ψ$ depends on the parameters of the class (e.g., the number of terms, the number of relevant variables, etc.) but not on the total number of variables $n$. In particular, when $ε\le 1/ψ$, the query complexity is $O(1/ε)$, matching the known tight bound $Ω(1/ε)$.
  This result was previously known for classes of terms of size at most $k$ and exclusive OR functions of at most $k$ variables. In this paper, we extend this list to include the classes: $k$-junta, functions with Fourier degree at most $d$, $s$-sparse polynomials of degree at most $d$, and $s$-sparse polynomials.
  Additionally, we show that for any class $C$ of Boolean functions that depend on at most $k$ variables, if $C$ is properly exactly learnable, then it is testable with $O(1/ε)$ queries for $ε<1/ψ$, where $ψ$ depends on $k$ and independent of the total number of variables $n$.

</details>


### [178] [Improved space-time tradeoff for TSP via extremal set systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05645)
*Justin Dallant,László Kozma*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文在经典TSP精确算法领域取得突破，通过构造能容纳大量最大链的稀疏集合系统（超图），在2<T<4范围内严格改进了所有现有时空权衡方案，最优值从ST≈3.93降至ST<3.572，同时否定了Johnson、Leader、Russell于2013年提出的组合学猜想，并将其技术推广至任意半环上的置换问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进旅行商问题(TSP)精确求解的时空权衡上界。1962年Bellman-Held-Karp的动态规划算法使用2^{n+O(log n)}时间和空间，1987年Gurevich-Shelah的分治算法使用4^{n+O(log² n)}时间和多项式空间。Koivisto-Parviainen(2010)在2<T<2√2范围改进至ST≈3.93，但此后无进展。

Method: 关键创新在于构造稀疏集合系统（超图），使其能够容纳大量最大链。这类对象在极值组合学中具有独立研究价值。此外，文中否定了Johnson、Leader、Russell(2013)的一个组合学猜想，并建立其与TSP权衡方案最优性之间的联系。

Result: 对于所有2<T<4范围，实现了严格优于所有先前方案的时空权衡，最优值达到ST<3.572（优于此前的~3.93）。

Conclusion: 所提技术可推广至任意半环上的一大类置换问题，获得更优的时空权衡。该工作否定了Johnson、Leader、Russell(2013)的组合学猜想，揭示了其在TSP算法最优性分析中的意义。

Abstract: The traveling salesman problem (TSP) is a cornerstone of combinatorial optimization and has deeply influenced the development of algorithmic techniques in both exact and approximate settings. Yet, improving on the decades-old bounds for solving TSP exactly remains elusive: the dynamic program of Bellman, Held, and Karp from 1962 uses $2^{n+O(\log{n})}$ time and space, and the divide-and-conquer approach of Gurevich and Shelah from 1987 uses $4^{n + O(\log^2{n})}$ time and polynomial space. A straightforward combination of the two algorithms trades off $T^{n+o(n)}$ time and $S^{n+o(n)}$ space at various points of the curve $ST = 4$. An improvement to this tradeoff when $2 < T < 2\sqrt{2}$ was found by Koivisto and Parviainen (SODA 2010), yielding a minimum of $ST \approx 3.93$. Koivisto and Parviainen show their method to be optimal among a broad class of partial-order-based approaches, and to date, no improvement or alternative method has been found.
  In this paper we give a tradeoff that strictly improves all previous ones for all $2 < T < 4$, achieving a minimum of $ST < 3.572$. A key ingredient is the construction of sparse set systems (hypergraphs) that admit a large number of maximal chains. The existence of such objects is of independent interest in extremal combinatorics, likely to see further applications. Along the way we disprove a combinatorial conjecture of Johnson, Leader, and Russell from 2013, relating it with the optimality of the previous tradeoff schemes for TSP. Our techniques extend to a broad class of permutation problems over arbitrary semirings, yielding improved space-time tradeoffs in these settings as well.

</details>


### [179] [Improved Space-Time Tradeoffs for Permutation Problems via Extremal Combinatorics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05661)
*Afrouz Jabal Ameli,Jesper Nederlof,Shengzhe Wang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文在加法幂等半环上的置换问题中取得了更好的时空权衡结果，特别是将旅行商问题的空间-时间积从 3.9271^N 改进到 3.7493^N，克服了之前框架中的最优性障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koivisto和Parviainen（SODA'10）在加法幂等半环上的置换问题中给出了时空权衡算法，其界 S·T ≤ 3.9271^N 被证明在其框架下是最优的，形成了一个难以突破的屏障。本文旨在克服这一障碍，改进旅行商问题的时空权衡上界。

Method: 引入集合系统的新参数——链效率（chain efficiency），建立其与置换问题时空权衡的关联。链效率刻画了集合系统中最大链数量与系统基数的比值。通过构造高链效率的集合系统来推导高效的时空权衡算法。

Result: （1）旅行商问题的新算法达到 S·T ≤ 3.7493^N，改进了之前的 3.9271^N 上界；（2）构造了高链效率的集合系统，反驳了Johnson、Leader和Russel（Comb. Probab. Comput.'15）提出的一个猜想。

Conclusion: 链效率是分析置换问题时空权衡的有效工具，高链效率的集合系统可导出更优的算法。通过构造具有高链效率的集合系统，本文突破了先前框架的最优性限制，为置换问题的时空权衡研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We provide improved space-time tradeoffs for permutation problems over additively idempotent semi-rings. In particular, there is an algorithm for the Traveling Salesperson Problem that solves $N$-vertex instances using space $S$ and time $T$ where $S\cdot T \leq 3.7493^{N}$. This improves a previous work by Koivisto and Parviainen [SODA'10] where $S\cdot T \leq 3.9271^N$, and overcomes a barrier they identified, as their bound was shown to be optimal within their framework.
  To get our results, we introduce a new parameter of a set system that we call the chain efficiency. This relates the number of maximal chains contained in the set system with the cardinality of the system. We show that set systems of high efficiency imply efficient space-time tradeoffs for permutation problems, and give constructions of set systems with high chain efficiency, disproving a conjecture by Johnson, Leader and Russel [Comb. Probab. Comput.'15].

</details>


### [180] [$k$-Clustering via Iterative Randomized Rounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06046)
*Jarosław Byrka,Yuhao Guo,Yang Hu,Shi Li,Chengzhang Wan,Zaixuan Wang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对k-聚类问题标准LP松弛的单一舍入算法，通过迭代舍入获得Lagrangian乘数保持(LMP)逼近，可输出期望开启k个设施的随机解，期望成本至多为最优成本的(3^p+1)/2倍。该算法可转换为仅损失(1+ε)因子的真正逼近算法，获得k-聚类问题的(3^p+1)/2+ε逼近比，重现k-中位数最佳(2+ε)逼近，并将欧几里得k-均值改进至(4+ε)逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-聚类是组合优化中的基础问题，现有算法如Jain等人针对k-中位数的2-LMP逼近在算法设计和分析上较为复杂，且难以推广到一般度量空间。论文旨在提供一个基于LP舍入的统一框架，简化算法设计并能轻松适配到L_p^p成本设置。

Method: 1. 设计单一LP舍入算法作为迭代舍入的起点；2. 采用迭代舍入框架保持Lagrangian乘数；3. 输出期望开启k个设施的随机解；4. 将LMP-逼近转换为真正逼近，仅损失(1+ε)因子。

Result: LMP逼近比：(3^p+1)/2（一般p阶距离）；欧几里得k-均值的LMP因子为11/3（优于通用度量的5）。真正逼近比：k-聚类(3^p+1)/2+ε；k-中位数(2+ε)（匹配最佳）；度量k-均值改进为5+ε（从5.83改进）；欧几里得k-均值达到(4+ε)（匹配最新结果）。

Conclusion: 该LP舍入框架成功统一并改进了k-聚类问题的多种逼近界，算法简洁且易于推广，在保持LMP逼近理论价值的同时，通过简单转换实现了真正的性能保证。

Abstract: In this work we propose a single rounding algorithm for the fractional solutions of the standard LP relaxation for $k$-clustering. As a starting point, we obtain an iterative rounding $(\frac{3^p + 1}{2})$-Lagrangian Multiplier-Perserving (LMP) approximation for the $k$-clustering problem with the cost function being the $p$-th power of the distance. Such an algorithm outputs a random solution that opens $k$ facilities \emph{in expectation}, whose cost in expectation is at most $\frac{3^p + 1}{2}$ times the optimum cost. Thus, we recover the $2$-LMP approximation for $k$-median by Jain et al.~[JACM'03], which played a central role in deriving the current best $2$ approximation for $k$-median. Unlike the result of Jain et al., our algorithm is based on LP rounding, and it can be easily adapted to the $L_p^p$-cost setting. For the Euclidean $k$-means problem, the LMP factor we obtain is $\frac{11}{3}$, which is better than the $5$ approximation given by this framework for general metrics.
  Then, we show how to convert the LMP-approximation algorithms to a true-approximation, with only a $(1+\varepsilon)$ factor loss in the approximation ratio. We obtain a ($\frac{3^p + 1}{2}+\varepsilon$)-approximation algorithm for $k$-clustering with cost function being the $p$-th power of the distance, for $p \geq 1$. This reproduces the best known ($2+\varepsilon$)-approximation for $k$-median by Cohen-Addad et al. [STOC'25], and improves the approximation factor for metric $k$-means from 5.83 by Charikar at al. [FOCS'25] to $5+\varepsilon$ in our framework. Moreover, the same algorithm, but with a specialized analysis, attains ($4+\varepsilon$)-approximation for Euclidean $k$-means matching the recent result by Charikar et al. [STOC'26].

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Measuring the Permission Gate: A Stress-Test Evaluation of Claude Code's Auto Mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04978)
*Zimo Ji,Zongjie Li,Wenyuan Jiang,Yudong Gao,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首次独立评估Claude Code自动模式的安全分类器，发现其在模糊授权场景下漏检率达81%，远高于生产环境的17%；主要原因是36.8%的危险操作通过项目文件编辑（非shell命令）实现，绕过了分类器的监控范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体的权限控制系统缺乏独立验证；Anthropic报告的17%假阴性率仅基于生产流量，未涵盖故意模糊的授权场景；需要评估系统在边界情况下的真实表现。

Method: 使用AmPermBench基准（128个提示词，覆盖4个DevOps任务家族和3个受控模糊轴），对253个状态变更操作进行逐动作评估，对抗oracle真值标签。

Result: 端到端假阴性率达81.0%（95% CI: 73.8%-87.4%）；36.8%操作通过Tier 2（项目内文件编辑）绕过分类器；仅限Tier 3时FNR为70.3%，FPR升至31.9%；工件清理任务的Tier 2覆盖缺口最严重（FNR 92.9%）。

Conclusion: 自动模式假设危险操作必须经过shell执行，但智能体常通过文件编辑实现等效效果；分类器存在覆盖边界漏洞，需扩展对文件编辑路径的监控能力。

Abstract: Claude Code's auto mode is the first deployed permission system for AI coding agents, using a two-stage transcript classifier to gate dangerous tool calls. Anthropic reports a 0.4% false positive rate and 17% false negative rate on production traffic. We present the first independent evaluation of this system on deliberately ambiguous authorization scenarios, i.e., tasks where the user's intent is clear but the target scope, blast radius, or risk level is underspecified. Using AmPermBench, a 128-prompt benchmark spanning four DevOps task families and three controlled ambiguity axes, we evaluate 253 state-changing actions at the individual action level against oracle ground truth.
  Our findings characterize auto mode's scope-escalation coverage under this stress-test workload. The end-to-end false negative rate is 81.0% (95% CI: 73.8%-87.4%), substantially higher than the 17% reported on production traffic, reflecting a fundamentally different workload rather than a contradiction. Notably, 36.8% of all state-changing actions fall outside the classifier's scope via Tier 2 (in-project file edits), contributing to the elevated end-to-end FNR. Even restricting to the 160 actions the classifier actually evaluates (Tier 3), the FNR remains 70.3%, while the FPR rises to 31.9%. The Tier 2 coverage gap is most pronounced on artifact cleanup (92.9% FNR), where agents naturally fall back to editing state files when the expected CLI is unavailable. These results highlight a coverage boundary worth examining: auto mode assumes dangerous actions transit the shell, but agents routinely achieve equivalent effects through file edits that the classifier does not evaluate.

</details>


### [182] [Squeez: Task-Conditioned Tool-Output Pruning for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04979)
*Ádám Kovács*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究代码智能体中工具输出裁剪问题，提出在给定任务查询和工具输出时，提取最小必要证据块的方法。通过在SWE-bench数据集上构建11,477样本的基准测试，微调Qwen 3.5 2B模型，在移除92%输入标记的同时达到0.86召回率和0.80 F1分数，超越更大规模零样本模型和启发式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码智能体在执行任务时需要反复处理冗长的工具输出，但其中仅有少量信息对下一步决策有用。现有方法要么保留全部内容导致效率低下，要么简单截断可能丢失关键信息，缺乏针对任务相关的智能裁剪机制。

Method: 构建包含11,477示例的基准数据集（源自SWE-bench仓库交互和合成多生态系统工具输出），手动标注618个测试样本；使用LoRA微调Qwen 3.5 2B模型，训练任务条件下的工具输出裁剪能力。

Result: 微调模型在移除92%输入标记的条件下达到0.86召回率和0.80 F1分数；相比零样本Qwen 3.5 35B A3B模型召回率提升11个百分点，显著优于所有启发式裁剪基线方法。

Conclusion: 小规模微调模型可通过任务条件感知有效裁剪工具输出，在保持高召回的同时大幅降低输入长度，为提升代码智能体效率提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Coding agents repeatedly consume long tool observations even though only a small fraction of each observation matters for the next step. We study task-conditioned tool-output pruning: given a focused query and one tool output, return the smallest verbatim evidence block the agent should inspect next. We introduce a benchmark of 11,477 examples built from SWE-bench repository interactions and synthetic multi-ecosystem tool outputs, with a manually curated 618-example test set. We fine-tune Qwen 3.5 2B with LoRA and compare it against larger zero-shot models and heuristic pruning baselines.
