Our model reaches 0.86 recall and 0.80 F1 while removing 92% of input tokens, outperforming zero-shot Qwen 3.5 35B A3B by 11 recall points and all heuristic baselines by a wide margin.

</details>


### [183] [Nidus: Externalized Reasoning for AI-Assisted Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05080)
*Danil Gorinevski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Nidus是一个治理运行时系统，通过机械化的V模型实现AI辅助软件交付。在自托管部署中，三个LLM家族（Claude、Gemini、Codex）在每次提交时验证证明义务的当前义务集下交付了一个100,000行系统，系统自行管理自身构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程不变量（追踪的需求、有据可查的架构、有证据的交付物）无法作为学习行为可靠维护；保证需要由提案者外部的机制强制执行。组织标准需编译成可导入的约束库，由可判定评估强制执行。

Method: 将工程方法论外部化为可判定工件，在每次变更持久化前验证。组织标准编译为指南手册——约束库由治理项目导入并由可判定评估强制执行。

Result: 三个LLM家族在自托管部署中交付了100,000行系统，每个义务永久消除一类未工程化输出。开发历史形成形式化开发——每个状态满足所有活动义务，义务集单调增长。

Conclusion: 四项贡献：(1)递归自我治理——约束表面约束对自身的变更；(2)信息素协调——通过表面摩擦引导智能体，无中央控制；(3)近端规范强化——活制品外部化工程上下文，UNSAT判决在推理时塑造行为，无需权重更新；(4)治理剧场预防——合规证据无法在建模的变更路径内伪造。

Abstract: We present Nidus, a governance runtime that mechanizes the V-model for AI-assisted software delivery. In the self-hosting deployment, three LLM families (Claude, Gemini, Codex) delivered a 100,000-line system under proof obligations verified against the current obligation set on every commit. The system governed its own construction.
  Engineering invariants - traced requirements, justified architecture, evidenced deliveries - cannot be reliably maintained as learned behavior; assurance requires enforcement by a mechanism external to the proposer. Nidus externalizes the engineering methodology into a decidable artifact verified on every mutation before persistence. Organizational standards compile into guidebooks - constraint libraries imported by governed projects and enforced by decidable evaluation.
  Four contributions: (1) recursive self-governance - the constraint surface constrains mutations to itself; (2) stigmergic coordination - friction from the surface routes agents without central control; (3) proximal spec reinforcement - the living artifact externalizes the engineering context that RL and long-chain reasoning try to internalize; the specification is the reward function, UNSAT verdicts shape behavior at inference time, no weight updates; (4) governance theater prevention - compliance evidence cannot be fabricated within the modeled mutation path. The constraint surface compounds: each obligation permanently eliminates a class of unengineered output. The artifact's development history is a formal development - every state satisfies all active obligations, and the obligation set grows monotonically.

</details>


### [184] [Closed-Loop Autonomous Software Development via Jira-Integrated Backlog Orchestration: A Case Study in Deterministic Control and Safety-Constrained Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05000)
*Elias Calboreanu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: This paper presents a closed-loop system for software lifecycle management framed as a control architecture rather than a code-generation tool. The system manages a backlog of approximately 1,602 rows across seven task families, ingests 13 structured source documents, and executes a deterministic seven-stage pipeline implemented as seven scheduled automation lanes. The automation stack comprises approximately 12,661 lines of Python across 23 scripts plus 6,907 lines of versioned prompt specifications, with checkpoint-based time budgets, 101 exception handlers, and 12 centralized lock mechanisms implemented through four core functions and eight reusable patterns. A Jira Status Contract provides externally observable collision locking, and a degraded-mode protocol supports continued local operation when Jira is unavailable. Artificial-intelligence assistance is bounded by structured context packages, configured resource caps, output re-validation, and human review gates. A formal evaluation of the initial 152-run window yielded 100% terminal-state success with a 95% Clopper-Pearson interval of [97.6%, 100%]; the system has since accumulated more than 795 run artifacts in continuous operation. Three rounds of adversarial code review identified 51 findings, all closed within the study scope (48 fully remediated, 3 closed with deferred hardening), with zero false negatives within the injected set. In an autonomous security ticket family of 10 items, six were completed through pipeline-autonomous dispatch and verification, two required manual remediation, and two were closed by policy decision. The results indicate that bounded, traceable lifecycle automation is practical when autonomy is embedded within explicit control, recovery, and audit mechanisms.

</details>


### [185] [Models as Values in a Model Expression Algebra: A Functional Approach to Model Driven Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05001)
*Sinisa Neskovic,Dejan Stojimirovic*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了模型表达式代数(Model Expression Algebra)作为模型驱动工程(MDE)的功能基础，通过单一形式主义统一模型构造、元模型、模板和转换。其中模型为值，模型表达式为项，求值为从项到值的解释同态。代数由四类算子组成：模型/元素创建算子、引用算子和计算算子。元模型被形式化为约束代数的类型模式，模板被理解为带自由变量的开项，转换则自然地表示为以源模型为输入参数的模型模板。论文证明了求值下的类型保持性和转换执行的类型安全性，并通过嵌入DSL在主流语言中实现，同时支持宏模型和编织模型而无需额外机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型驱动工程中模型构造、元模型、模板和转换通常使用不同的形式主义和工具链，缺乏统一语义基础，导致理论与实现之间存在语义鸿沟，且难以在单一语言中提供形式化保证。

Method: 建立模型表达式代数形式体系，将模型作为值、模型表达式作为项、求值作为解释同态；元模型形式化为类型模式约束代数；模板形式化为带自由变量的开项；转换形式化为以模型为输入参数的模型模板；通过主流语言的类型系统强制执行形式保证。

Result: 证明了求值下的类型保持性和转换执行的类型安全性；展示了如何用单一主流语言同时作为元建模、模型构造和转换语言；无需额外机制即可支持宏模型和编织模型。

Conclusion: 模型表达式代数为MDE提供了统一的功能语义基础，通过将模型作为一等公民值、模板作为开项、转换作为参数化模型表达式，实现了理论与实现的语义一致，并借助宿主语言类型系统提供形式保证。该方法为MDE工具链的整合提供了原则性途径。

Abstract: This paper proposes a functional foundation for model driven engineering that unifies model construction, metamodels, templates, and transformations under a single formalism: the model expression algebra. In this algebra, models are values, model expressions are terms, and evaluation is the interpretation homomorphism from terms to values. Model expressions are composed from four operators: model creation and element creation operators, reference operators for retrieving models and elements, and computation operators that embed functional computations. Metamodels are type schemas that constrain the algebra, and model templates, understood as parameterized model expressions, are formalized as open terms with free variables. Model transformations then arise naturally as model templates whose input parameter is a source model. We prove type preservation under evaluation and type safety of transformation execution. Since models are themselves model elements, the algebra also supports megamodels and weaving models without additional mechanisms. The approach is realized through an embedded domain-specific language (DSL) that demonstrates how a single mainstream language can serve simultaneously as the metamodeling, model construction, and transformation language, with formal guarantees enforced by the language's type system.

</details>


### [186] [Scaling Coding Agents via Atomic Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05013)
*Yingwei Ma,Yue Liu,Xinlong Yang,Yanhao Li,Kelin Fu,Yibo Miao,Yuchong Xie,Zhexu Wang,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出基于原子技能的新 scaling 范式，通过定义代码定位、编辑、单元测试生成、问题复现、代码审查五个基础原子技能并进行联合强化学习训练，提升 LLM 编程智能体的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 编程智能体主要在复合基准（如 bug 修复）上训练，导致任务特定的过拟合和有限的泛化能力。复合任务难以分解，难以实现通用技能的有效扩展。

Method: 首先形式化定义五个基础原子技能（代码定位、代码编辑、单元测试生成、问题复现、代码审查），这些技能更通用且可组合。然后通过对原子技能进行联合强化学习（Joint RL）来扩展编程智能体，避免技能间的负向干扰和权衡。

Result: 原子技能的提升能够泛化到其他未见复合编程任务（bug 修复、代码重构、机器学习工程、代码安全）。联合 RL 在 5 个原子技能和 5 个复合任务上平均性能提升 18.7%。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了"通过原子技能训练"这一新 scaling 范式的有效性，为编程智能体的训练提供了新方向，强调了基础技能分解对泛化能力的重要性。

Abstract: Current LLM coding agents are predominantly trained on composite benchmarks (e.g., bug fixing), which often leads to task-specific overfitting and limited generalization. To address this, we propose a novel scaling paradigm that shifts the focus from task-level optimization to atomic skill mastery. We first formalize five fundamental atomic skills, code localization, code editing, unit-test generation, issue reproduction, and code review, that serve as the basis vectors for complex software engineering tasks. Compared with composite coding tasks, these atomic skills are more generalizable and composable. Then, we scale coding agents by performing joint RL over atomic skills. In this manner, atomic skills are consistently improved without negative interference or trade-offs between them. Notably, we observe that improvements in these atomic skills generalize well to other unseen composite coding tasks, such as bug-fixing, code refactoring, machine learning engineering, and code security. The observation motivates a new scaling paradigm for coding agents by training with atomic skills. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed paradigm. Notably, our joint RL improves average performance by 18.7% on 5 atomic skills and 5 composite tasks.

</details>


### [187] [Typify: A Lightweight Usage-driven Static Analyzer for Precise Python Type Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05067)
*Ali Aman,Muhammad Asaduzzaman,Shaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Typify是一个轻量级的Python类型推断引擎，结合符号执行、迭代不动点分析和上下文匹配检索系统，在不依赖深度学习或大数据集的情况下，实现与现有最先进系统相当或更优的类型推断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python的动态类型系统为静态分析和自动化工具带来挑战，现有方法依赖类型注解或需大量训练数据和计算资源的深度学习模型，导致可扩展性和可解释性受限。

Method: Typify集成符号执行与迭代不动点分析，通过上下文匹配检索系统在项目范围内传播和预测类型信息，以执行感知方式构建和遍历依赖图，精确连接函数调用与其定义。

Result: 在ManyTypes4Py和Typilus数据集上，Typify在预测变量、参数和返回语句类型方面一致达到或超越Type4Py、HiTyper等深度学习系统以及Pyre等工业级静态类型推断工具。

Conclusion: 基于使用驱动的检索式推断可匹配或超越数据驱动方法的准确性，为大型和不断演进的Python代码库提供了实用、可解释且计算高效的类型推断替代方案。

Abstract: Python's dynamic type system, while offering significant flexibility and expressiveness, poses substantial challenges for static analysis and automated tooling, particularly in unannotated or partially annotated codebases. Existing type inference approaches often depend on existing type annotations or on deep learning models that require extensive training corpora and considerable computational resources, resulting in limited scalability and reduced interpretability. We introduce Typify, a lightweight, usage-driven static analysis engine designed to infer precise and contextually relevant type information without relying on statistical learning or large datasets. Typify integrates symbolic execution with iterative fixpoint analysis and a context-matching retrieval system to propagate and predict type information across entire projects. By constructing and traversing dependency graphs in an execution-aware manner, Typify accurately connects function calls to their definitions and infers usage-based type semantics, even in complex, interdependent modules. We evaluate Typify on a diverse corpus of real-world Python repositories, including the ManyTypes4Py and Typilus datasets, benchmarking its effectiveness in predicting types of variables, arguments, and return statements. Results from the evaluation show that Typify consistently matches or surpasses state-of-the-art deep learning-based systems such as Type4Py and HiTyper, as well as industry-standard static type inference tools like Pyre. Our findings demonstrate that usage-driven, retrieval-based inference can match or exceed the accuracy of data-driven methods, offering a practical, interpretable, and computationally efficient alternative for large and evolving Python codebases.

</details>


### [188] [Understanding: reframing automation and assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05662)
*Robin Bloomfield*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文探讨了安全与保障案例中"理解"这一关键要素逐渐被忽视的问题。随着系统复杂性和AI生成内容的增加,保障产出的形式可能看起来连贯,但实际上缺乏真正的人类理解。文章提出将"理解"作为决策中显式、可评估和可辩护的组成部分,并基于Catherine Elgin的理解认识论,通过Assurance 2.0框架提出两种具体成果物:Understanding Basis(理解基础)和Personal Understanding Statement(个人理解声明),同时警示自动化可能带来的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关键社会技术系统的生产与评估面临理解挑战:开发节奏加快、审查减少、软件复杂性增加、AI生成内容的广泛使用,可能导致产出看似连贯却缺乏真正人类理解。这对负责任的工程实践和治理决策构成威胁。

Method: 1) 基于Catherine Elgin的理解认识论建立概念基础;2) 以Assurance 2.0作为工程化路径,通过结构化论证、证据、置信度、反驳者(defeaters)和基于理论的自动化来操作化;3) 提出两种linked artefacts作为具体实现。

Result: 提出两个核心成果物:1) Understanding Basis(理解基础) - 证明现有理解对决策是否充分;2) Personal Understanding Statement(个人理解声明) - 使参与者明确表达并可质疑的理解。识别出自动化可能改善成果物生产但削弱理解的风险。

Conclusion: 理解应成为决策中显式、可评估和可辩护的组成部分;需要建立评估自动化效能和认知影响的方法论;当前威胁在于形式连贯性与实质理解之间的鸿沟正在扩大。

Abstract: Safety and assurance cases risk becoming detached from the understanding needed for responsible engineering and governance decisions. More broadly, the production and evaluation of critical socio-technical systems increasingly face an understanding challenge: pressures for increased tempo, reduced scrutiny, software complexity, and growing use of AI generated artefacts may produce outputs that appear coherent without supporting genuine human comprehension. We argue that understanding should become an explicit, assessable, and defensible component of decision making: what developers, assessors, and decision makers grasp about system behavior, evidence, assumptions, risks, and residual uncertainty. Drawing on Catherine Elgin's epistemology of understanding, we outline a conceptual foundation and then use Assurance 2.0 as an engineering route to operationalize using structured argumentation, evidence, confidence, defeaters, and theory based automation. This leads to two linked artefacts: an Understanding Basis, which justifies why available understanding is sufficient for a decision, and a Personal Understanding Statement, through which participants make their grasp explicit and challengeable. We also identify risks that automation may improve artefact production while weakening understanding, and we propose initial directions for evaluating both efficacy and epistemic impact.

</details>


### [189] [Edit, But Verify: An Empirical Audit of Instructed Code-Editing Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05100)
*Amir M. Ebrahimi,Gopi Krishnan Rajbahadur*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对现有的指令化代码编辑基准测试（CanItEdit和EDIT-Bench）进行了全面审计，发现这两个基准测试未能充分代表真实世界的代码编辑场景：超过90%的评估集中于Python，而GitHub最常用的TypeScript完全缺失；前后端开发（占真实编辑活动的46%）严重缺失；文档、测试和维护类编辑（占人类PR的31.4%）完全没有覆盖。EDIT-Bench存在严重的测试质量问题，59%的低覆盖率测试套件无法检测编辑区域外的修改，且有11个问题源于基准测试本身缺陷而非模型能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令化代码编辑（根据自然语言指令修改现有代码）占实际编码助手交互的19%，但直接评估这一能力的基准测试极少。作者调查了150多个代码相关基准测试，仅发现CanItEdit和EDIT-Bench两个符合条件的目标基准测试，因此需要对其质量进行严格审计。

Method: 作者从三个维度进行审计：（1）比较基准测试的编程语言、编辑意图和应用领域与真实世界分布（Copilot Arena、AIDev、GitHub Octoverse）的差异；（2）测量全部213个问题的测试数量、语句覆盖率和测试范围；（3）使用40个LLM在EDIT-Bench上测试，识别因基准测试缺陷导致的问题。

Result: 审计揭示了系统性偏差：两个基准测试>90%集中于Python，缺少TypeScript；前后端开发领域严重缺失；文档/测试/维护编辑零覆盖；测试数量不足（CanItEdit中位数13个，EDIT-Bench中位数4个）；EDIT-Bench中59%的低覆盖率测试套件无法检测编辑区域外的修改；40个LLM在15个问题上完全失败，其中11个源于基准测试artifact而非模型局限；29%的EDIT-Bench问题和6%的CanItEdit问题与其他问题共享代码库。

Conclusion: 现有基准测试测量的构念比实际部署决策所需的更窄。作者提出了六个基于实证的期望标准（desiderata），并发布所有审计 artifacts，以便社区构建能够可靠反映真实世界编辑能力的代码编辑基准测试。

Abstract: Instructed code editing, where an LLM modifies existing code based on a natural language instruction, accounts for roughly 19% of real-world coding assistant interactions. Yet very few benchmarks directly evaluate this capability. From a survey of over 150 code-related benchmarks, we find that only two, CanItEdit and EDIT-Bench, target instructed code editing with human-authored instructions and test-based evaluation. We audit both by comparing their programming languages, edit intents, and application domains against distributions observed in the wild (Copilot Arena, AIDev, GitHub Octoverse), and by measuring test counts, statement coverage, and test scope across all 213 problems. Both benchmarks concentrate over 90\% of evaluation on Python while TypeScript, GitHub's most-used language, is absent. Backend and frontend development, which together constitute 46% of real-world editing activity, are largely missing, and documentation, testing, and maintenance edits (31.4% of human PRs) have zero representation. Both benchmarks have modest test counts (CanItEdit median 13, EDIT-Bench median 4), though CanItEdit compensates with near-complete whole-file coverage and fail-before/pass-after validation. 59\% of EDIT-Bench's low-coverage suites would not detect modifications outside the edit region. EDIT-Bench has 15 problems that are not solved by any of 40 LLMs and 11 of these problems trace failures to poor benchmark artifacts rather than model limitations. Further, 29% of EDIT-Bench problems and 6% of CanItEdit problems share a codebase with at least one other problem within the benchmark. In summary, these benchmarks measure a narrower construct than deployment decisions require. We therefore propose six empirically grounded desiderata and release all audit artifacts so the community can build instructed code-editing benchmarks whose scores reliably reflect real-world editing capability.

</details>


### [190] [A Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Exploit Generation with Constraint-Guided Comprehension and Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05130)
*Siyi Chen,Tianhan Luo,Shijian Wu,Xiangyu Liu,Yilin Zhou,Qi Li,Wenyuan Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Vulnsage是一个用于自动化漏洞利用生成（AEG）的多智能体框架，通过模拟人类安全研究人员的工作流程，将复杂的AEG过程分解为多个专业子智能体（代码分析、代码生成、验证和反思智能体），由中央调度器协调进行迭代循环。实验表明，该框架比现有最先进工具（如explodejs）多生成34.64%的漏洞利用，并在实际场景中发现并验证了146个零日漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源库在现代软件开发中广泛使用，引入大量安全漏洞。现有静态分析工具产生大量误报，而传统AEG方法（如模糊测试、符号执行）面临路径覆盖和约束求解问题。虽然大语言模型在AEG中展现潜力，但如何有效利用它们理解漏洞并生成相应利用程序仍是开放问题。

Method: Vulnsage采用多智能体架构，包含四个核心组件：Code Analyzer Agent负责静态分析识别潜在漏洞并收集信息；Code Generation Agent利用LLM生成候选利用程序；Validation Agent与Reflection Agents形成反馈驱动的自改进循环，使用执行轨迹和运行时错误分析迭代改进利用程序或推理误报；中央调度器（supervisor）协调各智能体进行迭代循环。

Result: 实验评估表明，Vulnsage成功生成了比最先进工具（如explodejs）多34.64%的漏洞利用程序。此外，Vulnsage在现实场景中成功发现并验证了146个零日漏洞，证明了其在软件供应链安全评估中的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 多智能体框架通过模拟人类安全研究人员的协作工作流程，结合LLM的能力和反馈驱动的自改进机制，能够有效解决传统AEG方法的局限性，显著提高漏洞利用生成的成功率，并在实际安全评估中展现出强大的实用价值。

Abstract: Open-source libraries are widely used in modern software development, introducing significant security vulnerabilities. While static analysis tools can identify potential vulnerabilities at scale, they often generate overwhelming reports with high false positive rates. Automated Exploit Generation (AEG) emerges as a promising solution to confirm vulnerability authenticity by generating an exploit. However, traditional AEG approaches based on fuzzing or symbolic execution face path coverage and constraint-solving problems. Although LLMs show great potential for AEG, how to effectively leverage them to comprehend vulnerabilities and generate corresponding exploits is still an open question.
  To address these challenges, we propose Vulnsage, a multi-agent framework for AEG. Vulnsage simulates human security researchers' workflows by decomposing the complex AEG process into multiple specialized sub-agents: Code Analyzer Agent, Code Generation Agent, Validation Agent, and a set of Reflection Agents, orchestrated by a central supervisor through iterative cycles. Given a target program, the Code Analyzer Agent performs static analysis to identify potential vulnerabilities and collects relevant information for each one. The Code Generation Agent then utilizes an LLM to generate candidate exploits. The Validation Agent and Reflection Agents form a feedback-driven self-refinement loop that uses execution traces and runtime error analysis to either improve the exploit iteratively or reason about the false positive alert.
  Experimental evaluation demonstrates that Vulnsage succeeds in generating 34.64\% more exploits than state-of-the-art tools such as \explodejs. Furthermore, Vulnsage has successfully discovered and verified 146 zero-day vulnerabilities in real-world scenarios, demonstrating its practical effectiveness for assisting security assessment in software supply chains.

</details>


### [191] [Compiled AI: Deterministic Code Generation for LLM-Based Workflow Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05150)
*Geert Trooskens,Aaron Karlsberg,Anmol Sharma,Lamara De Brouwer,Max Van Puyvelde,Matthew Young,John Thickstun,Gil Alterovitz,Walter A. De Brouwer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"编译型AI"范式，让大语言模型在编译阶段生成可执行代码工件，之后工作流以确定性方式执行，无需再次调用模型。通过将生成约束在嵌入于验证模板中的窄粒度业务逻辑函数内，编译型AI牺牲运行时灵活性，换取可预测性、可审计性、成本效益和降低的安全风险。系统架构包含四阶段生成-验证流程，在函数调用任务（96%完成率）和文档智能任务（80% KILE准确率）上表现优异，同时在安全评估中实现96.7%的提示注入检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在企业级高风险工作流（尤其是医疗场景）中，可靠性和可审计性至关重要。现有运行时推理方式存在成本高、不确定性大、难以审计等问题。本文旨在研究将LLM的代码生成能力与确定性执行相结合的编译型AI范式，以平衡性能与安全性。

Method: 提出三个核心贡献：(i) 约束型LLM代码生成系统架构；(ii) 四阶段生成-验证管道（将概率模型输出转化为生产级代码工件）；(iii) 评估框架，测量令牌摊销、确定性、可靠性、安全性和成本等运营指标。在BFCL（n=400）和DocILE（n=5,680）数据集上评估。

Result: 函数调用任务：96%任务完成率，零执行令牌，约17笔交易即实现盈亏平衡，1,000笔交易时令牌消耗降低57倍。文档智能：Code Factory变体在关键字段提取（KILE: 80.0%）上匹配直接LLM，同时达到最高行项目识别准确率（LIR: 80.4%）。安全评估（135测试用例）：提示注入检测准确率96.7%，静态代码安全分析准确率87.5%，零假阳性。

Conclusion: 编译型AI通过将生成约束在验证模板中的窄粒度业务逻辑函数，牺牲运行时灵活性以换取可预测性、可审计性、成本效率和降低的安全暴露。该范式在企业级高风险工作流中具有实际应用价值，尤其适用于医疗等对可靠性要求严格的场景。

Abstract: We study compiled AI, a paradigm in which large language models generate executable code artifacts during a compilation phase, after which workflows execute deterministically without further model invocation. This paradigm has antecedents in prior work on declarative pipeline optimization (DSPy) and hybrid neural-symbolic planning (LLM+P); our contribution is a systems-oriented study of its application to high-stakes enterprise workflows, with particular emphasis on healthcare settings where reliability and auditability are critical.
  By constraining generation to narrow business-logic functions embedded in validated templates, compiled AI trades runtime flexibility for predictability, auditability, cost efficiency, and reduced security exposure. We introduce (i) a system architecture for constrained LLM-based code generation, (ii) a four-stage generation-and-validation pipeline that converts probabilistic model output into production-ready code artifacts, and (iii) an evaluation framework measuring operational metrics including token amortization, determinism, reliability, security, and cost.
  We evaluate on two task types: function-calling (BFCL, n=400) and document intelligence (DocILE, n=5,680 invoices). On function-calling, compiled AI achieves 96% task completion with zero execution tokens, breaking even with runtime inference at approximately 17 transactions and reducing token consumption by 57x at 1,000 transactions. On document intelligence, our Code Factory variant matches Direct LLM on key field extraction (KILE: 80.0%) while achieving the highest line item recognition accuracy (LIR: 80.4%). Security evaluation across 135 test cases demonstrates 96.7% accuracy on prompt injection detection and 87.5% on static code safety analysis with zero false positives.

</details>


### [192] [Corporate Training in Brazilian Software Engineering: A Quantitative Study of Professional Perceptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05263)
*Rodrigo Siqueira,Antonio Oliveira,Breno Alves Andrade,Lidiane C. S. Gomes,Danilo Monteiro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究针对软件工程专业人员的企业培训质量与效果影响因素进行定量分析。通过对282名巴西软件工程师的问卷调查，发现认知投入、活动多样性和讲师表现是预测培训质量与效果的最强因素。强制参与会显著降低动机和培训质量感知。研究结果与通用培训效果文献一致，表明软件企业可应用现有培训设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业战略培训对软件工程师的职业发展至关重要，但目前缺乏从专业人员视角评估培训质量和效果的因素分析工具，且软件工程领域尚无经过验证的测量工具。

Method: 采用定量问卷调查法，对282名巴西软件工程专业人员进行调查。开发结构化问卷，使用多氯相关(polychoric correlation)方法进行数据分析。

Result: 三个紧密相关的因素——认知投入、活动多样性和讲师表现，成为感知培训质量和效果的最强预测因子。强制参与显著降低动机和培训质量感知。个人时间影响感知与培训质量基本独立。这些发现与通用培训效果文献一致。

Conclusion: 软件工程领域的培训效果主要由三个因素决定：认知投入、活动多样性和讲师表现。强制参与对动机、相关性感知和培训质量产生负面影响，同时加剧时间负担感知。Salas和Cannon-Bowers框架在软件工程情境中产生一致结果，有望成为未来心理测量验证的候选框架。

Abstract: Context: Strategic corporate training is essential for the sustained professional development of software engineers. However, there is a knowledge gap regarding the factors that drive quality and effectiveness of such training from the professionals' perspective, and no validated instrument exists for assessing these factors in the software engineering (SE) domain. Objective: This study aims to quantitatively analyze which factors influence SE professionals' perceptions of corporate training quality and effectiveness. Method: A quantitative survey was conducted with 282 Brazilian SE professionals. A structured questionnaire was developed and polychoric correlation was adopted for data analysis. Results: Three tightly correlated factors (cognitive engagement, variety of activities, and instructor performance) emerged as the strongest predictors of perceived training quality and effectiveness. Mandatory participation significantly reduces motivation and perceived training quality. Perceived impact on personal time proved to be largely independent of training quality. These findings are consistent with the general training effectiveness literature. Conclusions: Training effectiveness in the SE context is predominantly determined by three factors: cognitive engagement, variety of activities, and instructor performance. Mandatory participation negatively influences motivation, perceived relevance, and perceived training quality, while also amplifying the perception of time burden. The consistency with the general literature suggests that software organizations do not need to reinvent training design principles and can apply established guidelines with confidence. Salas and Cannon-Bowers' framework produced coherent results in the SE context, making it a promising candidate for future psychometric validation.

</details>


### [193] [FLARE: Agentic Coverage-Guided Fuzzing for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05289)
*Mingxuan Hui,Xinyue Li,Lu Wang,Chengcheng Wan,Yifan Wang,Yimian Wang,Feiyue Song,Beining Shi,Yixi Li,Yaxiao Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FLARE是一个针对多智能体LLM系统（MAS）的新型测试框架，通过从源代码中提取规范和行为空间，结合覆盖引导的模糊测试技术检测MAS故障，解决了传统软件测试技术无法有效测试MAS的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAS应用常因LLM智能体的非确定性行为和智能体间复杂交互而出现故障（如无限循环、工具调用失败），但传统软件测试技术无法有效检测这些故障，原因包括：缺乏LLM智能体规范、MAS行为空间庞大、正确性判断依赖语义。

Method: FLARE框架工作流程：(1) 输入MAS源代码；(2) 从智能体定义中提取规范和行为空间；(3) 基于规范构建测试预言机；(4) 进行覆盖引导的模糊测试以暴露故障；(5) 分析执行日志判断测试通过与否；(6) 生成故障报告。覆盖指标包括智能体间覆盖（inter-agent coverage）和智能体内覆盖（intra-agent coverage）。

Result: 在16个多样化的开源应用上评估，FLARE达到96.9%的智能体间覆盖率和91.1%的智能体内覆盖率，分别优于基线方法9.5%和1.0%；此外，发现了56个MAS特有的此前未知故障。

Conclusion: FLARE能有效测试多智能体LLM系统，通过规范提取和覆盖引导模糊测试相结合的方法，可检测出MAS特有的复杂故障类型，为MAS质量保障提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Multi-Agent LLM Systems (MAS) have been adopted to automate complex human workflows by breaking down tasks into subtasks. However, due to the non-deterministic behavior of LLM agents and the intricate interactions between agents, MAS applications frequently encounter failures, including infinite loops and failed tool invocations. Traditional software testing techniques are ineffective in detecting such failures due to the lack of LLM agent specification, the large behavioral space of MAS, and semantic-based correctness judgment.
  This paper presents FLARE, a novel testing framework tailored for MAS. FLARE takes the source code of MAS as input and extracts specifications and behavioral spaces from agent definitions. Based on these specifications, FLARE builds test oracles and conducts coverage-guided fuzzing to expose failures. It then analyzes execution logs to judge whether each test has passed and generates failure reports. Our evaluation on 16 diverse open-source applications demonstrates that FLARE achieves 96.9% inter-agent coverage and 91.1% intra-agent coverage, outperforming baselines by 9.5% and 1.0%. FLARE also uncovers 56 previously unknown failures unique to MAS.

</details>


### [194] [On the Role of Fault Localization Context for LLM-Based Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05481)
*Melika Sepidband,Hung Viet Pham,Hadi Hemmati*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一项关于LLM自动程序修复中故障定位（FL）上下文策略的大规模实证研究，发现文件级定位是主导因素（15-17倍提升），6-10个相关文件为最优配置，上下文并非越多越好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 故障定位是LLM自动程序修复的关键组件，但其影响尚未得到充分探索。具体而言，尚不清楚需要多少定位、上下文是否有益、以及如何获取上下文。

Method: 在500个SWE-bench Verified实例上使用GPT-5-mini进行大规模实证研究，评估61种配置，涵盖文件级、元素级和行级上下文的变化。

Result: 文件级定位是主导因素，比无文件基线提升15-17倍；约6-10个相关文件的配置修复效果最佳；元素级上下文扩展提供条件性增益；行级上下文扩展因噪声放大而频繁降低性能；LLM检索优于结构启发式方法。

Conclusion: 最有效的FL策略通常在高抽象级别结合广泛语义理解与精确行级定位。这挑战了上下文越多修复效果越好的假设，为设计LLM FL策略提供实践指导。

Abstract: Fault Localization (FL) is a key component of Large Language Model (LLM)-based Automated Program Repair (APR), yet its impact remains underexplored. In particular, it is unclear how much localization is needed, whether additional context beyond the predicted buggy location is beneficial, and how such context should be retrieved. We conduct a large-scale empirical study on 500 SWE-bench Verified instances using GPT-5-mini, evaluating 61 configurations that vary file-level, element-level, and line-level context. Our results show that more context does not consistently improve repair performance. File-level localization is the dominant factor, yielding a 15-17x improvement over a no-file baseline. Expanding file context is often associated with improved performance, with successful repairs most commonly observed in configurations with approximately 6-10 relevant files. Element-level context expansion provides conditional gains that depend strongly on the file context quality, while line-level context expansion frequently degrades performance due to noise amplification. LLM-based retrieval generally outperforms structural heuristics while using fewer files and tokens. Overall, the most effective FL context strategy typically combines a broad semantic understanding at higher abstraction levels with precise line-level localization. These findings challenge our assumption that increasing the localization context uniformly improves APR, and provide practical guidance for designing LLM-based FL strategies.

</details>


### [195] [SCOPE: A Dataset of Stereotyped Prompts for Counterfactual Fairness Assessment of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05555)
*Alessandra Parziale,Gianmario Voria,Valeria Pontillo,Andrea De Lucia,Gemma Catolino,Fabio Palomba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SCOPE是一个大规模反事实提示数据集（241,280个提示，120,640个反事实对），用于系统性研究LLM的群体敏感行为，涵盖1,438个主题、9个偏见维度和1,536个群体，包含四种交际意图类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM偏见探测资源主要依赖小规模、模板化的反事实示例或固定句子对，存在语言多样性有限、主题覆盖狭窄、缺乏对交际意图影响模型行为分析支持等问题。

Method: 设计并构建了一个大规模反事实提示对数据集，所有提示基于四种不同交际意图（问答、推荐、指示、澄清）生成，并跨越多个维度和群体。

Result: SCOPE包含241,280个提示，组织为120,640个反事实对，涵盖1,438个主题、9个偏见维度和1,536个 demographic groups。

Conclusion: 该资源为评估公平性、鲁棒性和反事实一致性提供了受控的、语义对齐的、意图感知的评估基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) now serve as the foundation for a wide range of applications, from conversational assistants to decision support tools, making the issue of fairness in their results increasingly important. Previous studies have shown that LLM outputs can shift when prompts reference different demographic groups, even when intent and semantic content remain constant. However, existing resources for probing such disparities rely primarily on small, template-based counterfactual examples or fixed sentence pairs. These benchmarks offer limited linguistic diversity, narrow topical coverage, and little support for analyzing how communicative intent affects model behavior. To address these limitations, we introduce SCOPE (Stereotype-COnditioned Prompts for Evaluation), a large-scale dataset of counterfactual prompt pairs designed to enable systematic investigation of group-sensitive behavior in LLMs. SCOPE contains 241,280 prompts organized into 120,640 counterfactual pairs, each grounded in one of 1,438 topics and spanning nine bias dimensions and 1,536 demographic groups. All prompts are generated under four distinct communicative intents: Question, Recommendation, Direction, and Clarification, ensuring broad coverage of common interaction styles. This resource provides a controlled, semantically aligned, and intent-aware basis for evaluating fairness, robustness, and counterfactual consistency.

</details>


### [196] [An Iterative Test-and-Repair Framework for Competitive Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05560)
*Lingxiao Tang,Muyang Ye,Zhaoyang Chu,Xiaoxue Ren,Zhongxin Liu,Lingfeng Bao,He Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FixAudit通过迭代的测试-修复调试循环改进代码生成，训练一个共享模型同时扮演Fixer和Auditor两个角色，在APPS、CodeContests和xCodeEval三个基准上显著超越同等规模的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前CURE框架存在两个关键局限：(1) Tester不读取候选代码，导致生成的测试难以暴露实现特定的bug；(2) Coder每次从零生成候选程序，无法学习基于失败测试修复有bug的程序。

Method: FixAudit训练一个共享模型通过四个阶段实现两个专业角色：Fixer根据失败测试修复当前候选代码，Auditor读取候选代码生成能暴露其剩余bug的新测试。框架采用迭代的定向测试-修复调试循环，而非从零生成。

Result: 在APPS、CodeContests、xCodeEval三个基准上，应用于7B模型时，FixAudit超越了同家族32B模型的平均性能；相比基于相同7B基模型的强基线，FixAudit将平均Pass@1提升35.1%至36.8%，平均AvgPassRatio提升7.1%至24.5%。

Conclusion: FixAudit通过将竞争性代码生成重新定位为迭代测试-修复过程，结合专门训练的Fixer和Auditor角色，在保持模型规模效率的同时显著提升了代码生成质量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have made remarkable progress in code generation, but competitive programming remains a challenge. Recent training-based methods have improved code generation by using reinforcement learning (RL) with execution feedback. The more recent framework CURE further incorporates test generation into the training process, jointly training a Coder and a Tester within a single model. At inference time, the Coder generates many candidate programs, and the Tester generates tests from the problem description. The candidate who passes the most of the generated tests is selected as the final answer. However, CURE has two critical limitations. First, the Tester never reads any candidate code, so its tests often fail to expose implementation-specific bugs. Second, the Coder generates every candidate from scratch and never learns to fix a buggy program based on a failed test. To address these limitations, we propose FixAudit, which approaches competitive code generation from a new perspective: starting from a single initial candidate, it iteratively improves the candidate through a targeted test-and-repair debugging cycle. The framework trains one shared model with two specialized roles through four stages: the Fixer, which repairs the current candidate based on a failing test, and the Auditor, which reads the candidate code to generate new tests that expose its remaining bugs. We evaluate FixAudit on three benchmarks: APPS, CodeContests, and xCodeEval. Applied to a 7B model, the framework surpasses the average performance of the larger 32B baseline within the same model family under the zero-shot setting. Compared to strong baselines built on the same 7B base model, FixAudit improves average Pass@1 by 35.1% to 36.8% and average AvgPassRatio by 7.1% to 24.5%.

</details>


### [197] [Bias Ahead: Sensitive Prompts as Early Warnings for Fairness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05575)
*Gianmario Voria,Martina De Lucia,Alessandra Raia,Andrea De Lucia,Gemma Catolino,Fabio Palomba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"sensitive prompts"（敏感提示）作为一种新的公平性评估抽象概念，用于识别那些虽不固有偏见但更易引发有偏见或不当响应的输入。构建了包含12,801条提示的SensY数据集，涵盖七个主题领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性基准测试主要关注刻板印象关联，无法有效预见真实世界中多样化的风险场景。研究需要新的评估范式来提前识别LLM在处理敏感内容时的潜在偏见问题。

Method: 1）提出敏感提示概念；2）构建SensY数据集（12,801条提示，含敏感/非敏感分类，涵盖7个主题领域，结合合成生成与真实用户输入）；3）对3个开源LLM进行查询；4）人工分析4,500条响应；5）开发自动化敏感提示分类器。

Result: 模型虽常提供事实正确答案，但往往忽视敏感输入的伦理、关系或情境影响；自动化分类器在敏感提示预测上表现稳健。

Conclusion: 敏感提示可作为LLM公平性风险的早期预警机制，将公平性评估从被动缓解转向预防性设计，使开发者能在部署前预见和管理偏见。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are being increasingly integrated into software systems, offering powerful capabilities but also raising concerns about fairness. Existing fairness benchmarks, however, focus on stereotype-specific associations, which limit their ability to anticipate risks in diverse, real-world contexts. In this paper, we propose sensitive prompts as a new abstraction for fairness evaluation: inputs that are not inherently biased but are more likely to elicit biased or inadequate responses due to the sensitivity of their content. We construct and release SensY, a dataset of 12,801 prompts, categorized as sensitive and non-sensitive, spanning seven thematic domains, combining synthetic generation and real user inputs. Using this dataset, we query three open-source LLMs and manually analyze 4,500 responses to evaluate their adequacy in answering sensitive prompts. Results show that while models often provide factually correct answers, they frequently fail to acknowledge the ethical, relational, or contextual implications of sensitive inputs. In addition, we develop an automated classifier for predicting prompt sensitivity, achieving robust performance on sensitive prompts. Our findings demonstrate that prompt sensitivity can serve as an effective early-warning mechanism for fairness risks in LLMs. This perspective shifts fairness assessment from reactive mitigation toward preventive design, enabling developers to anticipate and manage bias before deployment.

</details>


### [198] [An End-to-End Approach for Fixing Concurrency Bugs via SHB-Based Context Extractor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05753)
*Zhuang Li,Qiuping Yi,Keyang Xiao,Zongcheng Ji,Hongliang Liang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ConFixAgent是一个由大语言模型驱动的智能体，能够端到端地修复各类并发Bug，无需任何先验Bug信息即可工作，通过静态 Happens-Before 图提取Bug相关上下文，显著优于现有最先进工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多核处理器和分布式系统的兴起，并发编程变得必不可少但极具挑战性，主要原因是线程执行的非确定性。手动修复并发Bug既耗时又容易出错。现有工具大多依赖Bug相关信息已可获取的假设，这在实际场景中往往不切实际。

Method: 提出一种专为并发Bug修复设计的上下文提取方法，利用静态 Happens-Before 图识别与Bug相关的代码区域，使系统能够在无需任何先验Bug信息的情况下端到端运行。

Result: ConFixAgent在多种类型的并发Bug修复任务中显著优于现有最先进工具，其上下文提取方法大幅提升了LLM生成修复方案的准确性。

Conclusion: 通过静态 Happens-Before 图引导的上下文提取方法，ConFixAgent有效解决了端到端并发Bug自动修复的核心挑战，为实际场景中的并发Bug修复提供了可行方案。

Abstract: With the rise of multi-core processors and distributed systems, concurrent programming has become essential yet challenging, primarily due to the non-deterministic nature of thread execution. Manually addressing concurrency bugs is time-consuming and error-prone. Automated Program Repair techniques provide a promising solution. However, developing an end-to-end concurrency bug repair tool is particularly challenging. Most existing tools rely on the assumption that bug-related information is readily available or that concurrency bug contexts are ideally extracted, which is often impractical in real-world scenarios. This paper introduces ConFixAgent, an LLM-driven agent capable of fixing various types of concurrency bugs in an end-to-end manner, eliminating the need for any prior bug-related information. Specifically, we propose a novel context extraction approach designed for concurrency bug repair, utilizing Static Happens-Before Graphs to identify bug-relevant sections.We implemented ConFixAgent and evaluated it across multiple benchmark sets. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that ConFixAgent significantly outperforms state-of-the-art tools in addressing diverse types of concurrency bugs, with its context extraction method markedly enhancing the accuracy of LLM-generated repair solutions.

</details>


### [199] [CAKE: Cloud Architecture Knowledge Evaluation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05755)
*Tim Lukas Adam,Phongsakon Mark Konrad,Riccardo Terrenzi,Florian Girardo Lukas,Rahime Yilmaz,Krzysztof Sierszecki,Serkan Ayvaz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了CAKE基准，用于评估LLM对云原生软件架构的理解能力，包含188个专家验证问题。实验表明模型规模超过3B参数后MCQ性能趋于平稳，而自由响应任务仍能持续区分模型能力，评估格式的选择显著影响对LLM架构知识的测量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏评估LLM对云原生软件架构真实理解能力的基准测试，现有评估无法全面衡量LLM作为软件架构协作者的效能。

Method: 使用22种模型配置(0.5B-70B参数)进行评估，采用选择题三次投票和自由响应LLM评判两种评分方式，涵盖Bloom修订分类法的四个认知层级和五个云原生主题。

Result: 1) MCQ准确率在3B参数以上趋于平稳，最佳模型达99.2%；2) 自由响应分数在各认知层级稳定增长；3) 两种格式捕获不同知识维度，MCQ趋近天花板而自由响应仍能区分模型；4) 推理增强提升自由响应质量，工具增强对小型模型有负面影响。

Conclusion: CAKE benchmark揭示了评估格式对LLM架构知识测量的根本性影响，推理增强(+think)提升自由响应质量，工具增强(+tool)对小型模型有负面影响。

Abstract: In today's software architecture, large language models (LLMs) serve as software architecture co-pilots. However, no benchmark currently exists to evaluate large language models' actual understanding of cloud-native software architecture. For this reason we present a benchmark called CAKE, which consists of 188 expert-validated questions covering four cognitive levels of Bloom's revised taxonomy -- recall, analyze, design, and implement -- and five cloud-native topics. Evaluation is conducted on 22 model configurations (0.5B--70B parameters) across four LLM families, using three-run majority voting for multiple-choice questions (MCQs) and LLM-as-a-judge scoring for free-responses (FR). Based on this evaluation, four notable findings were identified. First, MCQ accuracy plateaus above 3B parameters, with the best model reaching 99.2\%. Second, free-response scores scale steadily across all cognitive levels. Third, the two formats capture different facets of knowledge, as the MCQ accuracy approaches a ceiling while free-responses continue to differentiate models. Finally, reasoning augmentation (+think) improves free-response quality, while tool augmentation (+tool) degrades performance for small models. These results suggest that the evaluation format fundamentally shapes how we measure architectural knowledge in LLMs.

</details>


### [200] [An Empirical Study of Perceptions of General LLMs and Multimodal LLMs on Hugging Face](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05782)
*Yujian Liu,Xiao Yu,Jacky Keung,Xing Hu,Xin Xia,Xiaoxue Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究在Hugging Face平台上收集662条讨论线程（来自38个模型），分析用户对大语言模型（GLLM和MLLM）的真实感知，发现访问壁垒、生成质量、部署复杂性是主要痛点，为改进LLM生态系统提供可行建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究依赖问卷或单一平台数据（如Reddit/GitHub），存在预设问题限制或过度聚焦故障调试的问题，无法全面反映用户在实践中的多样化、经验驱动的跨模型视角。

Method: 在Hugging Face平台手动标注662条讨论线程，涵盖21个GLLM和17个MLLM，构建三级分类体系进行系统性用户关切分析。

Result: 用户最突出的关切包括：LLM访问壁垒、生成质量、部署与调用复杂性；次要问题涉及文档限制和资源约束。

Conclusion: 研究结果可为LLM生态系统的改进提供可操作的实践建议。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have rapidly evolved from general-purpose systems to multimodal models capable of processing text, images, and audio. As both general-purpose LLMs (GLLMs) and multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) gain widespread adoption, understanding user perceptions in real-world settings becomes increasingly important. However, existing studies often rely on surveys or platform-specific data (e.g., Reddit or GitHub issues), which either constrain user feedback through predefined questions or overemphasize failure-driven, debugging-oriented discussions, thus failing to capture diverse, experience-driven, and cross-model user perspectives in practice. To address this issue, we conduct an empirical study of user discussions on Hugging Face, a major model hub with diverse models and active communities. We collect and manually annotate 662 discussion threads from 38 representative models (21 GLLMs and 17 MLLMs), and develop a three-level taxonomy to systematically characterize user concerns. Our analysis reveals that LLM access barriers, generation quality, and deployment and invocation complexity are the most prominent concerns, alongside issues such as documentation limitations and resource constraints. Based on these findings, we derive actionable implications for improving LLM ecosystem.

</details>


### [201] [Reinforcement Learning with Negative Tests as Completeness Signal for Formal Specification Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05820)
*Zhechong Huang,Zhao Zhang,Zeyu Sun,Huifeng Sun,Yingfei Xiong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SpecRL使用强化学习框架在Dafny中合成程序规范，通过生成"负测试用例"（程序无法产生的输入-输出对）来衡量规范完整性，从而改进规范强度和验证成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范综合任务对于模块化推理和代码库复用验证至关重要，但现有验证器反馈存在根本性缺陷：验证通过只能证明声音性，无法区分弱规范和强规范，缺乏规范完整性的细粒度信号。

Method: 提出SpecRL框架，包含自包含流水线：生成负测试用例（即程序无法产生的输入-输出对），并利用候选规范拒绝这些负测试的比例作为规范完整性的信号，集成到RL训练的奖励中。实验覆盖四种模型规模。

Result: SpecRL在规范强度和验证成功率上均优于SFT和二元规范强度奖励的RL方法，能够泛化到分布外基准测试，且相比更大的通用LLM仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 负测试用例可作为规范完整性的有效信号；SpecRL为程序规范综合提供了一种基于细粒度反馈的强化学习解决方案。

Abstract: The specification synthesis task aims to automatically generate specifications, together with any necessary auxiliary verification annotations, for existing programs. This task is important because such specifications serve as behavioral contracts that support modular reasoning and reusable verification across a codebase. At the same time, it remains challenging because verifier-only feedback is fundamentally incomplete: passing verification establishes soundness, but cannot distinguish weak specifications from strong ones. What is missing is a fine-grained signal for specification completeness.
  We present SpecRL, a reinforcement learning framework for specification synthesis in Dafny. SpecRL introduces a self-contained pipeline that generates negative tests, i.e., input-output pairs that can never be produced by the program. We use the fraction of these negative tests rejected by a candidate specification as a signal of specification completeness, which is integrated into the reward for RL training. Experiments across four model sizes show that SpecRL improves both specification strength and verification success over SFT and RL with a binary specification-strength reward, generalizes to an out-of-distribution benchmark, and remains competitive on that unseen benchmark compared to much larger general-purpose LLMs.

</details>


### [202] [Proof of Concept as a First-Class Architectural Decision Instrument](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05835)
*Bruno Fernando Antognolli,Fabio Petrillo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统文献综述研究软件工程中的概念验证（PoC），发现PoC虽然应用广泛但在学术和工业界都缺乏清晰定义和方法论。研究提出了精炼的PoC定义和轻量级三阶段框架（规划、执行、决策），并识别出"未记录的建筑实验"反模式，强调将PoC提升为一级架构决策工具的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念验证（PoC）在软件工程中广泛采用，但无论在学术界还是工业界都存在概念定义模糊、方法论不够系统的问题，其定义和实施方法缺乏清晰性和一致性。

Method: 对学术文献和灰色文献进行系统综述，识别PoC的关键特征、相关流程，并通过综合不同来源的见解来构建框架。

Result: 识别了PoC的关键特征和流程；发现学术文献描述了PoC结果但很少涉及其过程；提出了精炼的PoC定义和轻量级三阶段框架（规划、执行、决策）；引入了"未记录的建筑实验"反模式，当PoC影响高影响力架构决策却未留下持久架构知识时会产生此反模式。

Conclusion: 将PoC提升为一级架构决策工具可以提高决策质量、增强可追溯性，并支持架构实践中的更系统性学习。

Abstract: Proofs of Concept (PoCs) are widely adopted practices in software engineering. Despite their relevance, PoCs remain conceptually underdefined and methodologically ad hoc in both research and industry, with definitions and implementation approaches that often lack clarity and consistency. This paper investigates the concept of PoCs with two complementary goals: (1) to provide a refined definition and astructured framework for PoC development grounded in a systematic review of academic and grey literature; and (2) to position PoCs as first-class architectural decision instruments rather than informal experiments or disposable artifacts. Through a systematic review of academic and grey literature we identify the key characteristics, processes, associated with PoCs and expose a significant gap the academic literature describes PoC outcomes but rarely its process. By synthesizing insights from diverse sources we propose a refined definition and a lightweight, three-phase framework (planning, execution, decision-making) that encompasses technical validation and explicit decision traceability. We also introduce the Undocumented Architectural Experiment anti-pattern, arising when PoCs influence high-impact architectural decisions without leaving durable architectural knowledge. We argue that elevating PoCs to first-class status improves decision quality, enhances traceability, and supports more systematic learning in architectural practice.

</details>


### [203] [Evolutionary Optimization of AI-Collapsed Software Development Stacks: Labor Tipping Points and Workforce Realignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05948)
*Matthew H. Kilbane*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个量化框架，通过形式化基线和AI崩溃劳动力模型，结合NSGAII多目标进化优化，确定软件团队中安全削减人力的"临界点"，实现成本降低同时保持质量和稳定工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI工具（如代码生成助手）在软件开发中的应用日益广泛，企业面临如何科学平衡人力成本削减与项目质量、团队稳定性的挑战，缺乏系统性的量化决策框架。

Method: 1) 形式化基线劳动力模型和AI崩溃劳动力模型；2) 推导安全削减人力的临界点方程；3) 将临界点约束嵌入NSGAII多目标进化优化框架，同时优化成本、质量和工作负载稳定性。

Result: NSGAII实验揭示了可复现的、分阶段的自动化策略，能够在保持质量和工作负载稳定性的前提下降低人力成本。

Conclusion: 该框架为AI辅助软件开发的劳动力配置决策提供了可量化的科学依据，具有向其他劳动类别迁移的通用性。

Abstract: This paper presents a quantitative framework for optimizing human AI workforce allocation in software development, translatable to other labor categories. I formalize baseline and AI-collapsed labor models, derive tipping point equations for safe headcount reduction, and embed them in a multi objective evolutionary optimization setup. NSGAII experiments reveal reproducible, phase specific automation strategies that reduce cost while maintaining quality and stable workloads.

</details>


### [204] [QiMeng-PRepair: Precise Code Repair via Edit-Aware Reward Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05963)
*Changxin Ke,Rui Zhang,Jiaming Guo,Yuanbo Wen,Li Ding,Shuo Wang,Xuyuan Zhu,Xiong Peng,Di Huang,Zidong Du,Xing Hu,Qi Guo,Yunji Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在程序修复中的"过度编辑"问题，提出精确修复任务和PRepair框架，通过自破坏生成多样化buggy程序，并使用编辑感知的组相对策略优化训练模型，实现更精确的代码修复，修复精度提升31.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在程序修复中常出现过度编辑问题，过多的代码修改覆盖正确代码，阻碍bug定位，导致修复效率低下。

Method: 提出精确修复任务概念；构建PRepair框架包含两部分：(1) Self-Breaking通过受控bug注入和min-max采样生成多样化buggy程序；(2) Self-Repairing使用编辑感知组相对策略优化(EA-GRPO)训练，编辑感知奖励鼓励最小但正确的编辑。

Result: 在fix_1@1指标下修复精度提升31.4%；结合推测编辑显著提高解码吞吐量。

Conclusion: PRepair有效缓解过度编辑问题，在修复精度和效率上均表现优异，为精确实用的代码修复提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong program repair performance but often suffer from over-editing, where excessive modifications overwrite correct code and hinder bug localization. We systematically quantify its impact and introduce precise repair task, which maximizes reuse of correct code while fixing only buggy parts. Building on this insight, we propose PRepair, a framework that mitigates over-editing and improves repair accuracy. PRepair has two components: Self-Breaking, which generates diverse buggy programs via controlled bug injection and min-max sampling, and Self-Repairing, which trains models with Edit-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization (EA-GRPO) using an edit-aware reward to encourage minimal yet correct edits. Experiments show that PRepair improves repair precision by up to 31.4% under $\mathrm{fix}_1@1$, a metric that jointly considers repair correctness and extent, and significantly increases decoding throughput when combined with speculative editing, demonstrating its potential for precise and practical code repair.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [A Theory-guided Weighted $L^2$ Loss for solving the BGK model via Physics-informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04971)
*Gyounghun Ko,Sung-Jun Son,Seung Yeon Cho,Myeong-Su Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对标准PINN的L²损失函数在BGK模型中无法保证宏观矩量精度的根本缺陷，提出了一种速度加权L²损失函数，并通过稳定性分析证明了该方法能保证近似解收敛，数值实验验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准L²损失函数在求解Bhatnagar-Gross-Krook（BGK）模型时存在根本性不足：仅最小化标准损失无法保证对宏观矩量的准确预测，导致近似解无法捕获真实物理解。

Method: 提出速度加权L²损失函数，通过对高速区域赋予更大权重来有效惩罚误差；建立所提方法的稳定性估计，理论证明最小化加权损失可保证近似解收敛。

Result: 数值实验表明，采用该加权PINN损失函数在多个基准测试中相比标准方法具有更高的精度和更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 速度加权策略可有效解决标准PINN在BGK模型中的失效问题，为该类问题提供了一种理论上可靠且实践上有效的解决方案。

Abstract: While Physics-Informed Neural Networks offer a promising framework for solving partial differential equations, the standard $L^2$ loss formulation is fundamentally insufficient when applied to the Bhatnagar-Gross-Krook (BGK) model. Specifically, simply minimizing the standard loss does not guarantee accurate predictions of the macroscopic moments, causing the approximate solutions to fail in capturing the true physical solution. To overcome this limitation, we introduce a velocity-weighted $L^2$ loss function designed to effectively penalize errors in the high-velocity regions. By establishing a stability estimate for the proposed approach, we shows that minimizing the proposed weighted loss guarantees the convergence of the approximate solution. Also, numerical experiments demonstrate that employing this weighted PINN loss leads to superior accuracy and robustness across various benchmarks compared to the standard approach.

</details>


### [206] [Territory Paint Wars: Diagnosing and Mitigating Failure Modes in Competitive Multi-Agent PPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04983)
*Diyansha Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Territory Paint Wars环境用于系统研究PPO在自博弈中的失败模式，识别出5个实现级失败因素，并发现"竞争性过拟合"这一新现象，通过20%随机对手混合训练可有效缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自博弈(self-play)是多智能体强化学习中的核心训练范式，但PPO在竞争性场景下的失败模式缺乏系统性研究，特别是为何智能体在自我对局中表现良好却泛化能力崩溃的问题未被充分理解。

Method: 在Unity中实现对称零和博弈环境Territory Paint Wars，使用控制变量法进行消融实验，系统测试5种潜在失败因素：奖励尺度不平衡、缺失终止信号、无效的长视野信用分配、未归一化观测、错误胜利检测。

Result: 识别出5个关键实现级失败模式；发现竞争性过拟合现象——自博弈胜率稳定在50%时，泛化胜率从73.5%崩溃至21.6%；通过20%随机对手混合训练可将泛化性能恢复至77.1%。

Conclusion: 竞争性过拟合是自博弈的关键隐患，标准自博弈指标无法检测该问题；对手混合(opponent mixing)是简单有效的干预手段，无需人口基训练或额外基础设施。

Abstract: We present Territory Paint Wars, a minimal competitive multi-agent reinforcement learning environment implemented in Unity, and use it to systematically investigate failure modes of Proximal Policy Optimisation (PPO) under self-play. A first agent trained for $84{,}000$ episodes achieves only $26.8\%$ win rate against a uniformly-random opponent in a symmetric zero-sum game. Through controlled ablations we identify five implementation-level failure modes -- reward-scale imbalance, missing terminal signal, ineffective long-horizon credit assignment, unnormalised observations, and incorrect win detection -- each of which contributes critically to this failure in this setting.
  After correcting these issues, we uncover a distinct emergent pathology: competitive overfitting, where co-adapting agents maintain stable self-play performance while generalisation win rate collapses from $73.5\%$ to $21.6\%$. Critically, this failure is undetectable via standard self-play metrics: both agents co-adapt equally, so the self-play win rate remains near $50\%$ throughout the collapse.
  We propose a minimal intervention -- opponent mixing, where $20\%$ of training episodes substitute a fixed uniformly-random policy for the co-adaptive opponent -- which mitigates competitive overfitting and restores generalisation to $77.1\%$ ($\pm 12.6\%$, $10$ seeds) without population-based training or additional infrastructure. We open-source Territory Paint Wars to provide a reproducible benchmark for studying competitive MARL failure modes.

</details>


### [207] [Enhancing sample efficiency in reinforcement-learning-based flow control: replacing the critic with an adaptive reduced-order model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04986)
*Zesheng Yao,Zhen-Hua Wan,Canjun Yang,Qingchao Xia,Mengqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自适应降阶模型（ROM）的深度强化学习框架，用于主动流动控制，通过结合线性动力学系统与神经常微分方程（NODE）来估计梯度信息，从而克服传统无模型DRL方法样本效率低的问题，并在两个典型流动控制问题上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无模型深度强化学习（DRL）方法存在样本效率差的问题，在流动控制等实际应用中导致数据采集成本高、训练时间长，限制了其工程应用。

Method: 提出一种自适应ROM-DRL框架：(1) 采用线性动力学系统+NODE组合结构估计流动非线性；(2) 线性部分通过算子推断识别参数；(3) NODE通过梯度优化数据驱动训练；(4) 控制过程中持续用新数据更新ROM；(5) 通过ROM的可微分仿真优化控制器。

Result: 在Blasius边界层流动中，实现单回合系统辨识与控制器优化，性能优于传统线性设计且媲美数据需求最少的DRL方法；在方柱绕流中，以显著更少的探索数据实现更优的减阻效果。

Conclusion: 所提ROM-DRL框架有效提升了DRL的样本效率，为设计更高效的主动流动控制器奠定了基础。

Abstract: Model-free deep reinforcement learning (DRL) methods suffer from poor sample efficiency. To overcome this limitation, this work introduces an adaptive reduced-order-model (ROM)-based reinforcement learning framework for active flow control. In contrast to conventional actor--critic architectures, the proposed approach leverages a ROM to estimate the gradient information required for controller optimization. The design of the ROM structure incorporates physical insights. The ROM integrates a linear dynamical system and a neural ordinary differential equation (NODE) for estimating the nonlinearity in the flow. The parameters of the linear component are identified via operator inference, while the NODE is trained in a data-driven manner using gradient-based optimization. During controller--environment interactions, the ROM is continuously updated with newly collected data, enabling adaptive refinement of the model. The controller is then optimized through differentiable simulation of the ROM. The proposed ROM-based DRL framework is validated on two canonical flow control problems: Blasius boundary layer flow and flow past a square cylinder. For the Blasius boundary layer, the proposed method effectively reduces to a single-episode system identification and controller optimization process, yet it yields controllers that outperform traditional linear designs and achieve performance comparable to DRL approaches with minimal data. For the flow past a square cylinder, the proposed method achieves superior drag reduction with significantly fewer exploration data compared with DRL approaches. The work addresses a key component of model-free DRL control algorithms and lays the foundation for designing more sample-efficient DRL-based active flow controllers.

</details>


### [208] [Cactus: Accelerating Auto-Regressive Decoding with Constrained Acceptance Speculative Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04987)
*Yongchang Hao,Lili Mou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Cactus方法，通过约束优化框架改进投机采样算法，在控制与验证器分布偏离程度的同时提高接受率，从而加速大语言模型的自回归解码过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的投机采样(SpS)严格强制生成分布与验证器LLM分布完全匹配，这一限制过于严格。实际上，允许与验证器分布存在适度偏差（如使用top-k或温度采样）也是可接受的。典型的接受采样(TAS)虽然通过熵启发式方法提高了接受率，但会扭曲验证器分布，可能在验证器编码关键信息时导致输出质量下降。

Method: 本文首先通过约束优化的视角对投机采样算法进行形式化建模。基于该理论框架，提出Cactus（约束接受投机采样）方法，该方法能够保证对验证器分布的受控偏离，同时提高接受率。

Result: 实验结果在多种基准测试上验证了该方法的有效性，证明了在保持输出质量的同时成功提升了token接受率。

Conclusion: Cactus通过约束优化框架成功平衡了接受率提升与分布保真度之间的关系，为投机采样提供了一种更具理论基础且实用的改进方案。

Abstract: Speculative sampling (SpS) has been successful in accelerating the decoding throughput of auto-regressive large language models by leveraging smaller draft models. SpS strictly enforces the generated distribution to match that of the verifier LLM. This is unnecessarily restrictive as slight variations of the verifier's distribution, such as sampling with top-$k$ or temperature, would also be acceptable. Typical acceptance sampling (TAS) alleviates this issue by accepting more tokens using entropy-based heuristics. However, this approach distorts the verifier distribution, potentially degrading output quality when the verifier encodes critical information. In this work, we formalize the speculative sampling algorithm through the lens of constrained optimization. Based on this formulation, we propose Cactus (constrained acceptance speculative sampling), a method that guarantees controlled divergence from the verifier distribution and increasing acceptance rates. Empirical results across a wide range of benchmarks confirm the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [209] [Learning-Based Multi-Criteria Decision Making Model for Sawmill Location Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04996)
*Mahid Ahmed,Ali Dogru,Chaoyang Zhang,Chao Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出一种基于学习的MCDM框架(LB-MCDM)，结合机器学习与GIS空间分析用于锯木厂选址。通过密西西比州案例验证，随机森林分类器表现最佳，发现供需比是影响选址的最重要因素，10-11%的区域适合建厂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锯木厂战略选址对木材供应链的效率、盈利能力和可持续性至关重要。传统选址方法存在主观性强、数据驱动不足、可复制性差等问题。

Method: 提出LB-MCDM框架，集成5种机器学习算法(RF、SVC、XGBoost、LR、KNN)与GIS空间分析；采用SHAP方法解释特征重要性。

Result: 随机森林分类器性能最优；供需比(反映当地市场竞争动态)是最具影响力的因素，其次是道路、铁路和城市区域距离；SHAP有效量化了各标准的相对重要性。

Conclusion: LB-MCDM框架提供了一种数据驱动、无偏且可复制的选址评估方法；密西西比州10-11%的区域高度适合锯木厂选址。

Abstract: Strategically locating a sawmill is vital for enhancing the efficiency, profitability, and sustainability of timber supply chains. Our study proposes a Learning-Based Multi-Criteria Decision-Making (LB-MCDM) framework that integrates machine learning (ML) with GIS-based spatial location analysis via MCDM. The proposed framework provides a data-driven, unbiased, and replicable approach to assessing site suitability. We demonstrate the utility of the proposed model through a case study in Mississippi (MS). We apply five ML algorithms (Random Forest Classifier, Support Vector Classifier, XGBoost Classifier, Logistic Regression, and K-Nearest Neighbors Classifier) to identify the most suitable sawmill locations in Mississippi. Among these models, the Random Forest Classifier achieved the highest performance. We use the SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations) technique to determine the relative importance of each criterion, revealing the Supply-Demand Ratio, a composite feature that reflects local market competition dynamics, as the most influential factor, followed by Road, Rail Line and Urban Area Distance. The validation of suitability maps generated by our LB-MCDM model suggests that 10-11% of the MS landscape is highly suitable for sawmill location.

</details>


### [210] [El Nino Prediction Based on Weather Forecast and Geographical Time-series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04998)
*Viet Trinh,Ha-Vy Luu,Quoc-Khiem Nguyen-Pham,Hung Tong,Thanh-Huyen Tran,Hoai-Nam Nguyen Dang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合CNN和LSTM的混合深度学习框架，通过整合实时全球天气预报数据、海表温度异常、次表层海洋热含量和大气压等多源数据，提升厄尔尼诺事件的预测精度和预见期，为全球气候、经济和社会影响提供更好的预警能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统厄尔尼诺预测模型主要依赖海洋和大气指数，存在数据粒度不足、动态交互捕捉不充分的问题，需要更全面、更高分辨率的预测方法来提升预测准确性和延长预见期，从而更有效地应对厄尔尼诺事件的全球影响。

Method: 提出了一个混合深度学习框架，集成CNN用于空间特征提取、LSTM用于时间依赖建模；输入数据包括实时全球天气预报数据及异常值、次表层海洋热含量和大气压数据，涵盖多种时间和空间分辨率。

Result: 提出了一个可整合多源气象与地理数据的厄尔尼诺预测框架，理论上能够识别厄尔尼诺事件的复杂前兆信号和演变规律。

Conclusion: 该框架结合空间特征提取与时间序列建模能力，有望突破传统模型的局限，为厄尔尼诺事件提供更精准、更具提前量的预测支持。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel framework for enhancing the prediction accuracy and lead time of El Niño events, crucial for mitigating their global climatic, economic, and societal impacts. Traditional prediction models often rely on oceanic and atmospheric indices, which may lack the granularity or dynamic interplay captured by comprehensive meteorological and geographical datasets. Our framework integrates real-time global weather forecast data with anomalies, subsurface ocean heat content, and atmospheric pressure across various temporal and spatial resolutions. Leveraging a hybrid deep learning architecture that combines a Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) for spatial feature extraction and a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network for temporal dependency modeling, the framework aims to identify complex precursors and evolving patterns of El Niño events.

</details>


### [211] [PRIME: Prototype-Driven Multimodal Pretraining for Cancer Prognosis with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04999)
*Kai Yu,Shuang Zhou,Yiran Song,Zaifu Zhan,Jie Peng,Kaixiong Zhou,Tianlong Chen,Feng Xie,Meng Wang,Huazhu Fu,Mingquan Lin,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRIME是一种面向缺失感知的多模态自监督预训练框架，用于癌症预后预测。该方法通过统一token空间映射、共享原型记忆库和结构化缺失增强，在32种癌症类型、32,000+患者的大规模无标签数据上预训练，在5个独立验证队列的总体生存预测、3年死亡率和3年复发分类三项任务上取得最优宏平均性能，同时具备测试时缺失鲁棒性和参数高效适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床队列数据高度碎片化，多数患者缺失部分模态（如基因表达或病理报告不完整），导致现有全模态监督融合和大规模多模态预训练方法无法直接应用。如何从部分观察的异构队列中学习到可迁移的鲁棒表征，是精准肿瘤学面临的核心挑战。

Method: PRIME包含三个核心设计：①异构模态嵌入统一映射到共享token空间；②共享原型记忆库通过患者级共识检索实现潜在语义填补（Latent-space semantic imputation），避免重建原始信号；③双重预训练目标——结构化缺失增强下的模态间对齐（inter-modality alignment）和融合后一致性（post-fusion consistency），联合优化使表征在任意模态子集下均具预测性。

Result: 在TCGA 32种癌症类型上进行无标签预训练，下游在5个独立队列的5折交叉验证评估显示：总体生存预测C-index达0.653、3年死亡率AUROC达0.689、3年复发AUROC达0.637，三项任务宏平均性能均优于所有对比方法；同时在测试时模态缺失条件下展现出强鲁棒性，并支持参数高效和标签高效适应。

Conclusion: 缺失感知的多模态自监督预训练是处理碎片化临床数据场景下肿瘤预后建模的实用策略，PRIME通过统一token空间和潜在空间语义填补实现了结构化对齐，为大规模临床级多模态学习提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: Multimodal self-supervised pretraining offers a promising route to cancer prognosis by integrating histopathology whole-slide images, gene expression, and pathology reports, yet most existing approaches require fully paired and complete inputs. In practice, clinical cohorts are fragmented and often miss one or more modalities, limiting both supervised fusion and scalable multimodal pretraining. We propose PRIME, a missing-aware multimodal self-supervised pretraining framework that learns robust and transferable representations from partially observed cohorts. PRIME maps heterogeneous modality embeddings into a unified token space and introduces a shared prototype memory bank for latent-space semantic imputation via patient-level consensus retrieval, producing structurally aligned tokens without reconstructing raw signals. Two complementary pretraining objectives: inter-modality alignment and post-fusion consistency under structured missingness augmentation, jointly learn representations that remain predictive under arbitrary modality subsets. We evaluate PRIME on The Cancer Genome Atlas with label-free pretraining on 32 cancer types and downstream 5-fold evaluation on five cohorts across overall survival prediction, 3-year mortality classification, and 3-year recurrence classification. PRIME achieves the best macro-average performance among all compared methods, reaching 0.653 C-index, 0.689 AUROC, and 0.637 AUROC on the three tasks, respectively, while improving robustness under test-time missingness and supporting parameter-efficient and label-efficient adaptation. These results support missing-aware multimodal pretraining as a practical strategy for prognosis modeling in fragmented clinical data settings.

</details>


### [212] [Learning $\mathsf{AC}^0$ Under Graphical Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06109)
*Gautam Chandrasekaran,Jason Gaitonde,Ankur Moitra,Arsen Vasilyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Linial-Mansour-Nisan的开创性工作（1993年的低度算法）从均匀分布扩展到更自然的图形模型分布，在多项式增长且具有强空间混合特性的图形模型上给出了拟多项式时间学习AC^0的算法。核心创新在于通过新的采样算法绕过传统傅里叶分析的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习理论依赖于乘积结构假设（在实际场景中难以成立），作者希望将LMN算法的理论保证扩展到更真实的关联分布场景，这是一个长期存在的挑战。

Method: 1. 在具有强空间混合特性的图形模型上设计算法；2. 开发新的采样算法，将均匀分布下的低度多项式逼近结论迁移到图形模型分布；3. 方法具有通用性，可扩展至其他函数类。

Result: 在多项式增长且具有强空间混合的图形模型上实现了拟多项式时间学习AC^0；方法可推广至单调函数和半空间等其他函数类。

Conclusion: 成功突破传统傅里叶分析方法的局限，证明了低度算法在非乘积分布下的有效性，为计算学习理论提供了更贴近实际的理论框架。

Abstract: In a landmark result, Linial, Mansour and Nisan (J. ACM 1993) gave a quasipolynomial-time algorithm for learning constant-depth circuits given labeled i.i.d. samples under the uniform distribution. Their work has had a deep and lasting legacy in computational learning theory, in particular introducing the $\textit{low-degree algorithm}$. However, an important critique of many results and techniques in the area is the reliance on product structure, which is unlikely to hold in realistic settings. Obtaining similar learning guarantees for more natural correlated distributions has been a longstanding challenge in the field.
  In particular, we give quasipolynomial-time algorithms for learning $\mathsf{AC}^0$ substantially beyond the product setting, when the inputs come from any graphical model with polynomial growth that exhibits strong spatial mixing. The main technical challenge is in giving a workaround to Fourier analysis, which we do by showing how new sampling algorithms allow us to transfer statements about low-degree polynomial approximation under the uniform setting to graphical models. Our approach is general enough to extend to other well-studied function classes, like monotone functions and halfspaces.

</details>


### [213] [Blind-Spot Mass: A Good-Turing Framework for Quantifying Deployment Coverage Risk in Machine Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05057)
*Biplab Pal,Santanu Bhattacharya,Madanjit Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"盲点质量"(Blind-spot mass)指标，基于Good-Turing未观测物种估计理论，量化机器学习模型在部署环境中的覆盖风险。该指标通过设定经验支持阈值τ，估算落在低支持区域的概率质量，帮助识别模型虽在标准测试集表现良好、但在长尾分布区域可能失效的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代ML系统面临重尾分布的运营状态分布，导致有效但罕见的状态在有限训练/评估数据中结构性地缺乏支持。这种"覆盖盲区"使得模型在标准测试集上表现准确，但在部署空间的大部分区域可能不可靠。需要一个原则性的指标来量化这种部署覆盖风险。

Method: 提出盲点质量B_n(τ)部署指标，使用Good-Turing未见物种估计法计算经验支持低于阈值τ的状态所对应的总概率质量。进一步推导出覆盖 imposed 精度上限，将整体性能分解为已支持分量和盲点分量，区分容量限制与数据限制。在两个结构独立领域验证：可穿戴人类活动识别(HAR)和MIMIC-IV临床数据库(275例入院)。

Result: 在HAR和MIMIC-IV两个跨领域(模态、特征空间、标签空间、应用均不同)中，盲点质量曲线收敛至相同结论：τ=5时盲点质量达95%。这表明盲点质量是一个通用的ML方法论，而非应用特定的 artifact。盲点分解可识别哪些活动或临床状态主导风险。

Conclusion: 盲点质量是一个通用且可复制的ML方法论，适用于跨领域量化组合覆盖风险。为工业界提供可操作的指导：针对性数据收集、归一化/重归一化、以及基于物理或领域知识的约束，以实现更安全的部署。

Abstract: Blind-spot mass is a Good-Turing framework for quantifying deployment coverage risk in machine learning. In modern ML systems, operational state distributions are often heavy-tailed, implying that a long tail of valid but rare states is structurally under-supported in finite training and evaluation data. This creates a form of 'coverage blindness': models can appear accurate on standard test sets yet remain unreliable across large regions of the deployment state space.
  We propose blind-spot mass B_n(tau), a deployment metric estimating the total probability mass assigned to states whose empirical support falls below a threshold tau. B_n(tau) is computed using Good-Turing unseen-species estimation and yields a principled estimate of how much of the operational distribution lies in reliability-critical, under-supported regimes. We further derive a coverage-imposed accuracy ceiling, decomposing overall performance into supported and blind components and separating capacity limits from data limits.
  We validate the framework in wearable human activity recognition (HAR) using wrist-worn inertial data. We then replicate the same analysis in the MIMIC-IV hospital database with 275 admissions, where the blind-spot mass curve converges to the same 95% at tau = 5 across clinical state abstractions. This replication across structurally independent domains - differing in modality, feature space, label space, and application - shows that blind-spot mass is a general ML methodology for quantifying combinatorial coverage risk, not an application-specific artifact.
  Blind-spot decomposition identifies which activities or clinical regimes dominate risk, providing actionable guidance for industrial practitioners on targeted data collection, normalization/renormalization, and physics- or domain-informed constraints for safer deployment.

</details>


### [214] [Dynamic Linear Coregionalization for Realistic Synthetic Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05064)
*Annita Vapsi,Penghang Liu,Saheed Obitayo,Aakriti,Manoj Cherukumalli,Prathamesh Patil,Amit Varshney,Nicolas Marchesotti,Elizabeth Fons,Vamsi K. Potluru,Manuela Veloso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DynLMC提出一种动态线性模型用于生成具有时变相关性和通道间滞后结构的多变量时间序列合成数据，可显著提升基础模型在零样本预测任务中的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列合成数据生成方法大多假设静态相关性，缺乏真实的通道间依赖建模能力，导致生成数据无法有效支撑基础模型的时间序列预训练。

Method: 提出DynLMC（Dynamic Linear Model of Coregionalization），该模型通过引入时变、状态切换的相关性结构以及跨通道滞后特征，实现对真实数据相关性动态特征的高保真捕捉。

Result: 在九个基准数据集上，使用DynLMC生成数据进行微调的三种基础模型均实现了一致的零样本预测性能提升。

Conclusion: 动态通道间相关性的建模能够有效增强时间序列基础模型的迁移能力，凸显了数据-centric预训练策略的重要性。

Abstract: Synthetic data is essential for training foundation models for time series (FMTS), but most generators assume static correlations, and are typically missing realistic inter-channel dependencies. We introduce DynLMC, a Dynamic Linear Model of Coregionalization, that incorporates time-varying, regime-switching correlations and cross-channel lag structures. Our approach produces synthetic multivariate time series with correlation dynamics that closely resemble real data. Fine-tuning three foundational models on DynLMC-generated data yields consistent zero-shot forecasting improvements across nine benchmarks. Our results demonstrate that modeling dynamic inter-channel correlations enhances FMTS transferability, highlighting the importance of data-centric pretraining.

</details>


### [215] [Towards Scaling Law Analysis For Spatiotemporal Weather Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05068)
*Alexander Kiefer,Prasanna Balaprakash,Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将神经缩放定律分析从NLP/CV领域扩展到自回归天气预测，针对长时滚动预测和逐通道指标进行系统性研究。研究发现预测误差在通道间和预测时长上存在显著异质性，汇总缩放可能表现良好但许多通道在后期会退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLP/CV领域的计算最优缩放定律已得到充分研究，但天气预测具有独特的挑战性：自回归滚动预测会累积误差、输出包含多尺度多物理通道、全球池化测试指标可能与短期训练的逐通道行为不一致。需要专门针对天气预测的神经缩放分析框架。

Method: 将神经缩放分析从单步训练损失扩展到长时滚动预测和逐通道指标；量化三个方面：(1)各通道预测误差分布及随预测时长增长趋势；(2)全局池化时测试误差与滚动长度关系的幂律是否成立；(3)参数、数据、计算三种缩放轴下拟合如何随时长和通道变化。

Result: 发现强烈的跨通道和跨时长异质性：汇总缩放可能表现良好，但许多通道在后期预测时段会退化。

Conclusion: 对加权目标函数、时长感知课程学习、跨输出的资源分配具有重要启示；强调了天气预测中考虑通道和时长异质性的必要性。

Abstract: Compute-optimal scaling laws are relatively well studied for NLP and CV, where objectives are typically single-step and targets are comparatively homogeneous. Weather forecasting is harder to characterize in the same framework: autoregressive rollouts compound errors over long horizons, outputs couple many physical channels with disparate scales and predictability, and globally pooled test metrics can disagree sharply with per-channel, late-lead behavior implied by short-horizon training. We extend neural scaling analysis for autoregressive weather forecasting from single-step training loss to long rollouts and per-channel metrics. We quantify (1) how prediction error is distributed across channels and how its growth rate evolves with forecast horizon, (2) if power law scaling holds for test error, relative to rollout length when error is pooled globally, and (3) how that fit varies jointly with horizon and channel for parameter, data, and compute-based scaling axes. We find strong cross-channel and cross-horizon heterogeneity: pooled scaling can look favorable while many channels degrade at late leads. We discuss implications for weighted objectives, horizon-aware curricula, and resource allocation across outputs.

</details>


### [216] [Hierarchical SVG Tokenization: Learning Compact Visual Programs for Scalable Vector Graphics Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05072)
*Ximing Xing,Ziteng Xue,Zhenxi Li,Weicong Liang,Linqing Wang,Zhantao Yang,Tiankai Hang,Zijin Yin,Qinglin Lu,Chunyu Wang,Qian Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HiVG，一个专为SVG生成设计的分层词元化框架，通过原子词元和段词元重组SVG结构，配合HMN初始化策略和课程学习训练范式，有效解决了传统字节级词元化在矢量图形生成中的坐标碎片化、空间关系破坏和序列效率低下等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型在SVG生成任务中沿用了自然语言处理中的字节级词元化方案，该方案存在三大核心缺陷：数值坐标被离散符号切分导致空间关系丧失、词元冗余严重、坐标幻觉问题以及长序列生成效率低下。这些问题根源在于通用词元化方案未能反映矢量图形的几何结构特性。

Method: HiVG框架包含三个关键技术：(1) 原子词元(atomic tokens)：将原始SVG字符串分解为结构化单元；(2) 段词元(segment tokens)：将可执行命令-参数组压缩为几何约束单元，在保持句法有效性的同时提升序列效率；(3) 分层均值-噪声(HMN)初始化：为新词元嵌入注入数值排序信号和语义先验，缓解空间错配问题；(4) 课程学习训练范式：逐步增加程序复杂度，实现更稳定的SVG程序学习。

Result: 在文本到SVG和图像到SVG两项任务的实验中，HiVG相比传统词元化方案在生成保真度、空间一致性和序列效率三个维度均展现出显著提升。

Conclusion: 分层词元化策略结合HMN初始化和课程学习能够使模型更稳定地学习可执行SVG程序，有效弥合了通用NLP词元化与几何图形结构之间的鸿沟，为矢量图形生成提供了更适配的表示方案。

Abstract: Recent large language models have shifted SVG generation from differentiable rendering optimization to autoregressive program synthesis. However, existing approaches still rely on generic byte-level tokenization inherited from natural language processing, which poorly reflects the geometric structure of vector graphics. Numerical coordinates are fragmented into discrete symbols, destroying spatial relationships and introducing severe token redundancy, often leading to coordinate hallucination and inefficient long-sequence generation. To address these challenges, we propose HiVG, a hierarchical SVG tokenization framework tailored for autoregressive vector graphics generation. HiVG decomposes raw SVG strings into structured \textit{atomic tokens} and further compresses executable command--parameter groups into geometry-constrained \textit{segment tokens}, substantially improving sequence efficiency while preserving syntactic validity. To further mitigate spatial mismatch, we introduce a Hierarchical Mean--Noise (HMN) initialization strategy that injects numerical ordering signals and semantic priors into new token embeddings. Combined with a curriculum training paradigm that progressively increases program complexity, HiVG enables more stable learning of executable SVG programs. Extensive experiments on both text-to-SVG and image-to-SVG tasks demonstrate improved generation fidelity, spatial consistency, and sequence efficiency compared with conventional tokenization schemes.

</details>


### [217] [Vintix II: Decision Pre-Trained Transformer is a Scalable In-Context Reinforcement Learner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05112)
*Andrei Polubarov,Lyubaykin Nikita,Alexander Derevyagin,Artyom Grishin,Igor Saprygin,Aleksandr Serkov,Mark Averchenko,Daniil Tikhonov,Maksim Zhdanov,Alexander Nikulin,Ilya Zisman,Albina Klepach,Alexey Zemtsov,Vladislav Kurenkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将DPT扩展到多域环境，使用Flow Matching作为训练方法，获得在数百个任务上训练的智能体，在泛化到未见任务上取得明显提升，证明了ICRL作为训练通用智能体的可行替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的算法蒸馏(AD)方法在多域设置中泛化到未见任务的能力有限；DPT虽然展示了更强的上下文强化学习能力，但其在简化领域外的可扩展性尚未建立。

Method: 将DPT扩展到多样化的多域环境，采用Flow Matching作为训练方法（其特性是保持贝叶斯后验采样的可解释性），训练跨数百个不同任务的智能体。

Result: 在保持-out测试集上获得明显的泛化提升；在在线和离线推理中都展示了更强的性能。

Conclusion: ICRL是训练通用智能体的可行替代方案，可替代专家蒸馏方法。

Abstract: Recent progress in in-context reinforcement learning (ICRL) has demonstrated its potential for training generalist agents that can acquire new tasks directly at inference. Algorithm Distillation (AD) pioneered this paradigm and was subsequently scaled to multi-domain settings, although its ability to generalize to unseen tasks remained limited. The Decision Pre-Trained Transformer (DPT) was introduced as an alternative, showing stronger in-context reinforcement learning abilities in simplified domains, but its scalability had not been established. In this work, we extend DPT to diverse multi-domain environments, applying Flow Matching as a natural training choice that preserves its interpretation as Bayesian posterior sampling. As a result, we obtain an agent trained across hundreds of diverse tasks that achieves clear gains in generalization to the held-out test set. This agent improves upon prior AD scaling and demonstrates stronger performance in both online and offline inference, reinforcing ICRL as a viable alternative to expert distillation for training generalist agents.

</details>


### [218] [Reasoning Through Chess: How Reasoning Evolves from Data Through Fine-Tuning and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05134)
*Lucas Dionisopoulos,Nicklas Majamaki,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型在从监督微调（SFT）到强化学习（RL）过程中推理能力的变化，以国际象棋为任务场景。通过对比直接预测最佳走法与多步轨迹训练两种方法，发现直接预测最佳走法能带来更强的下游性能但产生不忠实推理，而多步轨迹训练则能在保持相当性能的同时产生忠实推理和更稳定的RL。此外，RL显著提升走法质量分布并降低幻觉率，早期SFT检查点的多项指标可预测RL后模型表现。最终7B参数模型超越了领先的开源推理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在推理类任务（如国际象棋）中表现不佳，如何通过训练让其学会有效推理尚不明确。研究旨在追踪模型推理能力从SFT到RL的演变过程，理解不同训练信号对推理忠实性和任务性能的影响。

Method: 使用国际象棋作为测试场景，构建理论驱动的数据集。对比两种训练范式：（1）直接预测最佳走法；（2）训练多步轨迹序列。通过分析RL过程中模型表现、推理质量、幻觉率等指标变化，评估不同训练方法的效果。

Result: 1. 直接预测最佳走法→有效RL+最强下游性能，但产生不忠实推理（推理与所选走法不一致）；2. 多步轨迹训练→性能相当但推理更忠实、RL更稳定；3. RL显著改善走法质量分布并降低幻觉率；4. SFT检查点指标（评估表现、幻觉率、推理质量）可预测RL后模型性能；5. 7B参数模型超越领先开源推理模型。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了训练信号选择对推理忠实性的关键影响：虽然直接优化最终决策能带来更好性能，但可能导致模型使用虚假推理路径。多步轨迹训练在性能与推理可解释性间取得更好平衡。RL不仅提升决策质量，还附带减少幻觉的积极效应。早期训练指标对最终模型表现具有预测价值。

Abstract: How can you get a language model to reason in a task it natively struggles with? We study how reasoning evolves in a language model -- from supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to reinforcement learning (RL) -- by analyzing how a set of theoretically-inspired datasets impacts language model performance in chess. We find that fine-tuning a model to directly predict the best move leads to effective RL and the strongest downstream performance -- however, the RL step elicits unfaithful reasoning (reasoning inconsistent with the chosen move). Alternatively, training on multi-move trajectories yields comparable downstream performance with faithful reasoning and more stable RL.
